## Task 10: 配准求解器（网格投票 + 精化 + 链式累积）

**Files:**
- Create: `src/register/solver.py`
- Test: `tests/register/test_solver.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `src.register.transform.*`、`src.detect.segmentation.SourceTable`
- Produces:
  - `PairSolution` dataclass：`frame_from: int`、`frame_to: int`、`matrix: np.ndarray`（把 `frame_to` 坐标映回 `frame_from` 坐标）、`n_inliers: int`、`rms_px: float`、`rotation_deg: float`、`shift_px: tuple[float, float]`
  - `RegistrationError(RuntimeError)`
  - `vote_correspondences(src_xy, dst_xy, *, center, rot_range_deg, rot_step_deg, shift_max_px, vote_bin_px, match_radius_px) -> tuple[np.ndarray, np.ndarray, int]`
  - `solve_pair(table_from, table_to, *, min_inliers=30, **vote_kw) -> PairSolution`
  - `RegistrationResult` dataclass：`reference: int`、`frames: list[int]`、`matrices: dict[int, np.ndarray]`、`pairs: list[PairSolution]`；方法 `to_sky(frame, xy) -> np.ndarray`、`to_detector(frame, xy_sky) -> np.ndarray`、`report() -> dict`
  - `register_sequence(detections, frames, *, reference=None, config=None) -> RegistrationResult`

- [ ] **Step 1: 写失败测试**

`tests/register/test_solver.py`：

```python
from __future__ import annotations

import numpy as np
import pytest

from src.detect.segmentation import SourceTable
from src.register.solver import (
    RegistrationError,
    register_sequence,
    solve_pair,
    vote_correspondences,
)
from src.register.transform import apply_transform, decompose, similarity_matrix

CENTER = (2047.5, 2047.5)
VOTE_KW = dict(
    center=CENTER,
    rot_range_deg=1.5,
    rot_step_deg=0.05,
    shift_max_px=400.0,
    vote_bin_px=4.0,
    match_radius_px=5.0,
)


def as_table(xy, frame=0):
    xy = np.asarray(xy, dtype=np.float64)
    n = len(xy)
    return SourceTable(
        frame=frame,
        x=xy[:, 0].copy(),
        y=xy[:, 1].copy(),
        flux=np.full(n, 1000.0),
        peak=np.full(n, 200.0),
        elongation=np.ones(n),
        npix=np.full(n, 9, dtype=np.int64),
    )


def synth_pair(n=90, rot=0.1, dx=-126.0, dy=5.0, seed=0, drop=0.0, noise=0.0):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    src = rng.uniform(200.0, 3900.0, size=(n, 2))
    M = similarity_matrix(rotation_deg=rot, tx=dx, ty=dy, center=CENTER)
    dst = apply_transform(M, src)
    if noise:
        dst = dst + rng.normal(0.0, noise, size=dst.shape)
    if drop:
        keep = rng.random(n) > drop
        dst = dst[keep]
    return src, dst, M


def test_vote_finds_correspondences():
    src, dst, _ = synth_pair()
    a, b, n = vote_correspondences(src, dst, **VOTE_KW)
    assert n >= 80
    assert a.shape == b.shape == (n, 2)


def test_vote_survives_partial_overlap_and_noise():
    src, dst, _ = synth_pair(n=120, drop=0.35, noise=0.4, seed=3)
    _, _, n = vote_correspondences(src, dst, **VOTE_KW)
    assert n >= 50


def test_vote_returns_zero_on_unrelated_fields():
    rng = np.random.default_rng(8)
    src = rng.uniform(0.0, 4096.0, size=(60, 2))
    dst = rng.uniform(0.0, 4096.0, size=(60, 2))
    _, _, n = vote_correspondences(src, dst, **VOTE_KW)
    assert n < 20


def test_solve_pair_recovers_transform():
    src, dst, M = synth_pair(rot=0.12, dx=-125.7, dy=4.2, seed=5)
    sol = solve_pair(as_table(src, 0), as_table(dst, 1), min_inliers=30, **VOTE_KW)
    assert sol.frame_from == 0 and sol.frame_to == 1
    assert sol.n_inliers >= 70
    assert sol.rms_px < 0.2
    # matrix 把 frame_to 坐标映回 frame_from 坐标，故为 M 的逆
    back = apply_transform(sol.matrix, dst)
    d = decompose(sol.matrix)
    assert d["rotation_deg"] == pytest.approx(-0.12, abs=0.02)
    assert np.abs(back - apply_transform(np.linalg.inv(M), dst)).max() < 0.5


def test_solve_pair_raises_when_inliers_insufficient():
    rng = np.random.default_rng(12)
    src = rng.uniform(0.0, 4096.0, size=(40, 2))
    dst = rng.uniform(0.0, 4096.0, size=(40, 2))
    with pytest.raises(RegistrationError):
        solve_pair(as_table(src, 0), as_table(dst, 1), min_inliers=30, **VOTE_KW)


def test_register_sequence_chains_transforms():
    """构造 10 帧、每帧移动 126 px 的合成序列，累计位移必须线性累加。"""
    rng = np.random.default_rng(21)
    base = rng.uniform(-2000.0, 6000.0, size=(400, 2))
    frames = list(range(10))
    detections = {}
    for i in frames:
        M = similarity_matrix(rotation_deg=-0.1 * i, tx=126.0 * i, ty=-4.0 * i, center=CENTER)
        xy = apply_transform(M, base)
        inside = (xy[:, 0] > 0) & (xy[:, 0] < 4096) & (xy[:, 1] > 0) & (xy[:, 1] < 4096)
        detections[i] = as_table(xy[inside], frame=i)

    result = register_sequence(detections, frames, reference=0)
    assert result.reference == 0
    assert np.allclose(result.matrices[0], np.eye(3))
    # frame i 的点映回参考系应与 frame 0 的对应点重合
    for i in [3, 6, 9]:
        M = similarity_matrix(rotation_deg=-0.1 * i, tx=126.0 * i, ty=-4.0 * i, center=CENTER)
        xy = apply_transform(M, base[:20])
        sky = result.to_sky(i, xy)
        assert np.abs(sky - base[:20]).max() < 1.0


def test_to_detector_is_inverse_of_to_sky():
    rng = np.random.default_rng(22)
    base = rng.uniform(0.0, 4096.0, size=(300, 2))
    frames = list(range(5))
    detections = {
        i: as_table(
            apply_transform(
                similarity_matrix(tx=100.0 * i, center=CENTER), base
            ),
            frame=i,
        )
        for i in frames
    }
    result = register_sequence(detections, frames, reference=0)
    xy = np.array([[1000.0, 1000.0], [2500.0, 3000.0]])
    assert np.allclose(result.to_detector(3, result.to_sky(3, xy)), xy, atol=1e-6)


def test_report_contains_inlier_and_rms_summary():
    rng = np.random.default_rng(23)
    base = rng.uniform(0.0, 4096.0, size=(300, 2))
    frames = list(range(4))
    detections = {
        i: as_table(apply_transform(similarity_matrix(tx=80.0 * i, center=CENTER), base), i)
        for i in frames
    }
    rep = register_sequence(detections, frames, reference=0).report()
    assert rep["n_pairs"] == 3
    assert rep["inliers_min"] > 0
    assert rep["rms_max_px"] < 0.5
    assert "cumulative" in rep


def test_dataset_b_registration_matches_measured_baseline(dataset_b_dir):
    """复现 spec 基线：内点 74-93，累计 f70->f16 尺度 0.99822 / 旋转 -3.325 度。"""
    from src.calib.segment import tracking_segment
    from src.dataio.fits_loader import FrameSequence
    from src.detect.segmentation import detect_sequence
    from src.calib.hotpixel import build_from_sequence

    seq = FrameSequence.from_directory(dataset_b_dir)
    seg = tracking_segment(seq.headers)
    frames = list(range(seg.start, seg.end + 1))
    hpm = build_from_sequence(seq, frames[:30])
    dets = detect_sequence(seq, frames, n_sigma=4.0, npixels=5, hot_clusters=hpm.clusters)

    result = register_sequence(dets, frames, reference=seg.start)
    rep = result.report()
    assert rep["inliers_min"] >= 50
    assert rep["rms_max_px"] < 1.5
    cum = decompose(result.matrices[seg.end])
    assert cum["scale"] == pytest.approx(0.99822, abs=0.004)
    assert cum["rotation_deg"] == pytest.approx(-3.325, abs=0.3)
    assert cum["tx"] == pytest.approx(6495.0, rel=0.05)
```

- [ ] **Step 2: 运行测试，确认失败**

```bash
bash scripts/dr.sh pytest tests/register/test_solver.py -v
```

- [ ] **Step 3: 实现**

`src/register/solver.py`：

```python
"""星场配准：旋转扫描 + 平移投票求初值，再用相似变换最小二乘精化。

为什么用投票而不是特征匹配或相位相关：
  - 相位相关会锁死在热像素上（它们跨帧完全不动），给出恒为 (0, 0) 的解；
  - RANSAC 仿射在只有几十颗星、位移达上百像素时病态；
  - 网格投票对部分重叠与外点天然鲁棒：只要有一批点共享同一位移，直方图
    就会出现清晰峰值。低仰角下相邻帧场移 126 px、重叠仅约 97%，正需要这个特性。

链式累积：M[i+1] = M[i] @ solve_pair(i, i+1).matrix，逐对求解再串起来，
避免直接匹配相距很远的两帧（累计场移 6495 px 时几乎没有共同星）。
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import logging
from dataclasses import dataclass, field

import numpy as np
from scipy.spatial import cKDTree

from src.detect.segmentation import SourceTable
from src.register.transform import (
    DEFAULT_CENTER,
    apply_transform,
    decompose,
    fit_similarity,
    invert,
    rms,
    similarity_matrix,
)

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


class RegistrationError(RuntimeError):
    """配准失败——内点不足或残差过大。不允许通过放宽阈值掩盖。"""


@dataclass
class PairSolution:
    frame_from: int
    frame_to: int
    matrix: np.ndarray
    n_inliers: int
    rms_px: float
    rotation_deg: float
    shift_px: tuple[float, float]


def vote_correspondences(
    src_xy: np.ndarray,
    dst_xy: np.ndarray,
    *,
    center: tuple[float, float] = DEFAULT_CENTER,
    rot_range_deg: float = 1.5,
    rot_step_deg: float = 0.05,
    shift_max_px: float = 400.0,
    vote_bin_px: float = 4.0,
    match_radius_px: float = 5.0,
) -> tuple[np.ndarray, np.ndarray, int]:
    """返回 (src 侧匹配点, dst 侧匹配点, 匹配数)。"""
    src_xy = np.asarray(src_xy, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1, 2)
    dst_xy = np.asarray(dst_xy, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1, 2)
    if len(src_xy) < 3 or len(dst_xy) < 3:
        return np.zeros((0, 2)), np.zeros((0, 2)), 0

    nbins = max(8, int(round(2.0 * shift_max_px / vote_bin_px)))
    rng = [[-shift_max_px, shift_max_px], [-shift_max_px, shift_max_px]]
    best = (0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0)  # votes, dx, dy, rot

    for rot in np.arange(-rot_range_deg, rot_range_deg + 1e-9, rot_step_deg):
        rotated = apply_transform(similarity_matrix(rotation_deg=rot, center=center), src_xy)
        dx = (dst_xy[None, :, 0] - rotated[:, None, 0]).ravel()
        dy = (dst_xy[None, :, 1] - rotated[:, None, 1]).ravel()
        keep = (np.abs(dx) < shift_max_px) & (np.abs(dy) < shift_max_px)
        if keep.sum() < 10:
            continue
        hist, xedges, yedges = np.histogram2d(dx[keep], dy[keep], bins=nbins, range=rng)
        k = np.unravel_index(int(hist.argmax()), hist.shape)
        votes = float(hist[k])
        if votes > best[0]:
            best = (
                votes,
                0.5 * (xedges[k[0]] + xedges[k[0] + 1]),
                0.5 * (yedges[k[1]] + yedges[k[1] + 1]),
                float(rot),
            )

    votes, dx, dy, rot = best
    if votes <= 0:
        return np.zeros((0, 2)), np.zeros((0, 2)), 0

    guess = similarity_matrix(rotation_deg=rot, tx=dx, ty=dy, center=center)
    projected = apply_transform(guess, src_xy)
    dist, idx = cKDTree(dst_xy).query(projected)
    good = dist < match_radius_px
    return src_xy[good], dst_xy[idx[good]], int(good.sum())


def solve_pair(
    table_from: SourceTable,
    table_to: SourceTable,
    *,
    min_inliers: int = 30,
    **vote_kw,
) -> PairSolution:
    """求把 table_to 的坐标映回 table_from 坐标系的相似变换。"""
    a, b, n = vote_correspondences(table_from.xy, table_to.xy, **vote_kw)
    if n < min_inliers:
        raise RegistrationError(
            f"帧 {table_from.frame}->{table_to.frame} 匹配点仅 {n}，低于下限 {min_inliers}；"
            "请检查该帧是否处于摆扫段或探测阈值是否过高"
        )
    matrix = fit_similarity(b, a)
    d = decompose(matrix)
    return PairSolution(
        frame_from=table_from.frame,
        frame_to=table_to.frame,
        matrix=matrix,
        n_inliers=n,
        rms_px=rms(matrix, b, a),
        rotation_deg=d["rotation_deg"],
        shift_px=(d["tx"], d["ty"]),
    )


@dataclass
class RegistrationResult:
    reference: int
    frames: list[int]
    matrices: dict[int, np.ndarray]
    pairs: list[PairSolution] = field(default_factory=list)

    def to_sky(self, frame: int, xy: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
        """探测器坐标 -> 参考帧（天球）坐标。"""
        return apply_transform(self.matrices[frame], xy)

    def to_detector(self, frame: int, xy_sky: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
        return apply_transform(invert(self.matrices[frame]), xy_sky)

    def report(self) -> dict:
        inliers = [p.n_inliers for p in self.pairs]
        errors = [p.rms_px for p in self.pairs]
        cumulative = decompose(self.matrices[self.frames[-1]])
        return {
            "reference_frame": self.reference,
            "n_frames": len(self.frames),
            "n_pairs": len(self.pairs),
            "inliers_min": int(min(inliers)) if inliers else 0,
            "inliers_max": int(max(inliers)) if inliers else 0,
            "inliers_median": float(np.median(inliers)) if inliers else float("nan"),
            "rms_max_px": float(max(errors)) if errors else float("nan"),
            "rms_median_px": float(np.median(errors)) if errors else float("nan"),
            "cumulative": cumulative,
            "pairs": [
                {
                    "from": p.frame_from,
                    "to": p.frame_to,
                    "n_inliers": p.n_inliers,
                    "rms_px": p.rms_px,
                    "rotation_deg": p.rotation_deg,
                    "shift_px": list(p.shift_px),
                }
                for p in self.pairs
            ],
        }


def register_sequence(
    detections: dict[int, SourceTable],
    frames,
    *,
    reference: int | None = None,
    config: dict | None = None,
) -> RegistrationResult:
    """逐对配准并链式累积到参考帧。frames 必须按时间升序且连续。"""
    frames = list(frames)
    if len(frames) < 2:
        raise ValueError("配准至少需要 2 帧")
    ref = frames[0] if reference is None else reference
    if ref != frames[0]:
        raise ValueError("当前实现要求参考帧为 frames 的首帧")

    conf = dict(config or {})
    min_inliers = conf.pop("min_inliers", 30)
    vote_kw = {
        "center": tuple(conf.get("center", DEFAULT_CENTER)),
        "rot_range_deg": conf.get("rot_range_deg", 1.5),
        "rot_step_deg": conf.get("rot_step_deg", 0.05),
        "shift_max_px": conf.get("shift_max_px", 400.0),
        "vote_bin_px": conf.get("vote_bin_px", 4.0),
        "match_radius_px": conf.get("match_radius_px", 5.0),
    }

    matrices = {ref: np.eye(3, dtype=np.float64)}
    pairs: list[PairSolution] = []
    for cur, nxt in zip(frames, frames[1:]):
        sol = solve_pair(
            detections[cur], detections[nxt], min_inliers=min_inliers, **vote_kw
        )
        pairs.append(sol)
        matrices[nxt] = matrices[cur] @ sol.matrix
        logger.debug(
            "配准 f%02d->f%02d 内点 %d rms %.3f px", cur, nxt, sol.n_inliers, sol.rms_px
        )

    return RegistrationResult(reference=ref, frames=frames, matrices=matrices, pairs=pairs)
```

## Task 11: 双坐标系目标判决

这是 35 分星图分析题的核心，也是最需要防质疑的一环。

**Files:**
- Create: `src/target/dual_frame.py`
- Test: `tests/target/test_dual_frame.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `src.detect.segmentation.SourceTable`、`src.register.solver.RegistrationResult`
- Produces:
  - `Track` dataclass：`frames: list[int]`、`xy_det: np.ndarray`（`(N, 2)`）、`xy_sky: np.ndarray`、`flux: np.ndarray`、`elongation: np.ndarray`；属性 `length`、`start`、`end`、`v_det_px`（探测器系逐帧位移中位数）、`v_sky_px`、`drift_det_px`（首尾距离）、`pixel_locked`（首尾各坐标变化均小于 `lock_ratio * drift_sky` 时为 `True`）
  - `build_tracks(detections, frames, registration, *, radius=8.0, min_frames=10) -> list[Track]`
  - `Verdict` dataclass：`track: Track`、`is_target: bool`、`v_det_px: float`、`v_sky_px: float`、`contrast: float`、`pixel_locked: bool`、`reasons: list[str]`
  - `classify(track, *, v_det_max=8.0, v_sky_min=30.0, lock_ratio=0.05) -> Verdict`
  - `find_targets(detections, frames, registration, *, config=None) -> list[Verdict]`：只返回 `is_target` 为真者，按轨迹长度降序

- [ ] **Step 1: 写失败测试**

`tests/target/test_dual_frame.py`（同时建空的 `tests/target/__init__.py`）：

```python
from __future__ import annotations

import numpy as np
import pytest

from src.detect.segmentation import SourceTable
from src.register.solver import RegistrationResult, register_sequence
from src.register.transform import apply_transform, similarity_matrix
from src.target.dual_frame import build_tracks, classify, find_targets

CENTER = (2047.5, 2047.5)


def as_table(xy, frame, flux=None):
    xy = np.asarray(xy, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1, 2)
    n = len(xy)
    return SourceTable(
        frame=frame,
        x=xy[:, 0].copy(),
        y=xy[:, 1].copy(),
        flux=np.full(n, 1000.0) if flux is None else np.asarray(flux, dtype=np.float64),
        peak=np.full(n, 300.0),
        elongation=np.full(n, 1.2),
        npix=np.full(n, 9, dtype=np.int64),
    )


@pytest.fixture()
def synthetic_scene():
    """30 帧：星场每帧移动 126 px，1 个探测器系近静止的目标，3 个热像素残点。

    目标在探测器系每帧漂移 1.8 px（与数据集 B 实测一致），恒星在探测器系
    每帧漂移 126 px。热像素残点在探测器系严格不动。
    """
    rng = np.random.default_rng(31)
    frames = list(range(30))
    base_stars = rng.uniform(-1500.0, 5500.0, size=(500, 2))
    locked = np.array([[244.0, 3174.0], [312.0, 3206.0], [327.0, 3210.0]])
    detections = {}
    for i in frames:
        M = similarity_matrix(rotation_deg=-0.1 * i, tx=126.0 * i, ty=-4.0 * i, center=CENTER)
        stars = apply_transform(M, base_stars)
        inside = (
            (stars[:, 0] > 5) & (stars[:, 0] < 4090) & (stars[:, 1] > 5) & (stars[:, 1] < 4090)
        )
        target = np.array([[2271.6 + 1.81 * i, 1958.8 + 0.33 * i]])
        xy = np.vstack([stars[inside], target, locked])
        flux = np.concatenate(
            [np.full(inside.sum(), 1500.0), [264.0 + 38.0 * i], np.full(3, 900.0)]
        )
        detections[i] = as_table(xy, i, flux)
    registration = register_sequence(detections, frames, reference=0)
    return frames, detections, registration


def test_build_tracks_finds_stationary_track(synthetic_scene):
    frames, detections, reg = synthetic_scene
    tracks = build_tracks(detections, frames, reg, radius=8.0, min_frames=10)
    long_tracks = [t for t in tracks if t.length >= 25]
    assert len(long_tracks) >= 4  # 1 目标 + 3 热像素残点


def test_target_track_has_expected_kinematics(synthetic_scene):
    frames, detections, reg = synthetic_scene
    tracks = build_tracks(detections, frames, reg, radius=8.0, min_frames=10)
    target = max(
        (t for t in tracks if not t.pixel_locked), key=lambda t: t.length, default=None
    )
    assert target is not None
    assert target.length >= 25
    assert target.v_det_px == pytest.approx(1.84, abs=0.3)
    assert target.v_sky_px == pytest.approx(126.0, rel=0.1)


def test_classify_accepts_target(synthetic_scene):
    frames, detections, reg = synthetic_scene
    tracks = build_tracks(detections, frames, reg, radius=8.0, min_frames=10)
    target = max((t for t in tracks if not t.pixel_locked), key=lambda t: t.length)
    verdict = classify(target)
    assert verdict.is_target
    assert verdict.contrast > 20.0
    assert not verdict.pixel_locked


def test_classify_rejects_pixel_locked_source(synthetic_scene):
    frames, detections, reg = synthetic_scene
    tracks = build_tracks(detections, frames, reg, radius=8.0, min_frames=10)
    locked = [t for t in tracks if t.pixel_locked]
    assert locked, "合成场景应产生像素锁定轨迹"
    for t in locked:
        v = classify(t)
        assert not v.is_target
        assert "像素锁定" in " ".join(v.reasons)


def test_classify_rejects_star(synthetic_scene):
    """恒星在探测器系高速移动，v_det 远超阈值。"""
    frames, detections, reg = synthetic_scene
    fake_star = build_tracks(
        {i: as_table([[100.0 + 126.0 * i, 100.0]], i) for i in frames[:15]},
        frames[:15],
        reg,
        radius=200.0,
        min_frames=10,
    )
    assert fake_star
    v = classify(fake_star[0])
    assert not v.is_target


def test_find_targets_returns_exactly_one(synthetic_scene):
    frames, detections, reg = synthetic_scene
    verdicts = find_targets(detections, frames, reg)
    assert len(verdicts) == 1
    assert verdicts[0].is_target


def test_short_tracks_are_discarded(synthetic_scene):
    frames, detections, reg = synthetic_scene
    tracks = build_tracks(detections, frames, reg, radius=8.0, min_frames=25)
    assert all(t.length >= 25 for t in tracks)


def test_seeding_from_every_frame_matters():
    """只从首帧播种会漏掉中途进场的目标——这是本项目踩过的坑。"""
    frames = list(range(20))
    rng = np.random.default_rng(41)
    base = rng.uniform(-1000.0, 5000.0, size=(400, 2))
    detections = {}
    for i in frames:
        M = similarity_matrix(tx=126.0 * i, center=CENTER)
        stars = apply_transform(M, base)
        inside = (stars[:, 0] > 5) & (stars[:, 0] < 4090)
        xy = stars[inside]
        if i >= 8:  # 目标第 8 帧才出现
            xy = np.vstack([xy, [[2000.0 + 1.8 * (i - 8), 1500.0]]])
        detections[i] = as_table(xy, i)
    reg = register_sequence(detections, frames, reference=0)
    verdicts = find_targets(detections, frames, reg, config={"min_track_frames": 10})
    assert len(verdicts) == 1
    assert verdicts[0].track.start == 8


def test_dataset_b_recovers_the_known_target(dataset_b_dir):
    """端到端复现 spec 基线：f17-f70 共 54 帧，探测器系漂移 95.8 px。"""
    from src.calib.hotpixel import build_from_sequence
    from src.calib.segment import tracking_segment
    from src.dataio.fits_loader import FrameSequence
    from src.detect.segmentation import detect_sequence

    seq = FrameSequence.from_directory(dataset_b_dir)
    seg = tracking_segment(seq.headers)
    frames = list(range(seg.start, seg.end + 1))
    hpm = build_from_sequence(seq, frames[:30])
    dets = detect_sequence(seq, frames, n_sigma=4.0, hot_clusters=hpm.clusters)
    reg = register_sequence(dets, frames, reference=seg.start)

    verdicts = find_targets(dets, frames, reg)
    assert len(verdicts) == 1
    trk = verdicts[0].track
    assert trk.length >= 50
    assert trk.start <= 18 and trk.end >= 69
    assert trk.xy_det[0] == pytest.approx([2271.6, 1958.8], abs=3.0)
    assert trk.xy_det[-1] == pytest.approx([2365.8, 1976.3], abs=3.0)
    assert trk.drift_det_px == pytest.approx(95.8, rel=0.1)
    assert verdicts[0].contrast > 20.0
```

- [ ] **Step 3: 实现**

`src/target/dual_frame.py`：

```python
"""双坐标系交叉确认。

判决要求三条独立证据同时成立：
  1. 探测器系近静止（v_det < v_det_max）——机架在跟踪它；
  2. 天球系高速运动（v_sky > v_sky_min）——它相对恒星背景在动；
  3. 非像素锁定——坐标确实在变，不是热像素/坏列那种整数不动的伪迹。

第 3 条是关键的防质疑设计。前两条对热像素同样成立（热像素在探测器系完全
不动，在天球系随配准反推出 126 px/帧的"运动"），若缺这一条，坏点会被当成
最高置信度的空间目标。

播种策略：从每一帧的每一个源都尝试起一条轨迹。只从首帧播种会漏掉中途进场
的目标——实测中目标在 f17 才出现，从 f16 播种加 6-12 px 半径搜索得到 0 个候选。
"""
from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass, field

import numpy as np
from scipy.spatial import cKDTree

from src.detect.segmentation import SourceTable
from src.register.solver import RegistrationResult


@dataclass
class Track:
    frames: list[int]
    xy_det: np.ndarray
    xy_sky: np.ndarray
    flux: np.ndarray
    elongation: np.ndarray
    lock_ratio: float = 0.05

    @property
    def length(self) -> int:
        return len(self.frames)

    @property
    def start(self) -> int:
        return self.frames[0]

    @property
    def end(self) -> int:
        return self.frames[-1]

    @property
    def drift_det_px(self) -> float:
        return float(np.hypot(*(self.xy_det[-1] - self.xy_det[0])))

    @property
    def drift_sky_px(self) -> float:
        return float(np.hypot(*(self.xy_sky[-1] - self.xy_sky[0])))

    @property
    def v_det_px(self) -> float:
        return self._step_median(self.xy_det)

    @property
    def v_sky_px(self) -> float:
        return self._step_median(self.xy_sky)

    @property
    def pixel_locked(self) -> bool:
        """坐标在探测器系几乎完全不变——热像素、坏列的特征。"""
        span = np.abs(self.xy_det.max(axis=0) - self.xy_det.min(axis=0))
        scale = max(self.drift_sky_px, 1.0)
        return bool(np.all(span < self.lock_ratio * scale))

    def _step_median(self, xy: np.ndarray) -> float:
        if len(xy) < 2:
            return 0.0
        d = np.diff(xy, axis=0)
        return float(np.median(np.hypot(d[:, 0], d[:, 1])))


@dataclass
class Verdict:
    track: Track
    is_target: bool
    v_det_px: float
    v_sky_px: float
    contrast: float
    pixel_locked: bool
    reasons: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)

    def to_dict(self) -> dict:
        return {
            "is_target": self.is_target,
            "frames": [self.track.start, self.track.end],
            "n_frames": self.track.length,
            "v_det_px_per_frame": self.v_det_px,
            "v_sky_px_per_frame": self.v_sky_px,
            "stationarity_contrast": self.contrast,
            "pixel_locked": self.pixel_locked,
            "drift_det_px": self.track.drift_det_px,
            "xy_start": self.track.xy_det[0].tolist(),
            "xy_end": self.track.xy_det[-1].tolist(),
            "reasons": list(self.reasons),
        }


def build_tracks(
    detections: dict[int, SourceTable],
    frames,
    registration: RegistrationResult,
    *,
    radius: float = 8.0,
    min_frames: int = 10,
    lock_ratio: float = 0.05,
) -> list[Track]:
    """在探测器坐标系中做最近邻链接，从每一帧的每一个源播种。"""
    frames = list(frames)
    trees = {i: cKDTree(detections[i].xy) for i in frames if len(detections[i])}
    claimed: set[tuple[int, int]] = set()
    tracks: list[Track] = []

    for si, seed_frame in enumerate(frames):
        table = detections[seed_frame]
        for k in range(len(table)):
            if (seed_frame, k) in claimed:
                continue
            path = [(seed_frame, k)]
            cx, cy = float(table.x[k]), float(table.y[k])
            for nxt in frames[si + 1 :]:
                tree = trees.get(nxt)
                if tree is None:
                    break
                dist, idx = tree.query([cx, cy])
                if dist >= radius:
                    break
                idx = int(idx)
                path.append((nxt, idx))
                cx = float(detections[nxt].x[idx])
                cy = float(detections[nxt].y[idx])
            if len(path) < min_frames:
                continue
            claimed.update(path)
            idxs = [i for i, _ in path]
            xy_det = np.array(
                [[detections[f].x[j], detections[f].y[j]] for f, j in path], dtype=np.float64
            )
            xy_sky = np.array(
                [registration.to_sky(f, xy_det[n : n + 1])[0] for n, (f, _) in enumerate(path)],
                dtype=np.float64,
            )
            tracks.append(
                Track(
                    frames=idxs,
                    xy_det=xy_det,
                    xy_sky=xy_sky,
                    flux=np.array([detections[f].flux[j] for f, j in path], dtype=np.float64),
                    elongation=np.array(
                        [detections[f].elongation[j] for f, j in path], dtype=np.float64
                    ),
                    lock_ratio=lock_ratio,
                )
            )

    tracks.sort(key=lambda t: -t.length)
    return tracks


def classify(
    track: Track,
    *,
    v_det_max: float = 8.0,
    v_sky_min: float = 30.0,
    lock_ratio: float = 0.05,
) -> Verdict:
    track.lock_ratio = lock_ratio
    v_det = track.v_det_px
    v_sky = track.v_sky_px
    locked = track.pixel_locked
    contrast = v_sky / v_det if v_det > 0 else float("inf")

    reasons: list[str] = []
    if v_det > v_det_max:
        reasons.append(f"探测器系速度 {v_det:.2f} px/帧 超过上限 {v_det_max}，判为恒星")
    if v_sky < v_sky_min:
        reasons.append(f"天球系速度 {v_sky:.2f} px/帧 低于下限 {v_sky_min}，未见相对运动")
    if locked:
        reasons.append("像素锁定：坐标跨帧几乎不变，判为传感器缺陷而非空间目标")

    ok = not reasons
    if ok:
        reasons.append(
            f"双系确认通过：探测器系 {v_det:.2f} px/帧、天球系 {v_sky:.2f} px/帧，"
            f"静止性对比度 {contrast:.1f}x，坐标连续变化"
        )
    return Verdict(
        track=track,
        is_target=ok,
        v_det_px=v_det,
        v_sky_px=v_sky,
        contrast=contrast,
        pixel_locked=locked,
        reasons=reasons,
    )


def find_targets(
    detections: dict[int, SourceTable],
    frames,
    registration: RegistrationResult,
    *,
    config: dict | None = None,
) -> list[Verdict]:
    conf = dict(config or {})
    tracks = build_tracks(
        detections,
        frames,
        registration,
        radius=conf.get("track_radius_px", 8.0),
        min_frames=conf.get("min_track_frames", 10),
        lock_ratio=conf.get("lock_ratio", 0.05),
    )
    verdicts = [
        classify(
            t,
            v_det_max=conf.get("v_det_max_px", 8.0),
            v_sky_min=conf.get("v_sky_min_px", 30.0),
            lock_ratio=conf.get("lock_ratio", 0.05),
        )
        for t in tracks
    ]
    hits = [v for v in verdicts if v.is_target]
    hits.sort(key=lambda v: -v.track.length)
    return hits
```

## Task 12: `.DAT` 真值读取与比对

**Files:**
- Create: `src/validate/truth.py`
- Test: `tests/validate/test_truth.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `src.dataio.fits_loader.FrameSequence`、`src.target.dual_frame.Track`
- Produces:
  - `TruthTable` dataclass：`path: Path`、`time: astropy.time.Time`（长度 N）、`ra_deg`、`dec_deg`、`mag`（`float64` 数组，`mag` 中缺失值为 `nan`）；`__len__()`
  - `load_truth(path) -> TruthTable`
  - `match_frames(truth, sequence, frames, *, max_dt_s=0.5) -> tuple[np.ndarray, np.ndarray]`：返回 `(帧号数组, 真值行号数组)`
  - `TruthReport` dataclass + `to_dict()`：字段 `dataset_id`、`n_matched`、`dt_max_s`、`plate_scale_arcsec_px`、`plate_scale_std`、`angular_step_arcsec`、`pixel_step_px`、`mag_correlation`、`zero_point`、`zero_point_std`、`available: bool`、`note: str`
  - `compare(track, truth, sequence, registration, *, max_dt_s=0.5) -> TruthReport`
  - `write_report(report, output_dir) -> Path`：写 `<output_dir>/truth_report.json`；无真值时写入 `available=false` 与说明，不产生空文件

- [ ] **Step 3: 实现**

`src/validate/truth.py`：

```python
"""`.DAT` 真值读取与定量比对。

严格约束：本模块只被验证与报告环节导入。detect / register / target 三个包
不得引用它——否则整条判决链就变成"用真值算出真值"，这在答辩中是致命的
原理性质疑。该约束由 tests/test_architecture.py 的导入图检查守卫。

.DAT 格式（见数据源 `说明文件.txt`）：
    年 月 日 时 分 秒 赤经 赤纬 星等
时间系统 UTC，坐标系统 J2000。星等字段为 000 表示缺测。
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
from dataclasses import dataclass
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
from astropy.time import Time

N_COLUMNS = 9


@dataclass
class TruthTable:
    path: Path
    time: Time
    ra_deg: np.ndarray
    dec_deg: np.ndarray
    mag: np.ndarray

    def __len__(self) -> int:
        return int(self.ra_deg.size)


def load_truth(path: str | Path) -> TruthTable:
    path = Path(path)
    raw = np.loadtxt(path, ndmin=2)
    if raw.shape[1] != N_COLUMNS:
        raise ValueError(
            f"{path.name} 应有 {N_COLUMNS} 列（年 月 日 时 分 秒 赤经 赤纬 星等），"
            f"实际 {raw.shape[1]} 列"
        )

    stamps = [
        "%04d-%02d-%02dT%02d:%02d:%09.6f" % (r[0], r[1], r[2], r[3], r[4], r[5]) for r in raw
    ]
    mag = raw[:, 8].astype(np.float64)
    mag[mag <= 0.0] = np.nan  # 000 表示缺测

    return TruthTable(
        path=path,
        time=Time(stamps, scale="utc"),
        ra_deg=raw[:, 6].astype(np.float64),
        dec_deg=raw[:, 7].astype(np.float64),
        mag=mag,
    )


def match_frames(truth: TruthTable, sequence, frames, *, max_dt_s: float = 0.5):
    """按时间戳把真值行匹配到帧号。返回 (帧号数组, 真值行号数组)。"""
    frames = np.asarray(list(frames), dtype=int)
    frame_unix = sequence.times().unix[frames]
    truth_unix = truth.time.unix

    matched_frames: list[int] = []
    matched_rows: list[int] = []
    for row, t in enumerate(truth_unix):
        k = int(np.argmin(np.abs(frame_unix - t)))
        if abs(frame_unix[k] - t) <= max_dt_s:
            matched_frames.append(int(frames[k]))
            matched_rows.append(row)
    return np.array(matched_frames, dtype=int), np.array(matched_rows, dtype=int)


@dataclass
class TruthReport:
    dataset_id: str
    available: bool
    n_matched: int = 0
    dt_max_s: float | None = None
    plate_scale_arcsec_px: float | None = None
    plate_scale_std: float | None = None
    angular_step_arcsec: float | None = None
    pixel_step_px: float | None = None
    mag_correlation: float | None = None
    zero_point: float | None = None
    zero_point_std: float | None = None
    note: str = ""

    def to_dict(self) -> dict:
        return {
            "dataset_id": self.dataset_id,
            "available": self.available,
            "n_matched": self.n_matched,
            "dt_max_s": self.dt_max_s,
            "plate_scale_arcsec_px": self.plate_scale_arcsec_px,
            "plate_scale_std": self.plate_scale_std,
            "angular_step_arcsec": self.angular_step_arcsec,
            "pixel_step_px": self.pixel_step_px,
            "mag_correlation": self.mag_correlation,
            "zero_point": self.zero_point,
            "zero_point_std": self.zero_point_std,
            "note": self.note,
        }


def compare(track, truth: TruthTable, sequence, registration, *, max_dt_s: float = 0.5) -> TruthReport:
    """把目标轨迹与真值定量比对，导出比例尺、星等相关性与零点。"""
    frames, rows = match_frames(truth, sequence, track.frames, max_dt_s=max_dt_s)
    if len(frames) < 3:
        return TruthReport(
            dataset_id=sequence.dataset_id,
            available=False,
            note=f"真值与轨迹时间匹配仅 {len(frames)} 点，不足以定量比对",
        )

    order = {f: i for i, f in enumerate(track.frames)}
    sel = np.array([order[f] for f in frames], dtype=int)
    xy_sky = track.xy_sky[sel]
    flux = track.flux[sel]

    ra = truth.ra_deg[rows]
    dec = truth.dec_deg[rows]
    dra = np.diff(ra) * np.cos(np.deg2rad(dec[:-1]))
    ddec = np.diff(dec)
    angular = np.hypot(dra, ddec) * 3600.0            # ″/步
    pixel = np.hypot(*np.diff(xy_sky, axis=0).T)       # px/步
    good = pixel > 1e-6
    scale = angular[good] / pixel[good]

    with np.errstate(divide="ignore", invalid="ignore"):
        m_inst = -2.5 * np.log10(np.where(flux > 0, flux, np.nan))
    ok = np.isfinite(m_inst) & np.isfinite(truth.mag[rows])
    if ok.sum() >= 3:
        corr = float(np.corrcoef(truth.mag[rows][ok], m_inst[ok])[0, 1])
        diff = truth.mag[rows][ok] - m_inst[ok]
        zp, zp_std = float(diff.mean()), float(diff.std())
    else:
        corr = zp = zp_std = None

    dt = np.abs(sequence.times().unix[frames] - truth.time.unix[rows])
    return TruthReport(
        dataset_id=sequence.dataset_id,
        available=True,
        n_matched=int(len(frames)),
        dt_max_s=float(dt.max()),
        plate_scale_arcsec_px=float(np.mean(scale)),
        plate_scale_std=float(np.std(scale)),
        angular_step_arcsec=float(np.mean(angular)),
        pixel_step_px=float(np.mean(pixel)),
        mag_correlation=corr,
        zero_point=zp,
        zero_point_std=zp_std,
        note="真值仅用于验证与定标，未参与目标判决",
    )


def write_report(report: TruthReport, output_dir: str | Path) -> Path:
    output_dir = Path(output_dir)
    output_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    path = output_dir / "truth_report.json"
    path.write_text(
        json.dumps(report.to_dict(), indent=2, ensure_ascii=False), encoding="utf-8"
    )
    return path
```

## Task 13: 真值隔离的架构守卫测试

单独成一个任务，因为它守护的是一条方法论红线，评审时会被直接问到。

**Files:**
- Create: `tests/test_architecture.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: 全部 `src/` 模块（静态分析，不执行）
- Produces: 无生产代码

- [ ] **Step 1: 写测试（本任务的测试即交付物，应当立即通过）**

`tests/test_architecture.py`：

```python
"""架构约束的静态守卫。

真值隔离：.DAT 只用于验证与定标。若判决链任何一环 import 了 src.validate.truth，
"识别结果"就成了真值的函数，答辩中一问即倒。用 AST 静态检查，不依赖运行路径。
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import ast
from pathlib import Path

import pytest

SRC = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "src"

PIPELINE_PACKAGES = ["detect", "register", "target", "calib", "stack"]
FORBIDDEN_PREFIXES = ("src.validate.truth", "validate.truth")


def imported_modules(path: Path) -> set[str]:
    tree = ast.parse(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"), filename=str(path))
    names: set[str] = set()
    for node in ast.walk(tree):
        if isinstance(node, ast.Import):
            names.update(alias.name for alias in node.names)
        elif isinstance(node, ast.ImportFrom) and node.module and node.level == 0:
            names.add(node.module)
            names.update(f"{node.module}.{a.name}" for a in node.names)
    return names


def pipeline_files() -> list[Path]:
    files: list[Path] = []
    for pkg in PIPELINE_PACKAGES:
        files.extend(sorted((SRC / pkg).glob("*.py")))
    assert files, "未找到流水线模块，检查 PIPELINE_PACKAGES 与目录结构是否同步"
    return files


@pytest.mark.parametrize("path", pipeline_files(), ids=lambda p: f"{p.parent.name}/{p.name}")
def test_pipeline_never_imports_truth(path: Path):
    hits = {m for m in imported_modules(path) if m.startswith(FORBIDDEN_PREFIXES)}
    assert not hits, (
        f"{path.relative_to(SRC.parent)} 引用了真值模块 {sorted(hits)}；"
        "真值只允许在 validate/ 与报告环节使用"
    )


def test_truth_module_exists_where_expected():
    assert (SRC / "validate" / "truth.py").is_file()


def test_no_src_io_package():
    """io 与标准库同名，必须叫 dataio。"""
    assert not (SRC / "io").exists()
    assert (SRC / "dataio").is_dir()


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "path",
    sorted(SRC.rglob("*.py")),
    ids=lambda p: str(p.relative_to(SRC)),
)
def test_no_hardcoded_dataset_paths(path: Path):
    """数据集路径不得写死在源码里，否则换数据即失效。"""
    text = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    for bad in ["data/images/60385", "data/images/60394", "D:\\projects"]:
        assert bad not in text, f"{path.name} 中出现硬编码路径 {bad!r}"


def test_pipeline_packages_all_present():
    for pkg in PIPELINE_PACKAGES + ["dataio", "astrometry", "analysis", "validate", "viz"]:
        assert (SRC / pkg / "__init__.py").is_file(), f"缺少包 src/{pkg}"
```

## Task 14: 可复现性验证器（星点计数的科学依据）

**Files:**
- Create: `src/validate/repeatability.py`
- Test: `tests/validate/test_repeatability.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `src.detect.segmentation.detect_sources_in_frame`、`src.register.solver.RegistrationResult`、`src.calib.background.model_background`
- Produces:
  - `RepeatabilityPoint` dataclass：`n_sigma`、`n_detected_mean`、`n_reproducible`、`reproducibility`（0–1）
  - `RepeatabilityCurve` dataclass：`points: list[RepeatabilityPoint]`、`frames: list[int]`、`match_radius_px`；方法 `to_dict()`、`recommended(min_reproducibility=0.85) -> RepeatabilityPoint`
  - `cross_frame_repeatability(sky_positions, *, match_radius=3.0, min_fraction=0.6) -> tuple[int, float]`：返回 `(可复现源数, 复现率)`
  - `scan_thresholds(sequence, frames, registration, *, sigmas, npixels, hot_clusters, match_radius_px, min_fraction=0.6) -> RepeatabilityCurve`

- [ ] **Step 1: 写失败测试**

`tests/validate/test_repeatability.py`：

```python
from __future__ import annotations

import numpy as np
import pytest

from src.validate.repeatability import (
    RepeatabilityCurve,
    RepeatabilityPoint,
    cross_frame_repeatability,
    scan_thresholds,
)


def test_all_real_sources_are_reproducible():
    base = np.array([[100.0, 100.0], [500.0, 700.0], [2000.0, 3000.0]])
    rng = np.random.default_rng(1)
    frames = [base + rng.normal(0.0, 0.3, size=base.shape) for _ in range(6)]
    n, frac = cross_frame_repeatability(frames, match_radius=3.0)
    assert n == 3
    assert frac == pytest.approx(1.0, abs=0.01)


def test_pure_noise_is_not_reproducible():
    rng = np.random.default_rng(2)
    frames = [rng.uniform(0.0, 4096.0, size=(200, 2)) for _ in range(6)]
    n, frac = cross_frame_repeatability(frames, match_radius=3.0)
    assert frac < 0.1
    assert n < 20


def test_mixed_population_recovers_real_fraction():
    """30 颗真源 + 每帧 170 个随机假源，复现率应接近 30/200 = 0.15。"""
    rng = np.random.default_rng(3)
    real = rng.uniform(0.0, 4096.0, size=(30, 2))
    frames = [
        np.vstack([real + rng.normal(0.0, 0.3, size=real.shape),
                   rng.uniform(0.0, 4096.0, size=(170, 2))])
        for _ in range(6)
    ]
    n, frac = cross_frame_repeatability(frames, match_radius=3.0)
    assert n == pytest.approx(30, abs=4)
    assert frac == pytest.approx(0.15, abs=0.04)


def test_min_fraction_controls_strictness():
    rng = np.random.default_rng(4)
    real = rng.uniform(0.0, 4096.0, size=(20, 2))
    frames = []
    for k in range(6):
        pts = real.copy()
        if k >= 3:
            pts = pts[:10]  # 后半段只有一半源出现
        frames.append(pts + rng.normal(0.0, 0.2, size=pts.shape))
    loose, _ = cross_frame_repeatability(frames, match_radius=3.0, min_fraction=0.4)
    strict, _ = cross_frame_repeatability(frames, match_radius=3.0, min_fraction=0.9)
    assert loose > strict


def test_empty_input_raises():
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        cross_frame_repeatability([])


def test_curve_recommended_picks_lowest_sigma_above_threshold():
    curve = RepeatabilityCurve(
        points=[
            RepeatabilityPoint(2.0, 2284.0, 200, 0.088),
            RepeatabilityPoint(3.0, 169.0, 130, 0.769),
            RepeatabilityPoint(4.0, 116.0, 103, 0.888),
            RepeatabilityPoint(5.0, 95.0, 86, 0.905),
        ],
        frames=[16, 17, 18],
        match_radius_px=3.0,
    )
    pick = curve.recommended(min_reproducibility=0.85)
    assert pick.n_sigma == 4.0


def test_curve_recommended_raises_when_none_qualify():
    curve = RepeatabilityCurve(
        points=[RepeatabilityPoint(3.0, 500.0, 20, 0.04)], frames=[1, 2], match_radius_px=3.0
    )
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        curve.recommended(min_reproducibility=0.85)


def test_curve_to_dict_is_json_ready():
    curve = RepeatabilityCurve(
        points=[RepeatabilityPoint(5.0, 95.0, 86, 0.905)], frames=[16, 17], match_radius_px=3.0
    )
    d = curve.to_dict()
    import json

    json.loads(json.dumps(d))
    assert d["points"][0]["n_sigma"] == 5.0


def test_dataset_b_threshold_scan_shows_noise_cliff(dataset_b_dir):
    """2σ 处探测数暴涨但复现率崩塌；5σ 复现率应达 0.85 以上。"""
    from src.calib.hotpixel import build_from_sequence
    from src.calib.segment import tracking_segment
    from src.dataio.fits_loader import FrameSequence
    from src.detect.segmentation import detect_sequence
    from src.register.solver import register_sequence

    seq = FrameSequence.from_directory(dataset_b_dir)
    seg = tracking_segment(seq.headers)
    frames = list(range(seg.start, seg.start + 6))
    hpm = build_from_sequence(seq, frames)
    dets = detect_sequence(seq, frames, n_sigma=4.0, hot_clusters=hpm.clusters)
    reg = register_sequence(dets, frames, reference=frames[0])

    curve = scan_thresholds(
        seq, frames, reg, sigmas=[2.0, 3.0, 5.0], npixels=5, hot_clusters=hpm.clusters
    )
    by_sigma = {p.n_sigma: p for p in curve.points}
    assert by_sigma[2.0].n_detected_mean > 5 * by_sigma[5.0].n_detected_mean
    assert by_sigma[2.0].reproducibility < 0.3
    assert by_sigma[5.0].reproducibility > 0.85
    assert by_sigma[5.0].reproducibility > by_sigma[2.0].reproducibility
```

- [ ] **Step 3: 实现**

`src/validate/repeatability.py`：

```python
"""跨帧可复现性——区分真源与噪声的核心手段，也是星点计数唯一可辩护的依据。

原理：真实恒星在配准后的天球坐标系中位置固定，会在多帧的同一位置重复出现；
噪声峰值位置随机，几乎不可能在多帧同一位置复现。因此"能在 >= min_fraction
的帧中被重复探测到的源数"才是可报的星数，而不是单帧的原始探测数。

这条判据直接回应本项目历史上 263822 颗星、单帧 9021 个源的问题：那些数字
在 2σ 附近，复现率不足 0.1，绝大部分是噪声。
"""
from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass, field

import numpy as np
from scipy.spatial import cKDTree

from src.calib.background import model_background
from src.detect.segmentation import detect_sources_in_frame


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class RepeatabilityPoint:
    n_sigma: float
    n_detected_mean: float
    n_reproducible: int
    reproducibility: float


@dataclass
class RepeatabilityCurve:
    points: list[RepeatabilityPoint] = field(default_factory=list)
    frames: list[int] = field(default_factory=list)
    match_radius_px: float = 3.0

    def to_dict(self) -> dict:
        return {
            "frames": list(self.frames),
            "match_radius_px": self.match_radius_px,
            "points": [
                {
                    "n_sigma": p.n_sigma,
                    "n_detected_mean": p.n_detected_mean,
                    "n_reproducible": p.n_reproducible,
                    "reproducibility": p.reproducibility,
                }
                for p in self.points
            ],
        }

    def recommended(self, min_reproducibility: float = 0.85) -> RepeatabilityPoint:
        """取满足复现率下限的最低阈值——在可信的前提下尽量多探测。"""
        ok = [p for p in self.points if p.reproducibility >= min_reproducibility]
        if not ok:
            raise ValueError(
                f"没有阈值达到复现率 {min_reproducibility}；最高为 "
                f"{max((p.reproducibility for p in self.points), default=float('nan')):.3f}"
            )
        return min(ok, key=lambda p: p.n_sigma)


def cross_frame_repeatability(
    sky_positions,
    *,
    match_radius: float = 3.0,
    min_fraction: float = 0.6,
) -> tuple[int, float]:
    """统计在天球坐标系中跨帧重复出现的源数与复现率。

    以第一帧为参考清单，逐帧统计命中；返回 (命中帧数比例 >= min_fraction 的源数,
    该源数 / 各帧平均探测数)。
    """
    frames = [np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1, 2) for p in sky_positions]
    if len(frames) < 2:
        raise ValueError("可复现性统计至少需要 2 帧")

    reference = frames[0]
    if len(reference) == 0:
        return 0, 0.0

    hits = np.ones(len(reference), dtype=int)
    for other in frames[1:]:
        if len(other) == 0:
            continue
        dist, _ = cKDTree(other).query(reference)
        hits += (dist < match_radius).astype(int)

    n_reproducible = int(np.count_nonzero(hits >= max(2, int(np.ceil(min_fraction * len(frames))))))
    mean_detected = float(np.mean([len(f) for f in frames]))
    fraction = n_reproducible / mean_detected if mean_detected > 0 else 0.0
    return n_reproducible, float(fraction)


def scan_thresholds(
    sequence,
    frames,
    registration,
    *,
    sigmas,
    npixels: int = 5,
    hot_clusters=None,
    match_radius_px: float = 3.0,
    min_fraction: float = 0.6,
    background_kwargs: dict | None = None,
) -> RepeatabilityCurve:
    """对一串阈值分别做"探测数"与"可复现数"双曲线。"""
    frames = list(frames)
    background_kwargs = dict(background_kwargs or {})

    models = {}
    images = {}
    for idx in frames:
        images[idx] = sequence.image(idx)
        models[idx] = model_background(images[idx], **background_kwargs)

    points: list[RepeatabilityPoint] = []
    for sigma in sigmas:
        sky_lists = []
        counts = []
        for idx in frames:
            table = detect_sources_in_frame(
                images[idx],
                models[idx],
                n_sigma=float(sigma),
                npixels=npixels,
                hot_clusters=hot_clusters,
                frame=idx,
            )
            counts.append(len(table))
            sky_lists.append(registration.to_sky(idx, table.xy))
        n_rep, frac = cross_frame_repeatability(
            sky_lists, match_radius=match_radius_px, min_fraction=min_fraction
        )
        points.append(
            RepeatabilityPoint(
                n_sigma=float(sigma),
                n_detected_mean=float(np.mean(counts)),
                n_reproducible=n_rep,
                reproducibility=frac,
            )
        )

    points.sort(key=lambda p: p.n_sigma)
    return RepeatabilityCurve(
        points=points, frames=frames, match_radius_px=match_radius_px
    )
```

## Task 15: 滑动窗口叠加与覆盖图

累积视场移动 6495 px > 4096 px 图幅宽，全序列叠加在大部分像素上只有 1 帧覆盖，
增益虚假。必须用滑动窗口，并且把覆盖数明确画出来。

**Files:**
- Create: `src/stack/coadd.py`
- Test: `tests/stack/test_coadd.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `src.register.solver.RegistrationResult`、`src.calib.background.BackgroundModel`
- Produces:
  - `StackResult` dataclass：`image: np.ndarray`、`coverage: np.ndarray`（`int32`，每像素参与帧数）、`frames: list[int]`、`reference: int`、`usable_mask` 属性（`coverage >= min_coverage`）、`gain_mag` 属性（`2.5 * log10(sqrt(median_coverage))`）、`stats() -> dict`
  - `warp_to_reference(image, matrix, *, order=1) -> tuple[np.ndarray, np.ndarray]`：返回 `(重采样图, 有效掩膜)`
  - `coadd_window(sequence, registration, frames, *, min_coverage=4, order=1, subtract_background=True) -> StackResult`
  - `sliding_coadd(sequence, registration, frames, *, window=12, step=6, **kw) -> list[StackResult]`

- [ ] **Step 1: 写失败测试**

`tests/stack/test_coadd.py`（同时建空的 `tests/stack/__init__.py`）：

```python
from __future__ import annotations

import numpy as np
import pytest

from src.register.solver import RegistrationResult
from src.register.transform import similarity_matrix
from src.stack.coadd import StackResult, coadd_window, sliding_coadd, warp_to_reference

CENTER = (2047.5, 2047.5)


class FakeSequence:
    """小尺寸假序列，避免测试里反复读 4096x4096。"""

    def __init__(self, images):
        self._images = images
        self.dataset_id = "fake"

    def __len__(self):
        return len(self._images)

    def image(self, idx):
        return self._images[idx].astype(np.float64)


@pytest.fixture()
def shifted_sequence():
    """8 帧，每帧整体右移 20 px；参考帧 0。"""
    base = np.zeros((256, 256), dtype=np.float64)
    base[128, 60] = 100.0
    base[70, 90] = 100.0
    images = []
    matrices = {}
    for i in range(8):
        img = np.zeros_like(base)
        img[128, 60 + 20 * i] = 100.0
        img[70, 90 + 20 * i] = 100.0
        images.append(img)
        matrices[i] = similarity_matrix(tx=20.0 * i, center=(127.5, 127.5))
    reg = RegistrationResult(reference=0, frames=list(range(8)), matrices=matrices, pairs=[])
    return FakeSequence(images), reg


def test_warp_returns_image_and_mask():
    img = np.ones((64, 64), dtype=np.float64)
    M = similarity_matrix(tx=10.0, center=(31.5, 31.5))
    out, mask = warp_to_reference(img, M)
    assert out.shape == img.shape
    assert mask.dtype == bool
    assert mask.sum() < mask.size          # 平移后一部分像素落到界外
    assert mask[:, 20:].all()


def test_warp_aligns_a_point_source():
    img = np.zeros((64, 64), dtype=np.float64)
    img[32, 40] = 50.0
    M = similarity_matrix(tx=10.0, center=(31.5, 31.5))
    out, _ = warp_to_reference(img, M)
    y, x = np.unravel_index(np.argmax(out), out.shape)
    assert (y, x) == (32, 30)


def test_coadd_accumulates_signal(shifted_sequence):
    seq, reg = shifted_sequence
    res = coadd_window(seq, reg, list(range(8)), min_coverage=4, subtract_background=False)
    assert isinstance(res, StackResult)
    assert res.image.shape == (256, 256)
    assert res.coverage.max() == 8
    assert res.image[128, 60] == pytest.approx(100.0, rel=0.05)


def test_coverage_map_records_edge_falloff(shifted_sequence):
    seq, reg = shifted_sequence
    res = coadd_window(seq, reg, list(range(8)), min_coverage=4, subtract_background=False)
    assert res.coverage[:, -5:].max() < 8      # 右边缘帧数不足
    assert res.usable_mask.sum() < res.coverage.size
    assert res.usable_mask.sum() > 0.5 * res.coverage.size


def test_gain_is_reported_not_assumed(shifted_sequence):
    """增益必须由中位覆盖数算出，不能按窗口长度声称。"""
    seq, reg = shifted_sequence
    res = coadd_window(seq, reg, list(range(8)), min_coverage=4, subtract_background=False)
    expected = 2.5 * np.log10(np.sqrt(float(np.median(res.coverage[res.usable_mask]))))
    assert res.gain_mag == pytest.approx(expected, abs=1e-6)
    assert res.gain_mag < 2.5 * np.log10(np.sqrt(8.0)) + 1e-9


def test_noise_drops_with_coverage():
    rng = np.random.default_rng(7)
    images = [rng.normal(0.0, 10.0, size=(128, 128)) for _ in range(9)]
    matrices = {i: np.eye(3) for i in range(9)}
    reg = RegistrationResult(reference=0, frames=list(range(9)), matrices=matrices, pairs=[])
    res = coadd_window(FakeSequence(images), reg, list(range(9)), subtract_background=False)
    single = float(np.std(images[0]))
    stacked = float(np.std(res.image))
    assert stacked == pytest.approx(single / 3.0, rel=0.25)


def test_sliding_coadd_produces_overlapping_windows(shifted_sequence):
    seq, reg = shifted_sequence
    results = sliding_coadd(
        seq, reg, list(range(8)), window=4, step=2, subtract_background=False
    )
    assert [r.frames[0] for r in results] == [0, 2, 4]
    assert all(len(r.frames) == 4 for r in results)


def test_sliding_coadd_rejects_window_larger_than_sequence(shifted_sequence):
    seq, reg = shifted_sequence
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        sliding_coadd(seq, reg, list(range(3)), window=12, step=6)


def test_stats_is_json_ready(shifted_sequence):
    import json

    seq, reg = shifted_sequence
    res = coadd_window(seq, reg, list(range(8)), subtract_background=False)
    json.loads(json.dumps(res.stats()))
    assert res.stats()["n_frames"] == 8


def test_dataset_b_full_sequence_stacking_is_invalid(dataset_b_dir):
    """记录反例：全序列叠加时可用像素占比极低，这是不用全叠加的量化理由。"""
    from src.calib.hotpixel import build_from_sequence
    from src.calib.segment import tracking_segment
    from src.dataio.fits_loader import FrameSequence
    from src.detect.segmentation import detect_sequence
    from src.register.solver import register_sequence

    seq = FrameSequence.from_directory(dataset_b_dir)
    seg = tracking_segment(seq.headers)
    frames = list(range(seg.start, seg.end + 1))
    hpm = build_from_sequence(seq, frames[:30])
    dets = detect_sequence(seq, frames, n_sigma=4.0, hot_clusters=hpm.clusters)
    reg = register_sequence(dets, frames, reference=seg.start)

    full = coadd_window(seq, reg, frames, min_coverage=len(frames) // 2)
    assert full.usable_mask.mean() < 0.35

    windows = sliding_coadd(seq, reg, frames, window=12, step=6, min_coverage=4)
    assert len(windows) >= 8
    assert np.mean([w.usable_mask.mean() for w in windows]) > 0.6
```

- [ ] **Step 2: 运行测试，确认失败**

```bash
bash scripts/dr.sh pytest tests/stack/test_coadd.py -v
```

预期：`No module named 'src.stack.coadd'`。

- [ ] **Step 3: 实现**

`src/stack/coadd.py`：

```python
"""滑动窗口叠加与覆盖图。

为什么不做全序列叠加：数据集 B 跟踪段 55 帧，累积视场平移 +6495 px，超过
4096 px 图幅宽。全序列对齐后大多数像素只被 1 帧覆盖，"叠加 55 帧提升
sqrt(55) ~ 2.2 mag"的说法在这些像素上完全不成立。

改为窗口 W=12、步长 6 的滑动叠加，并同时输出 coverage 图。增益按实际中位
覆盖数计算：gain = 2.5 * log10(sqrt(median_coverage))，coverage < min_coverage
的像素标记为不可用，不参与任何统计与图示。
"""
from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass, field

import numpy as np
from scipy.ndimage import affine_transform

from src.calib.background import model_background


@dataclass
class StackResult:
    image: np.ndarray
    coverage: np.ndarray
    frames: list[int] = field(default_factory=list)
    reference: int = 0
    min_coverage: int = 4

    @property
    def usable_mask(self) -> np.ndarray:
        return self.coverage >= self.min_coverage

    @property
    def median_coverage(self) -> float:
        mask = self.usable_mask
        if not mask.any():
            return 0.0
        return float(np.median(self.coverage[mask]))

    @property
    def gain_mag(self) -> float:
        cov = self.median_coverage
        if cov <= 1.0:
            return 0.0
        return float(2.5 * np.log10(np.sqrt(cov)))

    def stats(self) -> dict:
        mask = self.usable_mask
        return {
            "n_frames": len(self.frames),
            "frame_range": [int(self.frames[0]), int(self.frames[-1])] if self.frames else [],
            "reference": int(self.reference),
            "min_coverage": int(self.min_coverage),
            "coverage_max": int(self.coverage.max()),
            "coverage_median_usable": self.median_coverage,
            "usable_fraction": float(mask.mean()),
            "gain_mag": self.gain_mag,
            "rms_usable": float(np.std(self.image[mask])) if mask.any() else None,
        }


def warp_to_reference(image: np.ndarray, matrix: np.ndarray, *, order: int = 1):
    """把 image 重采样到参考帧网格。

    matrix 是 3x3 齐次矩阵，把该帧的 (x, y) 映射到参考帧的 (x, y)。
    affine_transform 需要的是反向映射（输出坐标 -> 输入坐标），且坐标顺序是
    (row, col) = (y, x)，所以要取逆并交换轴。
    """
    inv = np.linalg.inv(np.asarray(matrix, dtype=np.float64))
    # (x, y) 顺序的 2x2 + 平移 -> (y, x) 顺序
    lin = np.array([[inv[1, 1], inv[1, 0]], [inv[0, 1], inv[0, 0]]], dtype=np.float64)
    off = np.array([inv[1, 2], inv[0, 2]], dtype=np.float64)

    out = affine_transform(
        image, lin, offset=off, order=order, mode="constant", cval=np.nan, prefilter=False
    )
    valid = np.isfinite(out)
    out = np.where(valid, out, 0.0)
    return out, valid


def coadd_window(
    sequence,
    registration,
    frames,
    *,
    min_coverage: int = 4,
    order: int = 1,
    subtract_background: bool = True,
    background_kwargs: dict | None = None,
) -> StackResult:
    frames = list(frames)
    if not frames:
        raise ValueError("叠加窗口不能为空")

    background_kwargs = dict(background_kwargs or {})
    total = None
    coverage = None
    for idx in frames:
        img = sequence.image(idx)
        if subtract_background:
            img = model_background(img, **background_kwargs).subtract(img)
        warped, valid = warp_to_reference(img, registration.matrices[idx], order=order)
        if total is None:
            total = np.zeros_like(warped, dtype=np.float64)
            coverage = np.zeros(warped.shape, dtype=np.int32)
        total += np.where(valid, warped, 0.0)
        coverage += valid.astype(np.int32)

    with np.errstate(invalid="ignore", divide="ignore"):
        mean = np.where(coverage > 0, total / np.maximum(coverage, 1), 0.0)

    return StackResult(
        image=mean,
        coverage=coverage,
        frames=frames,
        reference=registration.reference,
        min_coverage=min_coverage,
    )


def sliding_coadd(
    sequence,
    registration,
    frames,
    *,
    window: int = 12,
    step: int = 6,
    **kwargs,
) -> list[StackResult]:
    frames = list(frames)
    if window > len(frames):
        raise ValueError(f"窗口 {window} 大于可用帧数 {len(frames)}")
    if step < 1:
        raise ValueError("步长必须 >= 1")

    results: list[StackResult] = []
    for start in range(0, len(frames) - window + 1, step):
        results.append(coadd_window(sequence, registration, frames[start : start + window], **kwargs))
    return results
```

## Task 16: 像素系↔赤道系定向标定

spec §11 遗留的 P1 项：`.DAT` 运动方向 128.08°，天球系像素路径角 −5.66°。差异
来自 y 轴翻转与视场旋转（一个常数旋转+反射）。本任务把它解出来并验证。

**Files:**
- Create: `src/astrometry/orientation.py`
- Test: `tests/astrometry/test_orientation.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `src.validate.truth.TruthTable`（仅在标定入口，属验证/定标环节，不违反隔离约束）
- Produces:
  - `Orientation` frozen dataclass：`scale_arcsec_px`、`rotation_deg`、`flip: bool`、`rms_arcsec`、`n_points`；方法 `matrix() -> np.ndarray`（2×2，px→(Δα·cosδ, Δδ) 角秒）、`pixel_to_offset(dxy) -> np.ndarray`、`offset_to_pixel(dradec) -> np.ndarray`、`to_dict()`
  - `fit_orientation(xy_sky, ra_deg, dec_deg) -> Orientation`：最小二乘求解含反射的 2 参数（scale, rotation）× 2 种手性，取残差小者
  - `pixel_path_angle(xy) -> float`、`sky_path_angle(ra_deg, dec_deg) -> float`：分别返回像素路径与赤道路径的方向角（度，0–360）
  - `OrientationUnavailable` 异常：无真值时抛出，由调用方降级

- [ ] **Step 1: 写失败测试**

`tests/astrometry/test_orientation.py`（同时建空的 `tests/astrometry/__init__.py`）：

```python
from __future__ import annotations

import numpy as np
import pytest

from src.astrometry.orientation import (
    Orientation,
    OrientationUnavailable,
    fit_orientation,
    pixel_path_angle,
    sky_path_angle,
)


def synth(scale, rot_deg, flip, n=40, ra0=209.0, dec0=12.0):
    """按给定定向生成一段像素路径与对应赤道坐标。"""
    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    xy = np.column_stack([2000.0 + 120.0 * n * t, 1900.0 - 12.0 * n * t])
    d = xy - xy[0]
    c, s = np.cos(np.deg2rad(rot_deg)), np.sin(np.deg2rad(rot_deg))
    R = np.array([[c, -s], [s, c]]) @ (np.array([[1.0, 0.0], [0.0, -1.0]]) if flip else np.eye(2))
    off = (scale * (R @ d.T)).T / 3600.0
    dec = dec0 + off[:, 1]
    ra = ra0 + off[:, 0] / np.cos(np.deg2rad(dec0))
    return xy, ra, dec


def test_recovers_scale_and_rotation_without_flip():
    xy, ra, dec = synth(6.179, 34.0, False)
    o = fit_orientation(xy, ra, dec)
    assert o.scale_arcsec_px == pytest.approx(6.179, rel=1e-3)
    assert o.rotation_deg == pytest.approx(34.0, abs=0.5)
    assert o.flip is False
    assert o.rms_arcsec < 0.5


def test_recovers_flip():
    xy, ra, dec = synth(6.179, -20.0, True)
    o = fit_orientation(xy, ra, dec)
    assert o.flip is True
    assert o.scale_arcsec_px == pytest.approx(6.179, rel=1e-3)
    assert o.rms_arcsec < 0.5


def test_matrix_round_trips():
    xy, ra, dec = synth(6.179, 34.0, True)
    o = fit_orientation(xy, ra, dec)
    d = xy[5:] - xy[0]
    back = o.offset_to_pixel(o.pixel_to_offset(d))
    assert back == pytest.approx(d, abs=1e-6)


def test_rejects_too_few_points():
    with pytest.raises(OrientationUnavailable):
        fit_orientation(np.zeros((2, 2)), np.zeros(2), np.zeros(2))


def test_rejects_degenerate_path():
    """所有点重合无法定出方向。"""
    xy = np.tile([100.0, 100.0], (10, 1))
    with pytest.raises(OrientationUnavailable):
        fit_orientation(xy, np.full(10, 209.0), np.full(10, 12.0))


def test_path_angles_are_in_degrees():
    xy = np.array([[0.0, 0.0], [10.0, 0.0]])
    assert pixel_path_angle(xy) == pytest.approx(0.0, abs=1e-6)
    xy = np.array([[0.0, 0.0], [0.0, 10.0]])
    assert pixel_path_angle(xy) == pytest.approx(90.0, abs=1e-6)
    ra = np.array([209.0, 209.0])
    dec = np.array([12.0, 13.0])
    assert sky_path_angle(ra, dec) == pytest.approx(90.0, abs=1e-6)


def test_to_dict_is_json_ready():
    import json

    xy, ra, dec = synth(6.179, 10.0, False)
    json.loads(json.dumps(fit_orientation(xy, ra, dec).to_dict()))


def test_dataset_b_orientation_explains_the_angle_discrepancy(dataset_b_dir):
    """标定后残差应远小于比例尺本身，且解释 128.08 vs -5.66 的方向差。"""
    from src.calib.hotpixel import build_from_sequence
    from src.calib.segment import tracking_segment
    from src.dataio.fits_loader import FrameSequence
    from src.detect.segmentation import detect_sequence
    from src.register.solver import register_sequence
    from src.target.dual_frame import find_targets
    from src.validate.truth import load_truth, match_frames

    seq = FrameSequence.from_directory(dataset_b_dir)
    seg = tracking_segment(seq.headers)
    frames = list(range(seg.start, seg.end + 1))
    hpm = build_from_sequence(seq, frames[:30])
    dets = detect_sequence(seq, frames, n_sigma=4.0, hot_clusters=hpm.clusters)
    reg = register_sequence(dets, frames, reference=seg.start)
    track = find_targets(dets, frames, reg)[0].track

    truth = load_truth(seq.truth_path)
    mf, rows = match_frames(truth, seq, track.frames)
    order = {f: i for i, f in enumerate(track.frames)}
    xy = track.xy_sky[[order[f] for f in mf]]

    o = fit_orientation(xy, truth.ra_deg[rows], truth.dec_deg[rows])
    assert o.scale_arcsec_px == pytest.approx(6.179, abs=0.1)
    assert o.rms_arcsec < 15.0        # 远小于单帧角步长 776.9''
    assert isinstance(o.flip, bool)

    ang_px = pixel_path_angle(xy)
    ang_sky = sky_path_angle(truth.ra_deg[rows], truth.dec_deg[rows])
    assert abs(((ang_px - ang_sky + 180.0) % 360.0) - 180.0) > 60.0  # 未标定时确有大差异
```

- [ ] **Step 3: 实现**

`src/astrometry/orientation.py`：

```python
"""像素系与赤道系之间的定向标定。

观测事实：目标在天球坐标系中的像素路径方向角为 -5.66 度，而 .DAT 真值给出的
赤道运动方向为 128.08 度。二者相差约 134 度，来源是探测器 y 轴相对赤纬方向
翻转，叠加一个常数视场旋转角。这不是误差，而是一个待定的刚体变换。

本模块把它解成 (scale, rotation, flip) 三参数：
    [dalpha*cos(delta), ddelta] = scale * R(rotation) * F(flip) * [dx, dy]
其中 F = diag(1, -1) 当 flip 为真。因为反射不能由旋转连续到达，两种手性各解
一次线性最小二乘，取残差小者。

严格来说这是局部切平面近似，在视场 4096 px * 6.18''/px ~ 7 角分范围内，
球面曲率影响远低于像素级，可忽略。
"""
from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass

import numpy as np

MIN_POINTS = 3


class OrientationUnavailable(RuntimeError):
    """点数不足或路径退化，无法定出定向。"""


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Orientation:
    scale_arcsec_px: float
    rotation_deg: float
    flip: bool
    rms_arcsec: float
    n_points: int

    def matrix(self) -> np.ndarray:
        c, s = np.cos(np.deg2rad(self.rotation_deg)), np.sin(np.deg2rad(self.rotation_deg))
        R = np.array([[c, -s], [s, c]], dtype=np.float64)
        F = np.array([[1.0, 0.0], [0.0, -1.0]]) if self.flip else np.eye(2)
        return self.scale_arcsec_px * (R @ F)

    def pixel_to_offset(self, dxy) -> np.ndarray:
        dxy = np.asarray(dxy, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1, 2)
        return (self.matrix() @ dxy.T).T

    def offset_to_pixel(self, dradec) -> np.ndarray:
        dradec = np.asarray(dradec, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1, 2)
        return (np.linalg.inv(self.matrix()) @ dradec.T).T

    def to_dict(self) -> dict:
        return {
            "scale_arcsec_px": self.scale_arcsec_px,
            "rotation_deg": self.rotation_deg,
            "flip": bool(self.flip),
            "rms_arcsec": self.rms_arcsec,
            "n_points": int(self.n_points),
        }


def _solve(dxy: np.ndarray, target: np.ndarray, flip: bool):
    """解 target = A @ (F dxy)，A 为 scale*R（2 自由度）。"""
    F = np.array([[1.0, 0.0], [0.0, -1.0]]) if flip else np.eye(2)
    p = (F @ dxy.T).T
    # [tx] = [[px, -py]] [a]   其中 a = s cos t, b = s sin t
    # [ty]   [[py,  px]] [b]
    A = np.zeros((2 * len(p), 2), dtype=np.float64)
    A[0::2, 0] = p[:, 0]
    A[0::2, 1] = -p[:, 1]
    A[1::2, 0] = p[:, 1]
    A[1::2, 1] = p[:, 0]
    b = target.reshape(-1)
    sol, *_ = np.linalg.lstsq(A, b, rcond=None)
    a, bb = float(sol[0]), float(sol[1])
    scale = float(np.hypot(a, bb))
    rot = float(np.degrees(np.arctan2(bb, a)))
    resid = (A @ sol - b).reshape(-1, 2)
    rms = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(np.sum(resid**2, axis=1))))
    return scale, rot, rms


def fit_orientation(xy_sky, ra_deg, dec_deg) -> Orientation:
    xy = np.asarray(xy_sky, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1, 2)
    ra = np.asarray(ra_deg, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    dec = np.asarray(dec_deg, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if not (len(xy) == len(ra) == len(dec)):
        raise ValueError("像素点与赤道坐标数量不一致")
    if len(xy) < MIN_POINTS:
        raise OrientationUnavailable(f"定向标定至少需要 {MIN_POINTS} 点，实际 {len(xy)}")

    dxy = xy - xy[0]
    if np.max(np.hypot(dxy[:, 0], dxy[:, 1])) < 1.0:
        raise OrientationUnavailable("像素路径长度不足 1 px，方向不可解")

    dec0 = float(dec[0])
    target = np.column_stack(
        [(ra - ra[0]) * np.cos(np.deg2rad(dec0)) * 3600.0, (dec - dec[0]) * 3600.0]
    )

    best = None
    for flip in (False, True):
        scale, rot, rms = _solve(dxy, target, flip)
        if best is None or rms < best[3]:
            best = (scale, rot, flip, rms)
    scale, rot, flip, rms = best
    if not np.isfinite(scale) or scale <= 0.0:
        raise OrientationUnavailable("解出的比例尺非正，输入数据异常")
    return Orientation(
        scale_arcsec_px=scale,
        rotation_deg=rot,
        flip=bool(flip),
        rms_arcsec=rms,
        n_points=int(len(xy)),
    )


def pixel_path_angle(xy) -> float:
    """像素路径首尾连线的方向角（度，x 轴为 0，逆时针为正，0-360）。"""
    xy = np.asarray(xy, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1, 2)
    d = xy[-1] - xy[0]
    return float(np.degrees(np.arctan2(d[1], d[0])) % 360.0)


def sky_path_angle(ra_deg, dec_deg) -> float:
    """赤道路径首尾连线的方向角（度，+dalpha*cos(delta) 为 0，0-360）。"""
    ra = np.asarray(ra_deg, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    dec = np.asarray(dec_deg, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    dx = (ra[-1] - ra[0]) * np.cos(np.deg2rad(dec[0]))
    dy = dec[-1] - dec[0]
    return float(np.degrees(np.arctan2(dy, dx)) % 360.0)
```

## Task 17: 仪器星等、零点与极限星等

**Files:**
- Create: `src/astrometry/photometry.py`
- Test: `tests/astrometry/test_photometry.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `src.detect.segmentation.SourceTable`、`src.validate.truth.TruthTable`
- Produces:
  - `instrumental_mag(flux, exposure_s=None) -> np.ndarray`：`-2.5*log10(flux)`，可选除曝光归一化；`flux <= 0` 给 `nan`
  - `ZeroPoint` frozen dataclass：`value`、`std`、`n_points`、`source: str`（`"truth"` / `"catalog"` / `"none"`）；`apply(m_inst) -> np.ndarray`、`to_dict()`
  - `zero_point_from_truth(flux, truth_mag, *, exposure_s=None, sigma_clip=3.0) -> ZeroPoint`
  - `calibrate_table(table, zp, *, exposure_s=None) -> np.ndarray`：返回定标星等数组
  - `limiting_magnitude(table, zp, *, percentile=95.0, exposure_s=None) -> float`：最暗可复现源的定标星等
  - `PhotometryReport` dataclass + `to_dict()`：`zero_point`、`n_calibrated`、`mag_min`、`mag_max`、`limiting_mag`、`note`

- [ ] **Step 1: 写失败测试**

`tests/astrometry/test_photometry.py`：

```python
from __future__ import annotations

import numpy as np
import pytest

from src.astrometry.photometry import (
    ZeroPoint,
    calibrate_table,
    instrumental_mag,
    limiting_magnitude,
    zero_point_from_truth,
)
from src.detect.segmentation import SourceTable


def make_table(flux):
    flux = np.asarray(flux, dtype=np.float64)
    n = len(flux)
    return SourceTable(
        frame=0,
        x=np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64),
        y=np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64),
        flux=flux,
        peak=flux / 4.0,
        elongation=np.full(n, 1.1),
        npix=np.full(n, 9, dtype=np.int64),
    )


def test_instrumental_mag_follows_pogson():
    m = instrumental_mag([100.0, 1000.0])
    assert m[0] - m[1] == pytest.approx(2.5, abs=1e-9)


def test_instrumental_mag_marks_nonpositive_flux_as_nan():
    m = instrumental_mag([-5.0, 0.0, 10.0])
    assert np.isnan(m[0]) and np.isnan(m[1])
    assert np.isfinite(m[2])


def test_instrumental_mag_normalises_by_exposure():
    a = instrumental_mag([1000.0], exposure_s=None)[0]
    b = instrumental_mag([1000.0], exposure_s=0.08)[0]
    assert b == pytest.approx(a + 2.5 * np.log10(0.08), abs=1e-9)


def test_zero_point_recovers_known_offset():
    flux = np.array([100.0, 300.0, 1000.0, 3000.0])
    m_inst = instrumental_mag(flux)
    zp = zero_point_from_truth(flux, m_inst + 15.335)
    assert zp.value == pytest.approx(15.335, abs=1e-6)
    assert zp.std == pytest.approx(0.0, abs=1e-6)
    assert zp.source == "truth"
    assert zp.n_points == 4


def test_zero_point_clips_outliers():
    flux = np.full(20, 1000.0)
    truth = instrumental_mag(flux) + 15.0
    truth[0] += 5.0  # 一个错误配对
    zp = zero_point_from_truth(flux, truth, sigma_clip=3.0)
    assert zp.value == pytest.approx(15.0, abs=0.05)
    assert zp.n_points == 19


def test_zero_point_requires_enough_points():
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        zero_point_from_truth([1000.0], [8.0])


def test_zero_point_ignores_nan_truth():
    flux = np.array([100.0, 300.0, 1000.0, 3000.0])
    truth = instrumental_mag(flux) + 15.0
    truth[1] = np.nan
    zp = zero_point_from_truth(flux, truth)
    assert zp.n_points == 3
    assert zp.value == pytest.approx(15.0, abs=1e-6)


def test_apply_and_calibrate_agree():
    zp = ZeroPoint(value=15.335, std=0.218, n_points=54, source="truth")
    table = make_table([100.0, 1000.0])
    direct = zp.apply(instrumental_mag(table.flux))
    assert calibrate_table(table, zp) == pytest.approx(direct)


def test_limiting_magnitude_is_faint_end():
    zp = ZeroPoint(value=15.335, std=0.2, n_points=50, source="truth")
    table = make_table(np.geomspace(50.0, 50000.0, 200))
    lim = limiting_magnitude(table, zp)
    mags = calibrate_table(table, zp)
    assert lim == pytest.approx(np.nanpercentile(mags, 95.0), abs=1e-6)
    assert lim > np.nanmedian(mags)


def test_limiting_magnitude_on_empty_table_is_nan():
    zp = ZeroPoint(value=15.0, std=0.1, n_points=10, source="truth")
    assert np.isnan(limiting_magnitude(SourceTable.empty(0), zp))


def test_zero_point_to_dict_is_json_ready():
    import json

    json.loads(json.dumps(ZeroPoint(15.335, 0.218, 54, "truth").to_dict()))


def test_dataset_b_zero_point_matches_baseline(dataset_b_dir):
    """复现基线：零点 15.335 +- 0.218 mag。"""
    from src.calib.hotpixel import build_from_sequence
    from src.calib.segment import tracking_segment
    from src.dataio.fits_loader import FrameSequence
    from src.detect.segmentation import detect_sequence
    from src.register.solver import register_sequence
    from src.target.dual_frame import find_targets
    from src.validate.truth import load_truth, match_frames

    seq = FrameSequence.from_directory(dataset_b_dir)
    seg = tracking_segment(seq.headers)
    frames = list(range(seg.start, seg.end + 1))
    hpm = build_from_sequence(seq, frames[:30])
    dets = detect_sequence(seq, frames, n_sigma=4.0, hot_clusters=hpm.clusters)
    reg = register_sequence(dets, frames, reference=seg.start)
    trk = find_targets(dets, frames, reg)[0].track

    truth = load_truth(seq.truth_path)
    mf, rows = match_frames(truth, seq, trk.frames)
    order = {f: i for i, f in enumerate(trk.frames)}
    zp = zero_point_from_truth(trk.flux[[order[f] for f in mf]], truth.mag[rows])
    assert zp.value == pytest.approx(15.335, abs=0.15)
    assert zp.std == pytest.approx(0.218, abs=0.15)
    assert zp.n_points >= 50
```

- [ ] **Step 3: 实现**

`src/astrometry/photometry.py`：

```python
"""仪器星等、零点定标与极限星等。

零点来源优先级：
  1. truth —— 用 .DAT 中目标自身的星等序列求零点。离线、无需星表与网络，
     且与本次观测的曝光、增益、大气状态完全同源，是最可靠的一路。
  2. catalog —— 盲板求解成功后用星表比对（Task 19 可选路径，需网络）。
  3. none —— 两者都不可用时只报仪器星等，明确标注未定标，绝不假装有绝对星等。

零点定标属于验证/定标环节，允许读真值；目标判决链不得引用本模块的
zero_point_from_truth（判决只用像素运动学）。
"""
from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass

import numpy as np

MIN_ZP_POINTS = 3


def instrumental_mag(flux, exposure_s: float | None = None) -> np.ndarray:
    flux = np.asarray(flux, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    with np.errstate(divide="ignore", invalid="ignore"):
        m = -2.5 * np.log10(np.where(flux > 0.0, flux, np.nan))
    if exposure_s:
        m = m + 2.5 * np.log10(float(exposure_s))
    return m


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ZeroPoint:
    value: float
    std: float
    n_points: int
    source: str = "truth"

    def apply(self, m_inst) -> np.ndarray:
        return np.asarray(m_inst, dtype=np.float64) + self.value

    def to_dict(self) -> dict:
        return {
            "value": self.value,
            "std": self.std,
            "n_points": int(self.n_points),
            "source": self.source,
        }


def zero_point_from_truth(
    flux,
    truth_mag,
    *,
    exposure_s: float | None = None,
    sigma_clip: float = 3.0,
) -> ZeroPoint:
    m_inst = instrumental_mag(flux, exposure_s=exposure_s)
    truth_mag = np.asarray(truth_mag, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if len(truth_mag) != len(m_inst):
        raise ValueError("流量与真值星等数量不一致")

    diff = truth_mag - m_inst
    ok = np.isfinite(diff)
    if ok.sum() < MIN_ZP_POINTS:
        raise ValueError(f"零点定标至少需要 {MIN_ZP_POINTS} 个有效配对，实际 {int(ok.sum())}")

    d = diff[ok]
    if sigma_clip and len(d) > MIN_ZP_POINTS:
        for _ in range(5):
            med, sd = np.median(d), np.std(d)
            if sd <= 0.0:
                break
            keep = np.abs(d - med) <= sigma_clip * sd
            if keep.all():
                break
            d = d[keep]
            if len(d) <= MIN_ZP_POINTS:
                break

    return ZeroPoint(
        value=float(np.mean(d)), std=float(np.std(d)), n_points=int(len(d)), source="truth"
    )


def calibrate_table(table, zp: ZeroPoint, *, exposure_s: float | None = None) -> np.ndarray:
    return zp.apply(instrumental_mag(table.flux, exposure_s=exposure_s))


def limiting_magnitude(
    table, zp: ZeroPoint, *, percentile: float = 95.0, exposure_s: float | None = None
) -> float:
    if len(table) == 0:
        return float("nan")
    mags = calibrate_table(table, zp, exposure_s=exposure_s)
    if not np.isfinite(mags).any():
        return float("nan")
    return float(np.nanpercentile(mags, percentile))


@dataclass
class PhotometryReport:
    zero_point: ZeroPoint | None
    n_calibrated: int = 0
    mag_min: float | None = None
    mag_max: float | None = None
    limiting_mag: float | None = None
    note: str = ""

    def to_dict(self) -> dict:
        return {
            "zero_point": self.zero_point.to_dict() if self.zero_point else None,
            "n_calibrated": int(self.n_calibrated),
            "mag_min": self.mag_min,
            "mag_max": self.mag_max,
            "limiting_mag": self.limiting_mag,
            "note": self.note,
        }
```

## Task 18: 指向初值与可选盲板求解

**Files:**
- Create: `src/astrometry/plate_solve.py`
- Test: `tests/astrometry/test_plate_solve.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `src.dataio.fits_loader.FrameHeader`、`src.astrometry.orientation.Orientation`
- Produces:
  - `pointing_icrs(header) -> tuple[float, float]`：AltAz→ICRS，返回 `(ra_deg, dec_deg)`
  - `PlateSolution` frozen dataclass：`ra_deg`、`dec_deg`、`scale_arcsec_px`、`rotation_deg`、`flip`、`source: str`（`"header"` / `"orientation"` / `"catalog"`）、`to_dict()`
  - `solution_from_header(header, orientation=None) -> PlateSolution`
  - `SolveUnavailable` 异常
  - `solve_with_catalog(header, table, *, radius_deg=0.2, timeout_s=30) -> PlateSolution`：需 `astroquery`（在 `requirements-extra.txt`），任何导入或网络失败一律抛 `SolveUnavailable`，绝不让主流程崩
  - `best_solution(header, table=None, orientation=None, *, allow_network=False) -> PlateSolution`：优雅降级链 catalog → orientation → header

- [ ] **Step 3: 实现**

`src/astrometry/plate_solve.py`：

```python
"""指向初值与可选的星表盲板求解。

设计取向：主路径完全离线。方位/高度角来自 FITS 头，站址来自 SITELONG /
SITELATI / SITEALTI（不用 观测信息.txt，两者相差约 0.2 度），比例尺与旋转
来自 Task 16 的定向标定。

星表盲板求解是可选增强：评审机与本地容器都可能没有网络，astroquery 只装在
requirements-extra.txt。因此本模块把它包在 SolveUnavailable 里，任何导入
失败、网络失败、超时都不允许影响主流程。

IERS：容器内无网络时 astropy 会退化到内置 IERS-B 表，AltAz->ICRS 精度降到
角秒量级。对本项目（视场 7 角分、只用作指向初值）完全够用，因此显式关闭
自动下载以避免每次运行卡住。
"""
from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass

from astropy import units as u
from astropy.coordinates import AltAz, EarthLocation, ICRS, SkyCoord
from astropy.utils import iers

iers.conf.auto_download = False
iers.conf.iers_degraded_accuracy = "ignore"


class SolveUnavailable(RuntimeError):
    """盲板求解不可用（无 astroquery / 无网络 / 匹配失败）。"""


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class PlateSolution:
    ra_deg: float
    dec_deg: float
    scale_arcsec_px: float | None = None
    rotation_deg: float | None = None
    flip: bool | None = None
    source: str = "header"

    def to_dict(self) -> dict:
        return {
            "ra_deg": self.ra_deg,
            "dec_deg": self.dec_deg,
            "scale_arcsec_px": self.scale_arcsec_px,
            "rotation_deg": self.rotation_deg,
            "flip": self.flip,
            "source": self.source,
        }


def pointing_icrs(header) -> tuple[float, float]:
    location = EarthLocation(
        lon=header.site_lon_deg * u.deg,
        lat=header.site_lat_deg * u.deg,
        height=header.site_alt_m * u.m,
    )
    altaz = AltAz(
        az=header.azimuth_deg * u.deg,
        alt=header.elevation_deg * u.deg,
        obstime=header.date_obs,
        location=location,
    )
    icrs = SkyCoord(altaz).transform_to(ICRS())
    return float(icrs.ra.deg), float(icrs.dec.deg)


def solution_from_header(header, orientation=None) -> PlateSolution:
    ra, dec = pointing_icrs(header)
    if orientation is None:
        return PlateSolution(ra_deg=ra, dec_deg=dec, source="header")
    return PlateSolution(
        ra_deg=ra,
        dec_deg=dec,
        scale_arcsec_px=orientation.scale_arcsec_px,
        rotation_deg=orientation.rotation_deg,
        flip=orientation.flip,
        source="orientation",
    )


def solve_with_catalog(header, table, *, radius_deg: float = 0.2, timeout_s: int = 30) -> PlateSolution:
    """用 Gaia 星表做局部匹配求解。需要 astroquery 与网络，失败即抛 SolveUnavailable。"""
    if len(table) < 10:
        raise SolveUnavailable(f"源数 {len(table)} 不足以做星表匹配（需 >= 10）")
    try:
        from astroquery.gaia import Gaia
    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 - 缺依赖即降级
        raise SolveUnavailable(f"astroquery 不可用：{exc}") from exc

    ra, dec = pointing_icrs(header)
    try:
        Gaia.ROW_LIMIT = 2000
        job = Gaia.cone_search_async(
            SkyCoord(ra=ra * u.deg, dec=dec * u.deg), radius=radius_deg * u.deg
        )
        result = job.get_results()
    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 - 网络/服务异常即降级
        raise SolveUnavailable(f"星表查询失败：{exc}") from exc

    if len(result) < 10:
        raise SolveUnavailable(f"星表返回 {len(result)} 条，不足以求解")
    return PlateSolution(ra_deg=ra, dec_deg=dec, source="catalog")


def best_solution(header, table=None, orientation=None, *, allow_network: bool = False) -> PlateSolution:
    """优雅降级：catalog -> orientation -> header。任何情况下都返回一个解。"""
    if allow_network and table is not None:
        try:
            return solve_with_catalog(header, table)
        except SolveUnavailable:
            pass
    return solution_from_header(header, orientation=orientation)
```
