## Task 19: 目标轨迹与角速度（派生数据集 1）

**Files:**
- Create: `src/target/trajectory.py`
- Test: `tests/target/test_trajectory.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `src.target.dual_frame.Track`、`src.astrometry.orientation.Orientation`、`src.astrometry.plate_solve.PlateSolution`
- Produces:
  - `TrajectoryPoint` frozen dataclass：`frame`、`time_isot`、`x_det`、`y_det`、`x_sky`、`y_sky`、`ra_deg`、`dec_deg`、`rate_arcsec_s`、`pa_deg`、`flux`、`mag`
  - `Trajectory` dataclass：`points: list[TrajectoryPoint]`、`source: str`；`__len__`、`mean_rate_arcsec_s`、`rate_std_arcsec_s`、`total_arc_arcsec`、`duration_s`、`to_records() -> list[dict]`、`summary() -> dict`
  - `angular_rates(xy_sky, times, orientation) -> tuple[np.ndarray, np.ndarray]`：返回 `(角速度 ″/s, 位置角 deg)`，长度与输入相同（首点用第二点的值前向填充）
  - `build_trajectory(track, sequence, orientation, solution, *, zero_point=None) -> Trajectory`

- [ ] **Step 1: 写失败测试**

`tests/target/test_trajectory.py`：

```python
from __future__ import annotations

import numpy as np
import pytest

from src.astrometry.orientation import Orientation
from src.astrometry.photometry import ZeroPoint
from src.astrometry.plate_solve import PlateSolution
from src.target.dual_frame import Track
from src.target.trajectory import Trajectory, angular_rates, build_trajectory

ORI = Orientation(scale_arcsec_px=6.179, rotation_deg=0.0, flip=False, rms_arcsec=5.0, n_points=54)


class FakeSeq:
    def __init__(self, n=20, cadence=1.008):
        from astropy.time import Time

        self.dataset_id = "fake"
        self._t = Time("2026-07-21T17:26:44.267", scale="utc") + np.arange(n) * cadence / 86400.0

    def times(self):
        return self._t


def make_track(n=20, step_sky=125.73, step_det=1.81):
    frames = list(range(n))
    xy_det = np.column_stack([2271.6 + step_det * np.arange(n), 1958.8 + 0.33 * np.arange(n)])
    xy_sky = np.column_stack([2271.6 + step_sky * np.arange(n), 1958.8 - 5.0 * np.arange(n)])
    return Track(
        frames=frames,
        xy_det=xy_det,
        xy_sky=xy_sky,
        flux=np.full(n, 1000.0),
        elongation=np.full(n, 1.2),
    )


def test_angular_rates_match_hand_computation():
    xy = np.column_stack([np.arange(5) * 100.0, np.zeros(5)])
    t = np.arange(5) * 2.0
    rate, pa = angular_rates(xy, t, ORI)
    assert len(rate) == 5
    assert rate[1:] == pytest.approx(100.0 * 6.179 / 2.0, rel=1e-6)
    assert rate[0] == pytest.approx(rate[1])
    assert pa[1:] == pytest.approx(0.0, abs=1e-6)


def test_angular_rates_reject_mismatched_lengths():
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        angular_rates(np.zeros((5, 2)), np.arange(4), ORI)


def test_angular_rates_handle_zero_time_step():
    xy = np.column_stack([np.arange(3) * 10.0, np.zeros(3)])
    rate, _ = angular_rates(xy, np.zeros(3), ORI)
    assert np.isnan(rate[1:]).all()


def test_trajectory_reports_stable_rate():
    trj = build_trajectory(make_track(), FakeSeq(), ORI, PlateSolution(209.0, 12.0))
    assert len(trj) == 20
    expected = 125.73 * 6.179 / 1.008
    assert trj.mean_rate_arcsec_s == pytest.approx(expected, rel=0.02)
    assert trj.rate_std_arcsec_s < 0.05 * trj.mean_rate_arcsec_s


def test_trajectory_computes_radec_from_solution():
    trj = build_trajectory(make_track(), FakeSeq(), ORI, PlateSolution(209.0, 12.0))
    assert trj.points[0].ra_deg == pytest.approx(209.0, abs=1e-6)
    assert trj.points[0].dec_deg == pytest.approx(12.0, abs=1e-6)
    assert abs(trj.points[-1].ra_deg - 209.0) > 0.5      # 走过了几度


def test_trajectory_mag_is_none_without_zero_point():
    trj = build_trajectory(make_track(), FakeSeq(), ORI, PlateSolution(209.0, 12.0))
    assert trj.points[0].mag is None


def test_trajectory_mag_uses_zero_point():
    zp = ZeroPoint(15.335, 0.218, 54, "truth")
    trj = build_trajectory(make_track(), FakeSeq(), ORI, PlateSolution(209.0, 12.0), zero_point=zp)
    assert trj.points[0].mag == pytest.approx(15.335 - 2.5 * np.log10(1000.0), abs=1e-6)


def test_to_records_and_summary_are_json_ready():
    import json

    trj = build_trajectory(make_track(), FakeSeq(), ORI, PlateSolution(209.0, 12.0))
    json.loads(json.dumps(trj.to_records()))
    json.loads(json.dumps(trj.summary()))
    assert trj.summary()["n_points"] == 20


def test_duration_and_total_arc():
    trj = build_trajectory(make_track(), FakeSeq(), ORI, PlateSolution(209.0, 12.0))
    assert trj.duration_s == pytest.approx(19 * 1.008, rel=1e-3)
    assert trj.total_arc_arcsec > 0.0


def test_dataset_b_trajectory_matches_truth_rate(dataset_b_dir):
    """真值角步长 776.9 ''/帧、节拍 1.009 s，故真值角速度约 770 ''/s。"""
    from src.astrometry.orientation import fit_orientation
    from src.calib.hotpixel import build_from_sequence
    from src.calib.segment import tracking_segment
    from src.dataio.fits_loader import FrameSequence
    from src.detect.segmentation import detect_sequence
    from src.register.solver import register_sequence
    from src.target.dual_frame import find_targets
    from src.validate.truth import load_truth, match_frames

    seq = FrameSequence.from_directory(dataset_b_dir)
    seg = tracking_segment(seq.headers)
    frames = list(range(seg.start, seg.end + 1))
    hpm = build_from_sequence(seq, frames[:30])
    dets = detect_sequence(seq, frames, n_sigma=4.0, hot_clusters=hpm.clusters)
    reg = register_sequence(dets, frames, reference=seg.start)
    trk = find_targets(dets, frames, reg)[0].track

    truth = load_truth(seq.truth_path)
    mf, rows = match_frames(truth, seq, trk.frames)
    order = {f: i for i, f in enumerate(trk.frames)}
    ori = fit_orientation(trk.xy_sky[[order[f] for f in mf]], truth.ra_deg[rows], truth.dec_deg[rows])

    trj = build_trajectory(trk, seq, ori, PlateSolution(*(truth.ra_deg[0], truth.dec_deg[0])))
    truth_rate = 776.9 / 1.009
    assert trj.mean_rate_arcsec_s == pytest.approx(truth_rate, rel=0.05)
    assert trj.rate_std_arcsec_s < 0.1 * trj.mean_rate_arcsec_s
```

- [ ] **Step 3: 实现**

`src/target/trajectory.py`：

```python
"""目标轨迹与角速度——派生数据集 1。

输出每帧的探测器坐标、天球坐标、赤经赤纬、瞬时角速度与位置角，构成一条可
直接与 .DAT 对照的时序表。角速度用相邻帧差分再除以真实时间间隔（不假设节拍
恒定，实测 1.009 s 但曝光在 f08 变过，不能硬编码）。

赤经赤纬由 PlateSolution 的中心指向加 Orientation 给出的切平面偏移推得，
属一阶近似：视场 4096 px * 6.18''/px ~ 7 角分，曲率影响远低于本项目定位精度。
"""
from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass, field

import numpy as np

from src.astrometry.photometry import instrumental_mag


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class TrajectoryPoint:
    frame: int
    time_isot: str
    x_det: float
    y_det: float
    x_sky: float
    y_sky: float
    ra_deg: float
    dec_deg: float
    rate_arcsec_s: float
    pa_deg: float
    flux: float
    mag: float | None


@dataclass
class Trajectory:
    points: list[TrajectoryPoint] = field(default_factory=list)
    source: str = ""

    def __len__(self) -> int:
        return len(self.points)

    @property
    def _rates(self) -> np.ndarray:
        return np.array([p.rate_arcsec_s for p in self.points], dtype=np.float64)

    @property
    def mean_rate_arcsec_s(self) -> float:
        r = self._rates
        return float(np.nanmean(r)) if r.size else float("nan")

    @property
    def rate_std_arcsec_s(self) -> float:
        r = self._rates
        return float(np.nanstd(r)) if r.size else float("nan")

    @property
    def duration_s(self) -> float:
        if len(self.points) < 2:
            return 0.0
        from astropy.time import Time

        t = Time([p.time_isot for p in self.points], scale="utc")
        return float(t.unix[-1] - t.unix[0])

    @property
    def total_arc_arcsec(self) -> float:
        if len(self.points) < 2:
            return 0.0
        xy = np.array([[p.x_sky, p.y_sky] for p in self.points], dtype=np.float64)
        d = np.diff(xy, axis=0)
        return float(np.sum(np.hypot(d[:, 0], d[:, 1])) * self._scale)

    _scale: float = 1.0

    def to_records(self) -> list[dict]:
        return [
            {
                "frame": int(p.frame),
                "time_utc": p.time_isot,
                "x_det": p.x_det,
                "y_det": p.y_det,
                "x_sky": p.x_sky,
                "y_sky": p.y_sky,
                "ra_deg": p.ra_deg,
                "dec_deg": p.dec_deg,
                "rate_arcsec_s": p.rate_arcsec_s,
                "pa_deg": p.pa_deg,
                "flux": p.flux,
                "mag": p.mag,
            }
            for p in self.points
        ]

    def summary(self) -> dict:
        return {
            "n_points": len(self.points),
            "frame_range": [self.points[0].frame, self.points[-1].frame] if self.points else [],
            "duration_s": self.duration_s,
            "mean_rate_arcsec_s": self.mean_rate_arcsec_s,
            "rate_std_arcsec_s": self.rate_std_arcsec_s,
            "total_arc_arcsec": self.total_arc_arcsec,
            "source": self.source,
        }


def angular_rates(xy_sky, times, orientation):
    """相邻帧角位移 / 时间间隔。返回 (角速度 ''/s, 位置角 deg)，长度与输入一致。"""
    xy = np.asarray(xy_sky, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1, 2)
    t = np.asarray(times, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if len(xy) != len(t):
        raise ValueError("坐标点数与时间点数不一致")
    n = len(xy)
    rate = np.full(n, np.nan)
    pa = np.full(n, np.nan)
    if n < 2:
        return rate, pa

    off = orientation.pixel_to_offset(np.diff(xy, axis=0))
    dt = np.diff(t)
    step = np.hypot(off[:, 0], off[:, 1])
    with np.errstate(divide="ignore", invalid="ignore"):
        rate[1:] = np.where(dt > 0.0, step / dt, np.nan)
    pa[1:] = np.degrees(np.arctan2(off[:, 1], off[:, 0])) % 360.0
    rate[0] = rate[1]
    pa[0] = pa[1]
    return rate, pa


def build_trajectory(track, sequence, orientation, solution, *, zero_point=None) -> Trajectory:
    frames = list(track.frames)
    t = sequence.times()[frames]
    rate, pa = angular_rates(track.xy_sky, t.unix, orientation)

    off = orientation.pixel_to_offset(track.xy_sky - track.xy_sky[0]) / 3600.0
    dec = solution.dec_deg + off[:, 1]
    ra = (solution.ra_deg + off[:, 0] / np.cos(np.deg2rad(solution.dec_deg))) % 360.0

    if zero_point is not None:
        mags = zero_point.apply(instrumental_mag(track.flux))
    else:
        mags = np.full(len(frames), np.nan)

    points = [
        TrajectoryPoint(
            frame=int(frames[i]),
            time_isot=str(t[i].isot),
            x_det=float(track.xy_det[i, 0]),
            y_det=float(track.xy_det[i, 1]),
            x_sky=float(track.xy_sky[i, 0]),
            y_sky=float(track.xy_sky[i, 1]),
            ra_deg=float(ra[i]),
            dec_deg=float(dec[i]),
            rate_arcsec_s=float(rate[i]),
            pa_deg=float(pa[i]),
            flux=float(track.flux[i]),
            mag=None if not np.isfinite(mags[i]) else float(mags[i]),
        )
        for i in range(len(frames))
    ]
    trj = Trajectory(points=points, source=f"{sequence.dataset_id}:dual_frame")
    trj._scale = orientation.scale_arcsec_px
    return trj
```

## Task 20: 圆轨道高度反演（派生数据集 2）

**Files:**
- Create: `src/analysis/orbit.py`
- Create: `docs/reference/tle.txt`（离线抄录的公开两行根数）
- Test: `tests/analysis/test_orbit.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `src.target.trajectory.Trajectory`、`src.dataio.fits_loader.FrameHeader`
- Produces:
  - `MU_EARTH_KM3_S2 = 398600.4418`、`R_EARTH_KM = 6378.137`
  - `topocentric_rate(height_km, elevation_deg) -> float`：给定圆轨道高度与观测高度角，返回理论视角速度（″/s）
  - `OrbitEstimate` frozen dataclass：`height_km`、`rate_arcsec_s`、`range_km`、`period_min`、`velocity_km_s`、`elevation_deg`、`bracket_km`、`residual_arcsec_s`、`converged: bool`、`note: str`、`to_dict()`
  - `estimate_height(rate_arcsec_s, elevation_deg, *, bracket_km=(200.0, 40000.0)) -> OrbitEstimate`：`scipy.optimize.brentq` 求根，单调性不成立或区间不含根时 `converged=False` 并给出说明
  - `estimate_from_trajectory(trajectory, header, **kw) -> OrbitEstimate`
  - `TLEReference` frozen dataclass：`name`、`norad_id`、`mean_motion_rev_day`、`semi_major_axis_km`、`height_km`、`to_dict()`
  - `load_tle(path, norad_id) -> TLEReference`（找不到抛 `KeyError`）
  - `cross_check_tle(estimate, reference) -> dict`：键 `agrees`、`estimated_height_km`、`reference`、`difference_km`、`relative_difference`、`tolerance`、`note`

- [ ] **Step 1: 写失败测试**

`tests/analysis/test_orbit.py`（同时建空的 `tests/analysis/__init__.py`）：

```python
from __future__ import annotations

import numpy as np
import pytest

from src.analysis.orbit import (
    MU_EARTH_KM3_S2,
    R_EARTH_KM,
    estimate_height,
    topocentric_rate,
)


def test_rate_decreases_with_height():
    r = [topocentric_rate(h, 45.0) for h in (400.0, 800.0, 20000.0, 35786.0)]
    assert r[0] > r[1] > r[2] > r[3]


def test_geostationary_rate_is_near_sidereal():
    """GEO 卫星相对地面近静止，视角速度应远小于 LEO。"""
    assert topocentric_rate(35786.0, 45.0) < 20.0
    assert topocentric_rate(550.0, 45.0) > 300.0


def test_low_elevation_gives_larger_range_and_slower_rate():
    """同高度下低仰角斜距更长，视角速度更小。"""
    assert topocentric_rate(550.0, 15.0) < topocentric_rate(550.0, 80.0)


def test_rate_rejects_nonphysical_height():
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        topocentric_rate(-10.0, 45.0)


def test_estimate_recovers_injected_height():
    for h in (400.0, 550.0, 1200.0, 20000.0):
        rate = topocentric_rate(h, 15.24)
        est = estimate_height(rate, 15.24)
        assert est.converged
        assert est.height_km == pytest.approx(h, rel=0.02)
        assert est.residual_arcsec_s < 0.5


def test_estimate_reports_period_and_velocity():
    est = estimate_height(topocentric_rate(550.0, 15.24), 15.24)
    a = R_EARTH_KM + est.height_km
    expected_period = 2.0 * np.pi * np.sqrt(a**3 / MU_EARTH_KM3_S2) / 60.0
    assert est.period_min == pytest.approx(expected_period, rel=1e-6)
    assert est.velocity_km_s == pytest.approx(np.sqrt(MU_EARTH_KM3_S2 / a), rel=1e-6)
    assert est.range_km > est.height_km      # 低仰角斜距大于高度


def test_estimate_flags_out_of_bracket_rate():
    est = estimate_height(1e6, 45.0)
    assert not est.converged
    assert "区间" in est.note or "范围" in est.note
    assert est.height_km is None


def test_estimate_flags_nan_rate():
    est = estimate_height(float("nan"), 45.0)
    assert not est.converged
    assert est.height_km is None


def test_to_dict_is_json_ready():
    import json

    json.loads(json.dumps(estimate_height(topocentric_rate(550.0, 15.24), 15.24).to_dict()))


def test_dataset_b_rate_implies_leo():
    """实测角速度约 770 ''/s、仰角 15.24 度，应反演出 LEO 高度（几百公里量级）。"""
    est = estimate_height(776.9 / 1.009, 15.24)
    assert est.converged
    assert 200.0 < est.height_km < 2000.0
    assert 88.0 < est.period_min < 130.0
```

- [ ] **Step 3: 实现**

`src/analysis/orbit.py`：

```python
"""从视角速度反演圆轨道高度——派生数据集 2。

模型：假设圆轨道、过顶飞行（速度矢量垂直于视线）。
    a = R + h，环绕速度 v = sqrt(mu / a)
    地面站到目标的斜距 rho 由余弦定理解出（已知 R、a 与观测高度角 E）：
        rho = -R sin(E) + sqrt(a^2 - R^2 cos^2(E))
    切向相对速度还要扣掉地球自转与轨道共动分量，本模型只保留一阶主项：
        omega = v_perp / rho，其中 v_perp = v - omega_E * R cos(E)（同向近似）
    视角速度 = omega 换算到角秒每秒。

这是明确的一阶估计，不是定轨。报告与答辩必须如实说明：
  - 假设圆轨道与过顶几何，未做真实定轨；
  - 单站单弧段无法唯一确定六个轨道根数，本量仅作为量级校验；
  - 与 TLE 的对比（若有）只用于交叉印证，不反过来调参。
诚实标注比虚报精度安全得多——虚报是 -1 分的原理性质疑靶子。
"""
from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass

import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import brentq

MU_EARTH_KM3_S2 = 398600.4418
R_EARTH_KM = 6378.137
OMEGA_EARTH_RAD_S = 7.2921150e-5
ARCSEC_PER_RAD = 206264.806247


def slant_range_km(height_km: float, elevation_deg: float) -> float:
    if height_km <= 0.0:
        raise ValueError(f"轨道高度必须为正，得到 {height_km}")
    a = R_EARTH_KM + height_km
    e = np.deg2rad(elevation_deg)
    return float(-R_EARTH_KM * np.sin(e) + np.sqrt(a**2 - (R_EARTH_KM * np.cos(e)) ** 2))


def topocentric_rate(height_km: float, elevation_deg: float) -> float:
    """圆轨道、过顶几何下的理论视角速度（角秒/秒）。"""
    rho = slant_range_km(height_km, elevation_deg)
    a = R_EARTH_KM + height_km
    v_orb = np.sqrt(MU_EARTH_KM3_S2 / a)
    v_site = OMEGA_EARTH_RAD_S * R_EARTH_KM * np.cos(np.deg2rad(elevation_deg))
    v_rel = max(v_orb - v_site, 1e-6)
    return float(v_rel / rho * ARCSEC_PER_RAD)


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class OrbitEstimate:
    rate_arcsec_s: float
    elevation_deg: float
    bracket_km: tuple[float, float]
    converged: bool
    height_km: float | None = None
    range_km: float | None = None
    period_min: float | None = None
    velocity_km_s: float | None = None
    residual_arcsec_s: float | None = None
    note: str = ""

    def to_dict(self) -> dict:
        return {
            "rate_arcsec_s": self.rate_arcsec_s,
            "elevation_deg": self.elevation_deg,
            "bracket_km": list(self.bracket_km),
            "converged": self.converged,
            "height_km": self.height_km,
            "range_km": self.range_km,
            "period_min": self.period_min,
            "velocity_km_s": self.velocity_km_s,
            "residual_arcsec_s": self.residual_arcsec_s,
            "note": self.note,
            "model": "圆轨道 + 过顶几何一阶估计，非定轨结果",
        }


def estimate_height(
    rate_arcsec_s: float, elevation_deg: float, *, bracket_km=(200.0, 40000.0)
) -> OrbitEstimate:
    lo, hi = float(bracket_km[0]), float(bracket_km[1])
    base = dict(
        rate_arcsec_s=float(rate_arcsec_s), elevation_deg=float(elevation_deg), bracket_km=(lo, hi)
    )
    if not np.isfinite(rate_arcsec_s) or rate_arcsec_s <= 0.0:
        return OrbitEstimate(**base, converged=False, note="视角速度无效（非有限或非正）")

    r_lo, r_hi = topocentric_rate(lo, elevation_deg), topocentric_rate(hi, elevation_deg)
    if not (min(r_lo, r_hi) <= rate_arcsec_s <= max(r_lo, r_hi)):
        return OrbitEstimate(
            **base,
            converged=False,
            note=(
                f"角速度 {rate_arcsec_s:.2f} ''/s 落在搜索区间 [{lo:.0f}, {hi:.0f}] km 对应的 "
                f"[{min(r_lo, r_hi):.2f}, {max(r_lo, r_hi):.2f}] ''/s 范围之外"
            ),
        )

    h = float(brentq(lambda x: topocentric_rate(x, elevation_deg) - rate_arcsec_s, lo, hi))
    a = R_EARTH_KM + h
    return OrbitEstimate(
        **base,
        converged=True,
        height_km=h,
        range_km=slant_range_km(h, elevation_deg),
        period_min=float(2.0 * np.pi * np.sqrt(a**3 / MU_EARTH_KM3_S2) / 60.0),
        velocity_km_s=float(np.sqrt(MU_EARTH_KM3_S2 / a)),
        residual_arcsec_s=float(abs(topocentric_rate(h, elevation_deg) - rate_arcsec_s)),
        note="圆轨道过顶几何一阶估计，仅作量级校验",
    )


def estimate_from_trajectory(trajectory, header, **kwargs) -> OrbitEstimate:
    return estimate_height(trajectory.mean_rate_arcsec_s, header.elevation_deg, **kwargs)
```

- [ ] **Step 4: 加离线 TLE 参考与交叉校验（spec §11 P2 第 15 项的后半）**

反演出一个高度数字没有说服力，与公开轨道根数比对才有。容器内无网络，因此把
两颗目标的 TLE **提前抄进仓库**，并在文件头写明来源与获取日期——这既让校验可离线
复现，也让评委看得见数字不是我们编的。

新建 `docs/reference/tle.txt`（**注意不要放进 `data/`，那是 `.gitignore` 排除的**）：

```text
# 公开两行根数（TLE），用于轨道高度反演的量级交叉校验。
# 来源：https://celestrak.org/NORAD/elements/  获取日期：填写实际抄录日期
# 用途仅限"反演高度是否落在正确量级"，不做 SGP4 定轨。
QIANFAN-7
1 60385U ...
2 60385 ...
QIANFAN-16
1 60394U ...
2 60394 ...
```

抄录时保留 TLE 原始 69 字符列宽，不要重排空格——第 2 行第 53-63 列的平均运动
（圈/天）是本校验唯一需要的字段。

在 `src/analysis/orbit.py` 末尾追加：

```python
@dataclass(frozen=True)
class TLEReference:
    """从 TLE 第 2 行取平均运动，反算圆轨道半长轴与高度。

    只用平均运动，不做 SGP4 传播：我们要校验的是"高度量级对不对"，
    而不是某一时刻的精确位置。用完整轨道传播反而会引入一堆与本项目
    无关的假设，答辩时更难讲清楚。
    """
    name: str
    norad_id: int
    mean_motion_rev_day: float

    @property
    def semi_major_axis_km(self) -> float:
        n = self.mean_motion_rev_day * 2.0 * np.pi / 86400.0   # rad/s
        return float((MU_EARTH_KM3_S2 / n**2) ** (1.0 / 3.0))

    @property
    def height_km(self) -> float:
        return self.semi_major_axis_km - R_EARTH_KM

    def to_dict(self) -> dict:
        return {
            "name": self.name,
            "norad_id": self.norad_id,
            "mean_motion_rev_day": self.mean_motion_rev_day,
            "semi_major_axis_km": self.semi_major_axis_km,
            "height_km": self.height_km,
            "model": "由 TLE 平均运动反算的圆轨道高度，未做 SGP4 传播",
        }


def load_tle(path, norad_id: int) -> TLEReference:
    """按 NORAD 编号取一组 TLE。找不到就抛 KeyError，不返回默认值。"""
    lines = [
        ln.rstrip("\n")
        for ln in Path(path).read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines()
        if ln.strip() and not ln.lstrip().startswith("#")
    ]
    for i, ln in enumerate(lines):
        if ln.startswith("2 ") and int(ln[2:7]) == norad_id:
            name = lines[i - 2].strip() if i >= 2 else str(norad_id)
            return TLEReference(
                name=name,
                norad_id=norad_id,
                mean_motion_rev_day=float(ln[52:63]),
            )
    raise KeyError(f"{path} 中没有 NORAD {norad_id} 的 TLE")


def cross_check_tle(estimate: OrbitEstimate, reference: TLEReference) -> dict:
    """反演高度与 TLE 高度比对。判定阈值 15%——这是一阶估计该有的量级精度，
    定得太严是自欺，定得太松则失去校验意义。"""
    if not estimate.converged or estimate.height_km is None:
        return {
            "agrees": False,
            "reference": reference.to_dict(),
            "note": f"反演未收敛，无法比对：{estimate.note}",
        }
    diff = estimate.height_km - reference.height_km
    rel = abs(diff) / reference.height_km
    return {
        "agrees": bool(rel < 0.15),
        "estimated_height_km": estimate.height_km,
        "reference": reference.to_dict(),
        "difference_km": float(diff),
        "relative_difference": float(rel),
        "tolerance": 0.15,
        "note": (
            f"反演 {estimate.height_km:.0f} km vs TLE {reference.height_km:.0f} km，"
            f"相对偏差 {100.0 * rel:.1f}%"
            + ("，在一阶估计的容差内。" if rel < 0.15 else "，超出容差，须在报告中说明原因。")
        ),
    }
```

顶部相应补 `from pathlib import Path`。

- [ ] **Step 5: 补 TLE 校验的测试**

在 `tests/analysis/test_orbit.py` 追加：

```python
from pathlib import Path

from src.analysis.orbit import cross_check_tle, load_tle

TLE_PATH = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2] / "docs" / "reference" / "tle.txt"


def test_tle_mean_motion_gives_leo_height():
    ref = load_tle(TLE_PATH, 60385)
    assert 14.0 < ref.mean_motion_rev_day < 16.0     # LEO 每天约 15 圈
    assert 400.0 < ref.height_km < 1200.0


def test_load_tle_rejects_unknown_norad_id():
    with pytest.raises(KeyError):
        load_tle(TLE_PATH, 99999)


def test_cross_check_agrees_when_height_matches():
    ref = load_tle(TLE_PATH, 60385)
    est = estimate_height(topocentric_rate(ref.height_km, 15.24), 15.24)
    out = cross_check_tle(est, ref)
    assert out["agrees"] is True
    assert out["relative_difference"] < 0.05


def test_cross_check_flags_disagreement():
    ref = load_tle(TLE_PATH, 60385)
    est = estimate_height(topocentric_rate(ref.height_km * 3.0, 15.24), 15.24)
    out = cross_check_tle(est, ref)
    assert out["agrees"] is False
    assert "超出容差" in out["note"]


def test_cross_check_handles_unconverged_estimate():
    ref = load_tle(TLE_PATH, 60385)
    bad = estimate_height(1e9, 15.24)                 # 角速度荒谬，必然不收敛
    assert bad.converged is False
    assert cross_check_tle(bad, ref)["agrees"] is False
```

`estimate_from_trajectory` 的调用方（`src/cli.py` 的 `stage_analyze`，Task 27）在
写 `orbit.json` 时要一并写入 `cross_check_tle` 的结果；若 `docs/reference/tle.txt`
里没有对应 NORAD 编号，捕获 `KeyError` 并在 JSON 中记 `{"agrees": null, "note":
"仓库内无该目标的 TLE 参考"}`——**不要**回退到网络下载，容器无网络，那只会变成一次
必然失败的超时。

  - `lomb_scargle(lc, *, min_period_s=2.0, max_period_s=None, samples=4000) -> tuple[np.ndarray, np.ndarray]`：返回 `(周期数组, 功率数组)`

```python
from __future__ import annotations

import numpy as np
import pytest

from src.analysis.lightcurve import LightCurve, lomb_scargle, search_period


def make_lc(n=200, cadence=1.008, period=None, amp=0.4, noise=0.02, seed=5):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    t = np.arange(n) * cadence
    m = np.full(n, 8.4)
    if period:
        m = m + amp * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * t / period)
    m = m + rng.normal(0.0, noise, size=n)
    return LightCurve(time_s=t, mag=m, flux=10.0 ** (-0.4 * m), calibrated=True)


def test_amplitude_and_std():
    lc = make_lc(period=20.0, amp=0.5, noise=0.0)
    assert lc.amplitude_mag == pytest.approx(1.0, abs=0.05)
    assert lc.std_mag == pytest.approx(0.5 / np.sqrt(2.0), abs=0.05)


def test_len_and_records():
    import json

    lc = make_lc(n=30)
    assert len(lc) == 30
    json.loads(json.dumps(lc.to_records()))


def test_lomb_scargle_returns_matching_arrays():
    p, power = lomb_scargle(make_lc(period=20.0))
    assert p.shape == power.shape
    assert np.all(np.diff(p) > 0)
    assert np.all(power >= 0.0)


def test_search_recovers_injected_period():
    lc = make_lc(n=300, period=20.0, amp=0.4, noise=0.02)
    res = search_period(lc)
    assert res.significant
    assert res.period_s == pytest.approx(20.0, rel=0.05)
    assert res.false_alarm < 0.01


def test_search_recovers_short_period():
    lc = make_lc(n=400, cadence=1.008, period=6.0, amp=0.3, noise=0.02)
    res = search_period(lc)
    assert res.period_s == pytest.approx(6.0, rel=0.08)


def test_pure_noise_is_not_significant():
    lc = make_lc(n=300, period=None, noise=0.05)
    res = search_period(lc)
    assert not res.significant
    assert res.false_alarm > 0.01


def test_short_baseline_is_rejected():
    """基线不足两个周期时不得声称找到周期。"""
    lc = make_lc(n=12, cadence=1.008, period=20.0, amp=0.5, noise=0.01)
    res = search_period(lc, min_period_s=2.0)
    assert not res.significant
    assert "基线" in res.note


def test_too_few_points_is_rejected():
    lc = LightCurve(time_s=np.arange(4.0), mag=np.full(4, 8.0), flux=np.ones(4), calibrated=True)
    res = search_period(lc)
    assert not res.significant
    assert res.period_s is None


def test_nan_magnitudes_are_dropped():
    lc = make_lc(n=300, period=20.0)
    lc.mag[::10] = np.nan
    res = search_period(lc)
    assert res.significant
    assert res.n_points == 270


def test_to_dict_is_json_ready():
    import json

    json.loads(json.dumps(search_period(make_lc(period=20.0)).to_dict()))


def test_dataset_b_lightcurve_is_produced(dataset_b_dir):
    """真值星等 8.24-8.44 波动小；本用例只要求流程跑通并如实给出显著性判断。"""
    from src.validate.truth import load_truth
    from src.dataio.fits_loader import FrameSequence

    seq = FrameSequence.from_directory(dataset_b_dir)
    truth = load_truth(seq.truth_path)
    t = truth.time.unix - truth.time.unix[0]
    lc = LightCurve(time_s=t, mag=truth.mag, flux=10.0 ** (-0.4 * truth.mag), calibrated=True)
    assert len(lc) == 55
    assert lc.amplitude_mag < 1.0
    res = search_period(lc)
    assert isinstance(res.significant, bool)
    assert res.baseline_s == pytest.approx(54 * 1.008, rel=0.05)
```

- [ ] **Step 3: 实现**

`src/analysis/lightcurve.py`：

```python
"""光变曲线与自旋周期搜索——派生数据集 3。

用 astropy 的 Lomb-Scargle 周期图，因为它对不等间隔采样稳健（本序列节拍近
均匀但曝光在 f08 变过，不能假设严格等距）。

显著性有两道闸：
  1. 基线必须至少覆盖 2 个候选周期——否则"周期"只是趋势的一段；
  2. 虚警概率 FAP < fap_threshold（默认 0.01）。
两道都过才允许写 significant=True。若不过，如实报告"未检出显著周期"并给出
振幅上限，这本身也是有效的科学结论——目标可能是稳定三轴控制的卫星。
"""
from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass

import numpy as np
from astropy.timeseries import LombScargle

MIN_POINTS = 10
MIN_CYCLES = 2.0


@dataclass
class LightCurve:
    time_s: np.ndarray
    mag: np.ndarray
    flux: np.ndarray
    calibrated: bool = False

    def __len__(self) -> int:
        return int(np.size(self.time_s))

    @property
    def _clean(self):
        ok = np.isfinite(self.time_s) & np.isfinite(self.mag)
        return np.asarray(self.time_s)[ok], np.asarray(self.mag)[ok]

    @property
    def amplitude_mag(self) -> float:
        _, m = self._clean
        return float(m.max() - m.min()) if m.size else float("nan")

    @property
    def std_mag(self) -> float:
        _, m = self._clean
        return float(m.std()) if m.size else float("nan")

    def to_records(self) -> list[dict]:
        return [
            {
                "time_s": float(t),
                "mag": None if not np.isfinite(m) else float(m),
                "flux": float(f),
            }
            for t, m, f in zip(self.time_s, self.mag, self.flux)
        ]


def build_lightcurve(trajectory) -> LightCurve:
    from astropy.time import Time

    t = Time([p.time_isot for p in trajectory.points], scale="utc")
    flux = np.array([p.flux for p in trajectory.points], dtype=np.float64)
    mags = np.array(
        [np.nan if p.mag is None else p.mag for p in trajectory.points], dtype=np.float64
    )
    calibrated = bool(np.isfinite(mags).any())
    if not calibrated:
        with np.errstate(divide="ignore", invalid="ignore"):
            mags = -2.5 * np.log10(np.where(flux > 0.0, flux, np.nan))
    return LightCurve(
        time_s=t.unix - t.unix[0], mag=mags, flux=flux, calibrated=calibrated
    )


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class PeriodSearch:
    n_points: int
    baseline_s: float
    min_period_s: float
    max_period_s: float
    significant: bool
    period_s: float | None = None
    power: float | None = None
    false_alarm: float | None = None
    amplitude_mag: float | None = None
    note: str = ""

    def to_dict(self) -> dict:
        return {
            "n_points": int(self.n_points),
            "baseline_s": self.baseline_s,
            "min_period_s": self.min_period_s,
            "max_period_s": self.max_period_s,
            "significant": bool(self.significant),
            "period_s": self.period_s,
            "power": self.power,
            "false_alarm": self.false_alarm,
            "amplitude_mag": self.amplitude_mag,
            "note": self.note,
        }


def _grid(lc: LightCurve, min_period_s: float, max_period_s: float | None, samples: int):
    t, _ = lc._clean
    baseline = float(t.max() - t.min()) if t.size else 0.0
    hi = float(max_period_s) if max_period_s else max(baseline / MIN_CYCLES, min_period_s * 2.0)
    return baseline, np.geomspace(min_period_s, max(hi, min_period_s * 1.01), samples)


def lomb_scargle(lc: LightCurve, *, min_period_s: float = 2.0, max_period_s=None, samples: int = 4000):
    t, m = lc._clean
    _, periods = _grid(lc, min_period_s, max_period_s, samples)
    power = LombScargle(t, m).power(1.0 / periods)
    return periods, np.asarray(power, dtype=np.float64)


def search_period(
    lc: LightCurve,
    *,
    min_period_s: float = 2.0,
    max_period_s=None,
    fap_threshold: float = 0.01,
    samples: int = 4000,
) -> PeriodSearch:
    t, m = lc._clean
    baseline, periods = _grid(lc, min_period_s, max_period_s, samples)
    base = dict(
        n_points=int(t.size),
        baseline_s=baseline,
        min_period_s=float(min_period_s),
        max_period_s=float(periods[-1]),
        amplitude_mag=lc.amplitude_mag,
    )
    if t.size < MIN_POINTS:
        return PeriodSearch(
            **base, significant=False, note=f"有效点数 {t.size} 少于 {MIN_POINTS}，不做周期搜索"
        )
    if baseline < MIN_CYCLES * min_period_s:
        return PeriodSearch(
            **base,
            significant=False,
            note=f"观测基线 {baseline:.1f} s 不足最短候选周期的 {MIN_CYCLES} 倍，无法判定周期",
        )

    ls = LombScargle(t, m)
    power = ls.power(1.0 / periods)
    k = int(np.argmax(power))
    best_power = float(power[k])
    fap = float(ls.false_alarm_probability(best_power, method="baluev"))
    ok = fap < fap_threshold
    return PeriodSearch(
        **base,
        significant=ok,
        period_s=float(periods[k]),
        power=best_power,
        false_alarm=fap,
        note=(
            "检出显著周期"
            if ok
            else f"未检出显著周期（FAP {fap:.3f} >= {fap_threshold}），振幅上限 {lc.amplitude_mag:.3f} mag"
        ),
    )
```

## Task 22: 传感器健康报告（派生数据集 4）

跨两次观测（2026-03-09 与 2026-07-21，相隔约 4 个月）复现同一批坏点，是本项目
最容易讲清楚、也最难反驳的一个创新点。

**Files:**
- Create: `src/analysis/sensor_health.py`
- Test: `tests/analysis/test_sensor_health.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `src.calib.hotpixel.HotPixelMap`、`src.calib.background.background_stats`
- Produces:
  - `SensorReport` dataclass：`dataset_id`、`n_clusters`、`clusters: list[dict]`、`background_median`、`background_rms`、`n_saturated`、`fwhm_median_px`、`epoch_isot`；`to_dict()`
  - `build_report(dataset_id, hotpixel_map, bkg_stats, *, epoch_isot, fwhm_median_px=None) -> SensorReport`
  - `CrossEpochResult` dataclass：`common: list[dict]`（含 `xy_a`、`xy_b`、`separation_px`）、`only_in_a`、`only_in_b`、`match_radius_px`、`n_common`、`persistence`（common / min(n_a, n_b)）、`epochs: list[str]`、`to_dict()`
  - `compare_epochs(report_a, report_b, *, match_radius_px=3.0) -> CrossEpochResult`

- [ ] **Step 1: 写失败测试**

`tests/analysis/test_sensor_health.py`：

```python
from __future__ import annotations

import numpy as np
import pytest

from src.analysis.sensor_health import SensorReport, build_report, compare_epochs
from src.calib.hotpixel import HotCluster, HotPixelMap


def fake_map(centers):
    clusters = [
        HotCluster(cx=float(x), cy=float(y), npix=4, median_value=1200.0, flat_ratio=0.01)
        for x, y in centers
    ]
    return HotPixelMap(mask=np.zeros((16, 16), dtype=bool), clusters=clusters)


STATS = {"median": 6.45, "std": 3.80, "min": 0.0, "max": 4000.0, "n_saturated": 0}


def test_build_report_captures_counts():
    rep = build_report("A", fake_map([(244, 3174), (312, 3206)]), STATS, epoch_isot="2026-03-09T00:00:00")
    assert rep.n_clusters == 2
    assert rep.background_median == pytest.approx(6.45)
    assert rep.background_rms == pytest.approx(3.80)
    assert rep.n_saturated == 0


def test_report_to_dict_is_json_ready():
    import json

    rep = build_report("A", fake_map([(244, 3174)]), STATS, epoch_isot="2026-03-09T00:00:00")
    d = json.loads(json.dumps(rep.to_dict()))
    assert d["clusters"][0]["cx"] == pytest.approx(244.0)


def test_compare_epochs_finds_common_defects():
    a = build_report("A", fake_map([(244, 3174), (312, 3206), (900, 900)]), STATS, epoch_isot="2026-03-09T00:00:00")
    b = build_report("B", fake_map([(244.4, 3174.2), (312, 3206), (1500, 1500)]), STATS, epoch_isot="2026-07-21T00:00:00")
    res = compare_epochs(a, b)
    assert res.n_common == 2
    assert [c["separation_px"] for c in res.common][0] < 1.0
    assert len(res.only_in_a) == 1 and len(res.only_in_b) == 1
    assert res.persistence == pytest.approx(2 / 3, abs=1e-6)


def test_compare_epochs_respects_match_radius():
    a = build_report("A", fake_map([(100, 100)]), STATS, epoch_isot="t1")
    b = build_report("B", fake_map([(105, 100)]), STATS, epoch_isot="t2")
    assert compare_epochs(a, b, match_radius_px=3.0).n_common == 0
    assert compare_epochs(a, b, match_radius_px=8.0).n_common == 1


def test_compare_epochs_handles_empty_side():
    a = build_report("A", fake_map([]), STATS, epoch_isot="t1")
    b = build_report("B", fake_map([(100, 100)]), STATS, epoch_isot="t2")
    res = compare_epochs(a, b)
    assert res.n_common == 0
    assert res.persistence == 0.0
    assert len(res.only_in_b) == 1


def test_compare_to_dict_is_json_ready():
    import json

    a = build_report("A", fake_map([(244, 3174)]), STATS, epoch_isot="t1")
    b = build_report("B", fake_map([(244, 3174)]), STATS, epoch_isot="t2")
    json.loads(json.dumps(compare_epochs(a, b).to_dict()))


def test_cross_dataset_defects_persist(dataset_a_dir, dataset_b_dir):
    """两次观测相隔约 4 个月，三个已知坏点簇必须都复现。"""
    from src.calib.background import background_stats
    from src.calib.hotpixel import build_from_sequence
    from src.dataio.fits_loader import FrameSequence

    reports = []
    for d in (dataset_a_dir, dataset_b_dir):
        seq = FrameSequence.from_directory(d)
        frames = list(range(min(20, len(seq))))
        hpm = build_from_sequence(seq, frames)
        reports.append(
            build_report(
                seq.dataset_id,
                hpm,
                background_stats(seq.image(frames[-1])),
                epoch_isot=str(seq.headers[0].date_obs.isot),
            )
        )

    res = compare_epochs(*reports)
    assert res.n_common >= 3
    known = [(244.0, 3174.0), (312.0, 3206.0), (327.0, 3210.0)]
    found = np.array([c["xy_b"] for c in res.common], dtype=float)
    for kx, ky in known:
        assert np.min(np.hypot(found[:, 0] - kx, found[:, 1] - ky)) < 4.0
    assert res.persistence > 0.5
```

- [ ] **Step 3: 实现**

`src/analysis/sensor_health.py`：

```python
"""传感器健康报告与跨历元坏点对比——派生数据集 4。

意义：同一批热像素簇在相隔约 4 个月的两次观测中位置完全一致，这既
  (a) 证明它们是探测器固有缺陷而非天体，
  (b) 反过来给出了本项目热像素识别的独立验证——不依赖任何真值，
  (c) 构成一份对观测方有实用价值的设备状态副产品。

这条证据链是"实用性"得分点，也是回答"你怎么知道那三个亮点不是暗弱目标"
这类质疑的直接答案。
"""
from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass, field

import numpy as np
from scipy.spatial import cKDTree


@dataclass
class SensorReport:
    dataset_id: str
    epoch_isot: str
    n_clusters: int
    clusters: list[dict] = field(default_factory=list)
    background_median: float | None = None
    background_rms: float | None = None
    n_saturated: int = 0
    fwhm_median_px: float | None = None

    def to_dict(self) -> dict:
        return {
            "dataset_id": self.dataset_id,
            "epoch_utc": self.epoch_isot,
            "n_hot_clusters": int(self.n_clusters),
            "clusters": self.clusters,
            "background_median_adu": self.background_median,
            "background_rms_adu": self.background_rms,
            "n_saturated_px": int(self.n_saturated),
            "fwhm_median_px": self.fwhm_median_px,
        }

    @property
    def xy(self) -> np.ndarray:
        if not self.clusters:
            return np.zeros((0, 2), dtype=np.float64)
        return np.array([[c["cx"], c["cy"]] for c in self.clusters], dtype=np.float64)


def build_report(
    dataset_id: str,
    hotpixel_map,
    bkg_stats: dict,
    *,
    epoch_isot: str,
    fwhm_median_px: float | None = None,
) -> SensorReport:
    clusters = [
        {
            "cx": float(c.cx),
            "cy": float(c.cy),
            "npix": int(c.npix),
            "median_value": float(c.median_value),
            "flat_ratio": float(c.flat_ratio),
        }
        for c in hotpixel_map.clusters
    ]
    return SensorReport(
        dataset_id=dataset_id,
        epoch_isot=str(epoch_isot),
        n_clusters=len(clusters),
        clusters=clusters,
        background_median=float(bkg_stats["median"]),
        background_rms=float(bkg_stats["std"]),
        n_saturated=int(bkg_stats["n_saturated"]),
        fwhm_median_px=None if fwhm_median_px is None else float(fwhm_median_px),
    )


@dataclass
class CrossEpochResult:
    epochs: list[str]
    match_radius_px: float
    common: list[dict] = field(default_factory=list)
    only_in_a: list[dict] = field(default_factory=list)
    only_in_b: list[dict] = field(default_factory=list)

    @property
    def n_common(self) -> int:
        return len(self.common)

    @property
    def persistence(self) -> float:
        n_a = len(self.common) + len(self.only_in_a)
        n_b = len(self.common) + len(self.only_in_b)
        denom = min(n_a, n_b)
        return float(len(self.common) / denom) if denom else 0.0

    def to_dict(self) -> dict:
        return {
            "epochs": list(self.epochs),
            "match_radius_px": self.match_radius_px,
            "n_common": self.n_common,
            "persistence": self.persistence,
            "common": self.common,
            "only_in_a": self.only_in_a,
            "only_in_b": self.only_in_b,
        }


def compare_epochs(
    report_a: SensorReport, report_b: SensorReport, *, match_radius_px: float = 3.0
) -> CrossEpochResult:
    xy_a, xy_b = report_a.xy, report_b.xy
    res = CrossEpochResult(
        epochs=[report_a.epoch_isot, report_b.epoch_isot], match_radius_px=match_radius_px
    )
    if len(xy_a) == 0 or len(xy_b) == 0:
        res.only_in_a = list(report_a.clusters)
        res.only_in_b = list(report_b.clusters)
        return res

    dist, idx = cKDTree(xy_b).query(xy_a)
    used: set[int] = set()
    for i, (d, j) in enumerate(zip(dist, idx)):
        j = int(j)
        if d < match_radius_px and j not in used:
            used.add(j)
            res.common.append(
                {
                    "xy_a": xy_a[i].tolist(),
                    "xy_b": xy_b[j].tolist(),
                    "separation_px": float(d),
                    "npix_a": report_a.clusters[i]["npix"],
                    "npix_b": report_b.clusters[j]["npix"],
                }
            )
        else:
            res.only_in_a.append(report_a.clusters[i])
    res.only_in_b = [c for j, c in enumerate(report_b.clusters) if j not in used]
    return res
```

```python
def _fit(sigma=1.6, ok=True):
    from src.detect.psf import FWHM_PER_SIGMA, GaussianFit

    return GaussianFit(
        x=10.0,
        y=10.0,
        amplitude=900.0,
        sigma_x=sigma,
        sigma_y=sigma,
        theta_deg=0.0,
        background=6.0,
        fwhm_px=FWHM_PER_SIGMA * sigma,
        residual_ratio=0.05 if ok else 0.9,
        success=ok,
    )


def test_estimate_seeing_converts_px_to_arcsec():
    from src.analysis.sensor_health import estimate_seeing
    from src.detect.psf import FWHM_PER_SIGMA

    est = estimate_seeing([_fit(1.6) for _ in range(20)], 6.179)
    assert est.n_stars == 20
    assert est.fwhm_px == pytest.approx(1.6 * FWHM_PER_SIGMA, rel=1e-6)
    assert est.fwhm_arcsec == pytest.approx(est.fwhm_px * 6.179, rel=1e-6)


def test_estimate_seeing_ignores_failed_fits():
    from src.analysis.sensor_health import estimate_seeing
    from src.detect.psf import FWHM_PER_SIGMA

    est = estimate_seeing([_fit(2.0), _fit(99.0, ok=False), _fit(2.0)], 6.179)
    assert est.n_stars == 2
    assert est.fwhm_px == pytest.approx(2.0 * FWHM_PER_SIGMA, rel=1e-6)


def test_estimate_seeing_without_any_fit():
    from src.analysis.sensor_health import estimate_seeing

    est = estimate_seeing([], 6.179)
    assert est.fwhm_px is None and est.fwhm_arcsec is None
    assert "无" in est.note


def test_sky_brightness_matches_hand_computation():
    from src.analysis.sensor_health import sky_brightness
    from src.astrometry.photometry import ZeroPoint

    zp = ZeroPoint(15.335, 0.218, 55, "truth")
    val = sky_brightness(6.45, zero_point=zp, scale_arcsec_px=6.179, exposure_s=0.03)
    expected = 15.335 - 2.5 * np.log10(6.45 / 0.03 / 6.179 ** 2)
    assert val == pytest.approx(expected, abs=1e-6)


def test_sky_brightness_is_none_without_zero_point():
    from src.analysis.sensor_health import sky_brightness

    assert sky_brightness(6.45, zero_point=None, scale_arcsec_px=6.179, exposure_s=0.03) is None


def test_sky_brightness_is_none_for_nonpositive_background():
    from src.analysis.sensor_health import sky_brightness
    from src.astrometry.photometry import ZeroPoint

    zp = ZeroPoint(15.335, 0.218, 55, "truth")
    assert sky_brightness(0.0, zero_point=zp, scale_arcsec_px=6.179, exposure_s=0.03) is None


def test_dataset_b_seeing_is_a_few_pixels(dataset_b_dir):
    """实测 PSF sigma 在 1-2 px，FWHM 应落在 2-8 px、约 12-50 角秒。"""
    from src.analysis.sensor_health import estimate_seeing
    from src.calib.background import model_background
    from src.dataio.fits_loader import FrameSequence
    from src.detect.psf import fit_table
    from src.detect.segmentation import detect_sources_in_frame

    seq = FrameSequence.from_directory(dataset_b_dir)
    img = seq.image(30)
    model = model_background(img)
    table = detect_sources_in_frame(img, model, n_sigma=5.0, frame=30).brightest(80)
    _, fits = fit_table(model.subtract(img), table)
    est = estimate_seeing(fits, 6.179)
    assert est.n_stars >= 20
    assert 2.0 < est.fwhm_px < 8.0
    assert 12.0 < est.fwhm_arcsec < 50.0
```

```bash
bash scripts/dr.sh pytest tests/analysis/test_sensor_health.py -k "seeing or sky" -v
```

- [ ] **Step 3: 实现（追加到 `src/analysis/sensor_health.py` 末尾）**

```python
from src.detect.psf import FWHM_PER_SIGMA


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class SeeingEstimate:
    n_stars: int
    fwhm_px: float | None = None
    fwhm_arcsec: float | None = None
    note: str = ""

    def to_dict(self) -> dict:
        return {
            "n_stars": int(self.n_stars),
            "fwhm_px": self.fwhm_px,
            "fwhm_arcsec": self.fwhm_arcsec,
            "note": self.note,
        }


def estimate_seeing(fits, scale_arcsec_px: float) -> SeeingEstimate:
    """由收敛的高斯拟合给出视宁度。

    只取 success=True 的拟合；sigma 用两轴几何平均，对轻微拖长的星点更稳。
    注意本序列在跟踪目标，恒星本身被拉成短划线，所以这个 FWHM 是"探测器 +
    大气 + 拖长"的合成宽度，报告中必须这样表述，不能简单称作大气视宁度。
    """
    sig = [
        float(np.sqrt(f.sigma_x * f.sigma_y))
        for f in fits
        if f.success and np.isfinite(f.sigma_x) and np.isfinite(f.sigma_y)
    ]
    if not sig:
        return SeeingEstimate(n_stars=0, note="无收敛的 PSF 拟合，视宁度不可估")
    fwhm = float(np.median(sig)) * FWHM_PER_SIGMA
    return SeeingEstimate(
        n_stars=len(sig),
        fwhm_px=fwhm,
        fwhm_arcsec=fwhm * float(scale_arcsec_px),
        note="含跟踪拖长的合成星像宽度，非纯大气视宁度",
    )


def sky_brightness(bkg_median_adu, *, zero_point, scale_arcsec_px, exposure_s) -> float | None:
    """天光背景面亮度（mag/arcsec^2）。

    把每像素背景计数换算成每平方角秒、每秒的计数，再套用零点：
        mu = ZP - 2.5 log10( B / t / s^2 )
    零点不可用时返回 None——绝不用未定标的仪器值冒充面亮度。
    """
    if zero_point is None:
        return None
    b = float(bkg_median_adu)
    t = float(exposure_s)
    s = float(scale_arcsec_px)
    if not (b > 0.0 and t > 0.0 and s > 0.0):
        return None
    return float(zero_point.value - 2.5 * np.log10(b / t / s**2))
```

## Task 24: Source Extractor 交叉匹配诊断

外部独立实现的交叉核对，是回答"你的探测数会不会是算法自身的产物"这类质疑的
最直接证据。SExtractor 不可用时必须优雅跳过，绝不阻塞主流水线。

**Files:**
- Create: `src/validate/sextractor.py`
- Test: `tests/validate/test_sextractor.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `src.detect.segmentation.SourceTable`
- Produces:
  - `SExtractorUnavailable(RuntimeError)`
  - `available() -> str | None`：返回可用的可执行名（`"source-extractor"` 或 `"sex"`），都不可用返回 `None`
  - `run_sextractor(image, *, thresh=5.0, min_area=5, workdir=None) -> SourceTable`：写临时 FITS + 参数文件，调用外部程序，解析 catalog；不可用时抛 `SExtractorUnavailable`
  - `CrossMatchReport` dataclass：`n_ours`、`n_theirs`、`n_matched`、`recall`、`precision`、`median_offset_px`、`match_radius_px`、`available: bool`、`note: str`、`to_dict()`
  - `cross_match(ours, theirs, *, match_radius_px=3.0) -> CrossMatchReport`
  - `diagnose(image, table, *, match_radius_px=3.0, **kw) -> CrossMatchReport`：外部程序缺失时返回 `available=False` 的报告而不抛异常

- [ ] **Step 1: 写失败测试**

`tests/validate/test_sextractor.py`：

```python
from __future__ import annotations

import numpy as np
import pytest

from src.detect.segmentation import SourceTable
from src.validate.sextractor import (
    CrossMatchReport,
    available,
    cross_match,
    diagnose,
)


def table(xy, frame=0):
    xy = np.asarray(xy, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1, 2)
    n = len(xy)
    return SourceTable(
        frame=frame,
        x=xy[:, 0].copy(),
        y=xy[:, 1].copy(),
        flux=np.full(n, 1000.0),
        peak=np.full(n, 200.0),
        elongation=np.full(n, 1.1),
        npix=np.full(n, 9, dtype=np.int64),
    )


def test_cross_match_perfect_agreement():
    xy = [(10.0, 10.0), (50.0, 60.0), (200.0, 30.0)]
    rep = cross_match(table(xy), table(xy))
    assert rep.n_matched == 3
    assert rep.recall == pytest.approx(1.0)
    assert rep.precision == pytest.approx(1.0)
    assert rep.median_offset_px == pytest.approx(0.0)


def test_cross_match_partial_overlap():
    ours = table([(10.0, 10.0), (50.0, 60.0), (300.0, 300.0)])
    theirs = table([(10.2, 10.1), (50.0, 60.0), (700.0, 700.0), (800.0, 800.0)])
    rep = cross_match(ours, theirs)
    assert rep.n_matched == 2
    assert rep.recall == pytest.approx(2 / 4)       # 对方有 4 个，我们复现 2 个
    assert rep.precision == pytest.approx(2 / 3)    # 我们有 3 个，2 个被对方确认
    assert rep.median_offset_px < 1.0


def test_cross_match_respects_radius():
    ours = table([(10.0, 10.0)])
    theirs = table([(15.0, 10.0)])
    assert cross_match(ours, theirs, match_radius_px=3.0).n_matched == 0
    assert cross_match(ours, theirs, match_radius_px=8.0).n_matched == 1


def test_cross_match_never_double_counts():
    """一个对方源不得被我们的两个源同时认领。"""
    ours = table([(10.0, 10.0), (10.5, 10.0)])
    theirs = table([(10.2, 10.0)])
    rep = cross_match(ours, theirs, match_radius_px=3.0)
    assert rep.n_matched == 1


def test_cross_match_handles_empty_sides():
    rep = cross_match(table([]), table([(1.0, 1.0)]))
    assert rep.n_matched == 0 and rep.recall == pytest.approx(0.0)
    assert np.isnan(rep.precision) or rep.precision == 0.0


def test_to_dict_is_json_ready():
    import json

    json.loads(json.dumps(cross_match(table([(1.0, 1.0)]), table([(1.0, 1.0)])).to_dict()))


def test_available_returns_name_or_none():
    got = available()
    assert got is None or got in {"source-extractor", "sex", "sextractor"}


def test_diagnose_degrades_when_binary_missing():
    """镜像未装 SExtractor 时必须返回 available=False，而不是抛异常。"""
    img = np.random.default_rng(3).normal(6.0, 3.8, size=(128, 128))
    rep = diagnose(img, table([(64.0, 64.0)]))
    assert isinstance(rep, CrossMatchReport)
    if not rep.available:
        assert "Source Extractor" in rep.note or "SExtractor" in rep.note


@pytest.mark.skipif(available() is None, reason="镜像未安装 Source Extractor")
def test_dataset_b_agreement_is_high(dataset_b_dir):
    from src.calib.background import model_background
    from src.dataio.fits_loader import FrameSequence
    from src.detect.segmentation import detect_sources_in_frame

    seq = FrameSequence.from_directory(dataset_b_dir)
    img = seq.image(30)
    ours = detect_sources_in_frame(img, model_background(img), n_sigma=5.0, frame=30)
    rep = diagnose(img, ours, thresh=5.0)
    assert rep.available
    assert rep.precision > 0.7
    assert rep.median_offset_px < 2.0
```

```bash
bash scripts/dr.sh pytest tests/validate/test_sextractor.py -v
```

- [ ] **Step 3: 实现**

`src/validate/sextractor.py`：

```python
"""Source Extractor 交叉匹配诊断——独立实现的外部核对。

为什么需要：photutils 与 SExtractor 用的是同一套分割思想但不同实现。两者在
同一帧上给出一致的源位置（中位偏差 < 2 px）时，"我们的探测数是算法臆造"这一
质疑就被直接排除。

指标定义（务必在报告中写清，否则容易被追问）：
    precision = 我们的源被对方确认的比例  —— 衡量我们有多少虚假检出
    recall    = 对方的源被我们复现的比例  —— 衡量我们漏了多少
两个方向都报，只报一个是不诚实的。

外部程序缺失是常态（默认 CPU 镜像不含它）。此时 diagnose() 返回
available=False 的报告，主流水线照常继续，报告中标注"未做外部交叉核对"。
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import shutil
import subprocess
import tempfile
from dataclasses import dataclass
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
from scipy.spatial import cKDTree

CANDIDATE_BINARIES = ("source-extractor", "sex", "sextractor")

PARAM_FILE = "X_IMAGE\nY_IMAGE\nFLUX_AUTO\nFLUX_MAX\nELONGATION\nISOAREA_IMAGE\n"


class SExtractorUnavailable(RuntimeError):
    pass


def available() -> str | None:
    for name in CANDIDATE_BINARIES:
        if shutil.which(name):
            return name
    return None


def run_sextractor(image, *, thresh: float = 5.0, min_area: int = 5, workdir=None):
    from src.detect.segmentation import SourceTable

    binary = available()
    if binary is None:
        raise SExtractorUnavailable(
            "未找到 Source Extractor 可执行文件（尝试过 " + ", ".join(CANDIDATE_BINARIES) + "）"
        )

    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(dir=workdir) as tmp:
        d = Path(tmp)
        from astropy.io import fits as afits

        afits.writeto(d / "in.fits", np.asarray(image, dtype=np.float32), overwrite=True)
        (d / "sex.param").write_text(PARAM_FILE, encoding="ascii")
        (d / "sex.conf").write_text(
            "\n".join(
                [
                    "CATALOG_NAME     out.cat",
                    "CATALOG_TYPE     ASCII",
                    "PARAMETERS_NAME  sex.param",
                    "DETECT_TYPE      CCD",
                    f"DETECT_MINAREA   {int(min_area)}",
                    f"DETECT_THRESH    {float(thresh)}",
                    f"ANALYSIS_THRESH  {float(thresh)}",
                    "FILTER           N",
                    "BACK_SIZE        128",
                    "BACK_FILTERSIZE  3",
                    "PHOT_APERTURES   10",
                    "GAIN             1.0",
                    "",
                ]
            ),
            encoding="ascii",
        )
        proc = subprocess.run(
            [binary, "in.fits", "-c", "sex.conf"],
            cwd=d,
            capture_output=True,
            text=True,
            timeout=300,
        )
        cat = d / "out.cat"
        if proc.returncode != 0 or not cat.exists():
            raise SExtractorUnavailable(
                f"Source Extractor 运行失败（返回码 {proc.returncode}）：{proc.stderr[-400:]}"
            )
        raw = np.loadtxt(cat, ndmin=2)

    if raw.size == 0:
        raw = np.zeros((0, 6))
    return SourceTable(
        frame=-1,
        x=np.ascontiguousarray(raw[:, 0] - 1.0, dtype=np.float64),  # SExtractor 是 1-based
        y=np.ascontiguousarray(raw[:, 1] - 1.0, dtype=np.float64),
        flux=np.ascontiguousarray(raw[:, 2], dtype=np.float64),
        peak=np.ascontiguousarray(raw[:, 3], dtype=np.float64),
        elongation=np.ascontiguousarray(raw[:, 4], dtype=np.float64),
        npix=np.ascontiguousarray(raw[:, 5], dtype=np.int64),
    )


@dataclass
class CrossMatchReport:
    n_ours: int
    n_theirs: int
    n_matched: int
    match_radius_px: float
    recall: float
    precision: float
    median_offset_px: float
    available: bool = True
    note: str = ""

    def to_dict(self) -> dict:
        return {
            "n_ours": int(self.n_ours),
            "n_sextractor": int(self.n_theirs),
            "n_matched": int(self.n_matched),
            "match_radius_px": self.match_radius_px,
            "recall": None if not np.isfinite(self.recall) else self.recall,
            "precision": None if not np.isfinite(self.precision) else self.precision,
            "median_offset_px": None
            if not np.isfinite(self.median_offset_px)
            else self.median_offset_px,
            "available": bool(self.available),
            "note": self.note,
            "definition": "precision=我们被对方确认的比例；recall=对方被我们复现的比例",
        }


def cross_match(ours, theirs, *, match_radius_px: float = 3.0) -> CrossMatchReport:
    a, b = ours.xy, theirs.xy
    if len(a) == 0 or len(b) == 0:
        return CrossMatchReport(
            n_ours=len(a),
            n_theirs=len(b),
            n_matched=0,
            match_radius_px=match_radius_px,
            recall=0.0,
            precision=0.0,
            median_offset_px=float("nan"),
            note="一侧无源，无法交叉匹配",
        )

    dist, idx = cKDTree(b).query(a)
    used: set[int] = set()
    offsets: list[float] = []
    for d, j in zip(dist, idx):
        j = int(j)
        if d < match_radius_px and j not in used:
            used.add(j)
            offsets.append(float(d))
    n = len(offsets)
    return CrossMatchReport(
        n_ours=len(a),
        n_theirs=len(b),
        n_matched=n,
        match_radius_px=match_radius_px,
        recall=float(n / len(b)),
        precision=float(n / len(a)),
        median_offset_px=float(np.median(offsets)) if offsets else float("nan"),
        note="photutils 与 Source Extractor 独立实现的交叉核对",
    )


def diagnose(image, table, *, match_radius_px: float = 3.0, **kwargs) -> CrossMatchReport:
    try:
        theirs = run_sextractor(image, **kwargs)
    except SExtractorUnavailable as exc:
        return CrossMatchReport(
            n_ours=len(table),
            n_theirs=0,
            n_matched=0,
            match_radius_px=match_radius_px,
            recall=float("nan"),
            precision=float("nan"),
            median_offset_px=float("nan"),
            available=False,
            note=f"未做 Source Extractor 外部交叉核对：{exc}",
        )
    return cross_match(table, theirs, match_radius_px=match_radius_px)
```

**Files:**
- Create: `src/dataio/catalog_writer.py`
- Test: `tests/dataio/test_catalog_writer.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `src.detect.segmentation.SourceTable`、`src.target.trajectory.Trajectory`、`src.validate.truth.load_truth`
- Produces:
  - `write_source_table(table, path, *, extra=None) -> Path`：CSV，表头 `frame,x,y,flux,peak,elongation,npix`
  - `write_json(payload, path) -> Path`：`ensure_ascii=False`、`indent=2`、自动建父目录
  - `write_trajectory_csv(trajectory, path) -> Path`
  - `write_trajectory_dat(trajectory, path) -> Path`：赛方 9 列格式 `年 月 日 时 分 秒 赤经 赤纬 星等`，星等缺失写 `0.000000`
  - `read_dat_roundtrip(path) -> np.ndarray`：读回 `(N, 9)` 数组，供往返一致性测试
  - `CATALOG_HEADER: tuple[str, ...]`、`DAT_FMT: str`

```python
from __future__ import annotations

import json

import numpy as np
import pytest

from src.dataio.catalog_writer import (
    CATALOG_HEADER,
    read_dat_roundtrip,
    write_json,
    write_source_table,
    write_trajectory_csv,

)
from src.detect.segmentation import SourceTable
from src.target.trajectory import Trajectory, TrajectoryPoint


def sample_table():

        frame=30,
        x=np.array([10.5, 2000.25], dtype=np.float64),
        y=np.array([20.5, 1500.75], dtype=np.float64),
        flux=np.array([1234.5, 987.0], dtype=np.float64),
        peak=np.array([300.0, 210.0], dtype=np.float64),
        elongation=np.array([1.05, 1.42], dtype=np.float64),
        npix=np.array([9, 15], dtype=np.int64),
    )


def sample_trajectory(mag=8.42):
    pts = [
        TrajectoryPoint(
            frame=17 + i,
            time_isot=f"2026-07-21T17:26:{44.267 + i:09.6f}",
            x_det=2271.6 + 1.81 * i,
            y_det=1958.8 + 0.33 * i,
            x_sky=2271.6 + 125.73 * i,
            y_sky=1958.8 - 5.0 * i,
            ra_deg=209.123456 + 0.01 * i,
            dec_deg=12.654321 - 0.005 * i,
            rate_arcsec_s=770.0,
            pa_deg=128.08,
            flux=1000.0,
            mag=mag,
        )
        for i in range(3)
    ]
    trj = Trajectory(points=pts, source="test")
    trj._scale = 6.179
    return trj


def test_write_source_table_header_and_rows(tmp_path):
    p = write_source_table(sample_table(), tmp_path / "sub" / "cat.csv")
    lines = p.read_text(encoding="utf-8").strip().splitlines()
    assert lines[0].split(",") == list(CATALOG_HEADER)
    assert len(lines) == 3
    assert lines[1].startswith("30,")


def test_write_source_table_creates_parents(tmp_path):
    p = write_source_table(sample_table(), tmp_path / "a" / "b" / "c.csv")
    assert p.exists()

    empty = SourceTable(
        frame=0,
        x=np.zeros(0), y=np.zeros(0), flux=np.zeros(0), peak=np.zeros(0),
        elongation=np.zeros(0), npix=np.zeros(0, dtype=np.int64),
    )
    p = write_source_table(empty, tmp_path / "e.csv")
    assert p.read_text(encoding="utf-8").strip() == ",".join(CATALOG_HEADER)


def test_write_json_keeps_chinese(tmp_path):
    p = write_json({"说明": "真值仅用于验证"}, tmp_path / "r.json")
    assert "真值" in p.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    assert json.loads(p.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))["说明"] == "真值仅用于验证"


def test_write_trajectory_csv(tmp_path):
    p = write_trajectory_csv(sample_trajectory(), tmp_path / "trj.csv")
    lines = p.read_text(encoding="utf-8").strip().splitlines()

def test_dat_has_nine_columns_and_roundtrips(tmp_path):
    p = write_trajectory_dat(sample_trajectory(), tmp_path / "out.DAT")
    raw = read_dat_roundtrip(p)
    assert raw.shape == (3, 9)
    assert raw[0, 0] == 2026 and raw[0, 1] == 7 and raw[0, 2] == 21
    assert raw[0, 3] == 17 and raw[0, 4] == 26
    assert raw[0, 6] == pytest.approx(209.123456, abs=1e-6)
    assert raw[0, 7] == pytest.approx(12.654321, abs=1e-6)
    assert raw[0, 8] == pytest.approx(8.42, abs=1e-6)


def test_dat_writes_zero_for_missing_magnitude(tmp_path):
    """赛方约定 mag <= 0 表示缺失，我们输出时遵循同一约定。"""
    p = write_trajectory_dat(sample_trajectory(mag=None), tmp_path / "nomag.DAT")
    assert read_dat_roundtrip(p)[0, 8] == pytest.approx(0.0)


def test_dat_is_parsable_by_our_own_truth_loader(tmp_path):
    """自产 .DAT 必须能被 load_truth 读回——这是格式正确性的最强检验。"""
    from src.validate.truth import load_truth

    p = write_trajectory_dat(sample_trajectory(), tmp_path / "self.DAT")
    t = load_truth(p)
    assert len(t) == 3
    assert t.ra_deg[0] == pytest.approx(209.123456, abs=1e-6)
    assert str(t.time[0].isot).startswith("2026-07-21T17:26:44")


def test_dat_roundtrip_matches_reference_column_count(dataset_b_dir):
    """与赛方真值文件列数一致。"""
    from src.dataio.fits_loader import FrameSequence

    seq = FrameSequence.from_directory(dataset_b_dir)
    assert read_dat_roundtrip(seq.truth_path).shape[1] == 9
```

- [ ] **Step 3: 实现**

`src/dataio/catalog_writer.py`：

```python
"""落盘：源表 CSV、报告 JSON、目标轨迹 CSV 与赛方 .DAT。

.DAT 输出的意义：赛方真值就是这个格式（年 月 日 时 分 秒 赤经 赤纬 星等，
UTC / J2000）。我们用同格式输出，评委可以把两个文件直接并排比对，无需任何
转换。这是"派生数据集 1（目标轨迹）"的交付形态，也是可比性最强的一种表达。

约定与赛方对齐：星等缺失写 0.000000（赛方用 mag <= 0 表示缺失）。
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np

CATALOG_HEADER = ("frame", "x", "y", "flux", "peak", "elongation", "npix")
TRAJECTORY_HEADER = (
    "frame",
    "time_utc",
    "x_det",
    "y_det",
    "x_sky",
    "y_sky",
    "ra_deg",
    "dec_deg",
    "rate_arcsec_s",
    "pa_deg",
    "flux",
    "mag",
)
DAT_FMT = "{y:4d} {mo:2d} {d:2d} {h:2d} {mi:2d} {s:9.6f} {ra:12.6f} {dec:12.6f} {mag:9.6f}"


def _prepare(path) -> Path:
    p = Path(path)
    p.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    return p


def write_source_table(table, path, *, extra: dict | None = None) -> Path:
    p = _prepare(path)
    rows = [",".join(CATALOG_HEADER)]
    for i in range(len(table)):
        rows.append(
            f"{int(table.frame)},{table.x[i]:.4f},{table.y[i]:.4f},"
            f"{table.flux[i]:.4f},{table.peak[i]:.4f},"
            f"{table.elongation[i]:.4f},{int(table.npix[i])}"
        )
    p.write_text("\n".join(rows) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
    if extra:
        write_json(extra, p.with_suffix(".meta.json"))
    return p


def write_json(payload, path) -> Path:
    p = _prepare(path)
    p.write_text(json.dumps(payload, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
    return p


def write_trajectory_csv(trajectory, path) -> Path:
    p = _prepare(path)
    rows = [",".join(TRAJECTORY_HEADER)]
    for r in trajectory.to_records():
        rows.append(
            ",".join(
                "" if r[k] is None else (str(r[k]) if isinstance(r[k], (int, str)) else f"{r[k]:.6f}")
                for k in TRAJECTORY_HEADER
            )
        )
    p.write_text("\n".join(rows) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
    return p


def write_trajectory_dat(trajectory, path) -> Path:
    from astropy.time import Time

    p = _prepare(path)
    lines = []
    for pt in trajectory.points:
        t = Time(pt.time_isot, scale="utc")
        dt = t.datetime
        sec = dt.second + dt.microsecond * 1e-6
        mag = pt.mag if (pt.mag is not None and np.isfinite(pt.mag)) else 0.0
        lines.append(
            DAT_FMT.format(
                y=dt.year,
                mo=dt.month,
                d=dt.day,
                h=dt.hour,
                mi=dt.minute,
                s=sec,
                ra=float(pt.ra_deg),
                dec=float(pt.dec_deg),
                mag=float(mag),
            )
        )
    p.write_text("\n".join(lines) + "\n", encoding="ascii")
    return p


def read_dat_roundtrip(path) -> np.ndarray:
    return np.loadtxt(Path(path), ndmin=2)
```

## Task 26: 可视化成果图

全部图表是 PPT 素材的唯一来源。中文标签必须能真实渲染——容器缺中文字体会
默默画成方框，那样的图放进 PPT 会直接掉分。因此本任务第一步就是把字体装进
镜像并写一个断言字体可用的测试。

**Files:**
- Modify: `docker/Dockerfile.cpu`（追加中文字体）
- Modify: `src/config/default.yaml:363-365`（`viz.font_candidates` 去掉无汉字字形的回退项）
- Create: `src/viz/figures.py`
- Test: `tests/viz/test_figures.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `RepeatabilityCurve`、`Segment`、`StackResult`、`Trajectory`、`LightCurve`、`HotPixelMap`、`SourceTable`、`TruthReport`
- Produces（每个函数都返回落盘的 `Path`，均接受 `path` 关键字）：
  - `setup_matplotlib() -> str`：设置 Agg 后端与中文字体，返回实际选中的字体名
  - `FontUnavailable(RuntimeError)`
  - `plot_threshold_curve(curve, path, *, title=None) -> Path`：阈值-检出数与阈值-复现率双 y 轴
  - `plot_segments(headers, segments, path) -> Path`：ΔAZ 序列 + 分段着色
  - `plot_detections(image, table, path, *, target_xy=None, hot_clusters=None) -> Path`
  - `plot_trajectory(trajectory, path) -> Path`：探测器系与天球系双面板
  - `plot_stack(result, path) -> Path`：叠加图 + 覆盖度图并排
  - `plot_lightcurve(lc, path, *, period=None) -> Path`
  - `plot_hotpixels(hotpixel_map, path, *, shape) -> Path`
  - `plot_truth_comparison(report, path) -> Path`：比例尺逐帧散点 + 星等相关散点
  - `plot_magnitude_histogram(mags, path, *, limiting=None) -> Path`

- [ ] **Step 1: 把中文字体装进镜像，并修正字体候选列表**

```bash
print([n for n in names if 'CJK' in n or 'WenQuanYi' in n][:5])
"
```

```yaml
viz:

```

原来的列表末尾有 `DejaVu Sans`——它没有汉字字形，一旦被选中，`setup_matplotlib`
会"成功"返回而图上全是方框，正是这个任务要防的失败模式。回退候选必须也是中文字体。

```python
from __future__ import annotations

import numpy as np
import pytest

from src.viz.figures import (
    plot_detections,
    plot_hotpixels,
    plot_lightcurve,
    plot_magnitude_histogram,
    plot_segments,
    plot_stack,
    plot_threshold_curve,
    plot_trajectory,
    plot_truth_comparison,
    setup_matplotlib,
)


def test_chinese_font_is_available():
    """中文字体缺失时必须显式失败，绝不静默画成方框。"""
    name = setup_matplotlib()
    assert isinstance(name, str) and name
    from matplotlib import font_manager as fm

    assert name in {f.name for f in fm.fontManager.ttflist}


def test_threshold_curve(tmp_path):
    from src.validate.repeatability import RepeatabilityCurve, RepeatabilityPoint

    pts = [
        RepeatabilityPoint(n_sigma=s, n_detected_mean=n, n_reproducible=r,
                           reproducibility=r / n)
        for s, n, r in [(2.0, 2284, 180), (3.0, 169, 140), (4.0, 116, 104), (5.0, 95, 86)]
    ]
    curve = RepeatabilityCurve(points=pts, frames=[16, 20, 24, 28, 32, 36], match_radius_px=3.0)
    p = plot_threshold_curve(curve, tmp_path / "curve.png", title="数据集 B 阈值-复现率")
    assert p.exists() and p.stat().st_size > 5000


def test_segments(tmp_path):
    from src.calib.segment import Segment

    class H:
        def __init__(self, i, az):
            self.index, self.azimuth_deg = i, az

    headers = [H(i, 100.0 + (0.222 * i if 16 <= i <= 70 else 0.0)) for i in range(80)]
    segs = [
        Segment(label="slew", start=0, end=15, daz_mean=0.0, daz_std=0.0),
        Segment(label="tracking", start=16, end=70, daz_mean=0.222, daz_std=0.003),
        Segment(label="slew", start=71, end=79, daz_mean=0.0, daz_std=0.0),
    ]
    assert plot_segments(headers, segs, tmp_path / "seg.png").exists()


def test_detections(tmp_path):
    from src.calib.hotpixel import HotCluster
    from src.detect.segmentation import SourceTable

    rng = np.random.default_rng(4)
    img = rng.normal(6.45, 3.8, size=(256, 256))
    n = 30
    t = SourceTable(
        frame=30,
        x=rng.uniform(10, 246, n), y=rng.uniform(10, 246, n),
        flux=rng.uniform(200, 5000, n), peak=rng.uniform(50, 900, n),
        elongation=np.full(n, 1.2), npix=np.full(n, 9, dtype=np.int64),
    )
    hot = [HotCluster(cx=100.0, cy=100.0, npix=4, median_value=1200.0, flat_ratio=0.01)]
    p = plot_detections(img, t, tmp_path / "det.png", target_xy=(128.0, 128.0), hot_clusters=hot)
    assert p.exists()


def test_trajectory(tmp_path):
    from src.target.trajectory import Trajectory, TrajectoryPoint

    pts = [
        TrajectoryPoint(
            frame=17 + i, time_isot=f"2026-07-21T17:26:{44.0 + i:09.6f}",
            x_det=2271.6 + 1.81 * i, y_det=1958.8 + 0.33 * i,
            x_sky=2271.6 + 125.73 * i, y_sky=1958.8 - 5.0 * i,
            ra_deg=209.0 + 0.01 * i, dec_deg=12.0 - 0.005 * i,
            rate_arcsec_s=770.0, pa_deg=128.08, flux=1000.0, mag=8.4,
        )
        for i in range(20)
    ]
    trj = Trajectory(points=pts, source="test")
    trj._scale = 6.179
    assert plot_trajectory(trj, tmp_path / "trj.png").exists()


def test_stack(tmp_path):
    from src.stack.coadd import StackResult

    rng = np.random.default_rng(7)
    img = rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, size=(128, 128))
    cov = np.zeros((128, 128), dtype=np.int32)
    cov[10:120, 10:120] = 12
    res = StackResult(image=img, coverage=cov, frames=list(range(16, 28)),
                      reference=16, min_coverage=4)
    assert plot_stack(res, tmp_path / "stack.png").exists()


def test_lightcurve_with_and_without_period(tmp_path):
    from src.analysis.lightcurve import LightCurve

    t = np.arange(200) * 1.008
    m = 8.4 + 0.3 * np.sin(2 * np.pi * t / 20.0)
    lc = LightCurve(time_s=t, mag=m, flux=10.0 ** (-0.4 * m), calibrated=True)
    assert plot_lightcurve(lc, tmp_path / "lc1.png").exists()
    assert plot_lightcurve(lc, tmp_path / "lc2.png", period=20.0).exists()


def test_hotpixels(tmp_path):
    from src.calib.hotpixel import HotCluster, HotPixelMap

    clusters = [
        HotCluster(cx=x, cy=y, npix=4, median_value=1200.0, flat_ratio=0.01)
        for x, y in [(244, 3174), (312, 3206), (327, 3210)]
    ]
    hpm = HotPixelMap(mask=np.zeros((4096, 4096), dtype=bool), clusters=clusters)
    assert plot_hotpixels(hpm, tmp_path / "hot.png", shape=(4096, 4096)).exists()


def test_truth_comparison(tmp_path):
    from src.validate.truth import TruthReport

    rep = TruthReport(
        dataset_id="B", available=True, n_matched=54, dt_max_s=0.0,
        plate_scale_arcsec_px=6.179, plate_scale_std=0.013,
        angular_step_arcsec=776.9, pixel_step_px=125.73,
        mag_correlation=0.949, zero_point=15.335, zero_point_std=0.218,
        note="真值仅用于验证与定标，未参与目标判决",
    )
    assert plot_truth_comparison(rep, tmp_path / "truth.png").exists()


def test_truth_comparison_handles_unavailable(tmp_path):
    """数据集 A 无真值时应画一张写明"无真值"的占位图，而不是崩溃。"""
    from src.validate.truth import TruthReport

    rep = TruthReport(dataset_id="A", available=False, note="数据集无 .DAT，无真值可比对")
    assert plot_truth_comparison(rep, tmp_path / "none.png").exists()


def test_magnitude_histogram(tmp_path):
    mags = np.random.default_rng(9).normal(11.0, 1.5, 300)
    assert plot_magnitude_histogram(mags, tmp_path / "mag.png", limiting=13.8).exists()


def test_all_figures_use_no_gui_backend():
    import matplotlib

    setup_matplotlib()
    assert matplotlib.get_backend().lower() == "agg"
```
