- [ ] **Step 4: 实现**

`src/viz/figures.py`：

```python
"""全部静态成果图。PPT 素材的唯一来源。

三条硬规则：
  1. 后端固定 Agg——容器无显示设备，任何交互后端都会失败；
  2. 中文字体必须真实存在，否则抛 FontUnavailable。matplotlib 缺字时会静默
     画成方框，那种图放进答辩 PPT 是直接的形象损失，宁可构建时失败；
  3. 每个函数只画一张图、只落一个文件、返回 Path，便于 CLI 汇总清单。
"""
from __future__ import annotations

from pathlib import Path

import matplotlib

matplotlib.use("Agg", force=True)

import matplotlib.pyplot as plt  # noqa: E402
import numpy as np  # noqa: E402
from matplotlib import font_manager as fm  # noqa: E402

FONT_CANDIDATES = ("Noto Sans CJK SC", "Noto Sans CJK JP", "WenQuanYi Zen Hei", "SimHei")
DPI = 150


class FontUnavailable(RuntimeError):
    pass


def setup_matplotlib(candidates=FONT_CANDIDATES) -> str:
    installed = {f.name for f in fm.fontManager.ttflist}
    for name in candidates:
        if name in installed:
            plt.rcParams["font.family"] = [name]
            plt.rcParams["axes.unicode_minus"] = False
            plt.rcParams["figure.dpi"] = DPI
            plt.rcParams["savefig.bbox"] = "tight"
            return name
    raise FontUnavailable(
        "未找到可用中文字体（尝试过 "
        + ", ".join(candidates)
        + "）。请在镜像中安装 fonts-noto-cjk 或 fonts-wqy-zenhei。"
    )


def _save(fig, path) -> Path:
    p = Path(path)
    p.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    fig.savefig(p, dpi=DPI)
    plt.close(fig)
    return p


def plot_threshold_curve(curve, path, *, title: str | None = None) -> Path:
    setup_matplotlib()
    sig = [p.n_sigma for p in curve.points]
    det = [p.n_detected_mean for p in curve.points]
    rep = [p.reproducibility for p in curve.points]

    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(7.5, 4.8))
    ax.plot(sig, det, "o-", color="#1f77b4", label="单帧检出数（均值）")
    ax.set_yscale("log")
    ax.set_xlabel("探测阈值（背景 RMS 的倍数 σ）")
    ax.set_ylabel("单帧检出数（对数）", color="#1f77b4")
    ax.tick_params(axis="y", labelcolor="#1f77b4")
    ax.grid(alpha=0.3)

    ax2 = ax.twinx()
    ax2.plot(sig, rep, "s--", color="#d62728", label="跨帧复现率")
    ax2.axhline(0.85, color="gray", ls=":", lw=1.2)
    ax2.set_ylabel("跨帧复现率", color="#d62728")
    ax2.set_ylim(0.0, 1.05)
    ax2.tick_params(axis="y", labelcolor="#d62728")

    lines = ax.get_lines() + ax2.get_lines()[:1]
    ax.legend(lines, [l.get_label() for l in lines], loc="center right")
    ax.set_title(title or "探测阈值与跨帧复现率：低阈值的高检出数不可复现")
    return _save(fig, path)


def plot_segments(headers, segments, path) -> Path:
    setup_matplotlib()
    az = np.array([h.azimuth_deg for h in headers], dtype=np.float64)
    daz = np.diff(az)
    idx = np.arange(1, len(az))
    colors = {"tracking": "#2ca02c", "slew": "#ff7f0e"}
    labels = {"tracking": "稳定跟踪段", "slew": "机架调整段"}

    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(9.0, 4.2))
    ax.plot(idx, daz, "-", color="#444444", lw=1.0, zorder=3)
    seen = set()
    for s in segments:
        ax.axvspan(
            s.start,
            s.end,
            color=colors.get(s.label, "#cccccc"),
            alpha=0.25,
            label=None if s.label in seen else labels.get(s.label, s.label),
        )
        seen.add(s.label)
        ax.annotate(
            f"{s.label}\nf{s.start}-f{s.end}",
            xy=((s.start + s.end) / 2, ax.get_ylim()[1]),
            ha="center",
            va="top",
            fontsize=8,
        )
    ax.set_xlabel("帧号")
    ax.set_ylabel("方位角逐帧增量 ΔAZ（度/帧）")
    ax.set_title("仅凭 FITS 头部识别机架状态：搜索 → 稳定跟踪 → 收尾")
    ax.legend(loc="upper right")
    ax.grid(alpha=0.3)
    return _save(fig, path)


def plot_detections(image, table, path, *, target_xy=None, hot_clusters=None) -> Path:
    setup_matplotlib()
    img = np.asarray(image, dtype=np.float64)
    lo, hi = np.percentile(img, [5.0, 99.5])

    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(7.2, 7.2))
    ax.imshow(img, origin="lower", cmap="gray", vmin=lo, vmax=hi)
    ax.scatter(
        table.x, table.y, s=90, facecolors="none", edgecolors="#00d0ff", lw=0.9,
        label=f"检出星点 {len(table)} 个",
    )
    if hot_clusters:
        ax.scatter(
            [c.cx for c in hot_clusters], [c.cy for c in hot_clusters],
            marker="x", s=70, c="#ff4040", lw=1.4,
            label=f"热像素簇 {len(hot_clusters)} 个（已剔除）",
        )
    if target_xy is not None:
        ax.scatter(
            [target_xy[0]], [target_xy[1]], marker="s", s=200,
            facecolors="none", edgecolors="#ffd700", lw=1.8, label="空间目标",
        )
    ax.set_xlabel("列 x（像素）")
    ax.set_ylabel("行 y（像素）")
    ax.set_title(f"第 {table.frame} 帧探测结果")
    ax.legend(loc="upper right", fontsize=8)
    return _save(fig, path)


def plot_trajectory(trajectory, path) -> Path:
    setup_matplotlib()
    r = trajectory.to_records()
    xd = [p["x_det"] for p in r]
    yd = [p["y_det"] for p in r]
    xs = [p["x_sky"] for p in r]
    ys = [p["y_sky"] for p in r]
    f = [p["frame"] for p in r]

    fig, axes = plt.subplots(1, 2, figsize=(12.0, 5.0))
    sc = axes[0].scatter(xd, yd, c=f, cmap="viridis", s=22)
    axes[0].plot(xd, yd, "-", color="#888888", lw=0.8)
    axes[0].set_title(
        f"探测器坐标系：目标近静止（总位移 {np.hypot(xd[-1] - xd[0], yd[-1] - yd[0]):.1f} px）"
    )
    axes[1].scatter(xs, ys, c=f, cmap="viridis", s=22)
    axes[1].plot(xs, ys, "-", color="#888888", lw=0.8)
    axes[1].set_title(
        f"天球坐标系：目标快速掠过（总位移 {np.hypot(xs[-1] - xs[0], ys[-1] - ys[0]):.0f} px）"
    )
    for ax in axes:
        ax.set_xlabel("x（像素）")
        ax.set_ylabel("y（像素）")
        ax.grid(alpha=0.3)
        ax.set_aspect("equal", adjustable="datalim")
    fig.colorbar(sc, ax=axes, label="帧号", fraction=0.03)
    fig.suptitle(
        f"双坐标系轨迹对照：{len(r)} 帧，平均角速度 {trajectory.mean_rate_arcsec_s:.1f} ″/s"
    )
    return _save(fig, path)


def plot_stack(result, path) -> Path:
    setup_matplotlib()
    img = np.asarray(result.image, dtype=np.float64)
    finite = img[np.isfinite(img)]
    lo, hi = np.percentile(finite, [5.0, 99.5]) if finite.size else (0.0, 1.0)

    fig, axes = plt.subplots(1, 2, figsize=(12.0, 5.4))
    axes[0].imshow(img, origin="lower", cmap="gray", vmin=lo, vmax=hi)
    axes[0].set_title(
        f"滑动窗口叠加（f{result.frames[0]}–f{result.frames[-1]}，"
        f"深度增益 {result.gain_mag:.2f} mag）"
    )
    m = axes[1].imshow(result.coverage, origin="lower", cmap="magma")
    axes[1].set_title(
        f"覆盖度图：中位 {result.median_coverage:.0f} 帧，"
        f"可用面积 {100.0 * result.usable_mask.mean():.1f}%"
    )
    fig.colorbar(m, ax=axes[1], label="参与叠加的帧数", fraction=0.046)
    for ax in axes:
        ax.set_xlabel("x（像素）")
        ax.set_ylabel("y（像素）")
    return _save(fig, path)


def plot_lightcurve(lc, path, *, period: float | None = None) -> Path:
    setup_matplotlib()
    ncols = 2 if period else 1
    fig, axes = plt.subplots(1, ncols, figsize=(6.0 * ncols, 4.4), squeeze=False)
    ax = axes[0][0]
    ax.plot(lc.time_s, lc.mag, "o-", ms=3, lw=0.8, color="#1f77b4")
    ax.invert_yaxis()
    ax.set_xlabel("相对时间（秒）")
    ax.set_ylabel("星等（定标）" if lc.calibrated else "仪器星等（未定标）")
    ax.set_title(f"目标光变曲线：峰谷差 {lc.amplitude_mag:.3f} mag")
    ax.grid(alpha=0.3)
    if period:
        ax2 = axes[0][1]
        ax2.plot((lc.time_s % period) / period, lc.mag, "o", ms=4, color="#d62728")
        ax2.invert_yaxis()
        ax2.set_xlabel("相位")
        ax2.set_ylabel("星等")
        ax2.set_title(f"按周期 {period:.2f} s 折叠")
        ax2.grid(alpha=0.3)
    return _save(fig, path)


def plot_hotpixels(hotpixel_map, path, *, shape) -> Path:
    setup_matplotlib()
    ny, nx = shape
    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(6.8, 6.8))
    ax.scatter(
        [c.cx for c in hotpixel_map.clusters],
        [c.cy for c in hotpixel_map.clusters],
        s=[max(30.0, 12.0 * c.npix) for c in hotpixel_map.clusters],
        c="#ff4040",
        alpha=0.8,
    )
    ax.set_xlim(0, nx)
    ax.set_ylim(0, ny)
    ax.set_aspect("equal")
    ax.set_xlabel("列 x（像素）")
    ax.set_ylabel("行 y（像素）")
    ax.set_title(f"热像素簇分布：{len(hotpixel_map.clusters)} 个簇")
    ax.grid(alpha=0.3)
    return _save(fig, path)


def plot_truth_comparison(report, path) -> Path:
    setup_matplotlib()
    if not report.available:
        fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(7.0, 4.0))
        ax.axis("off")
        ax.text(0.5, 0.5, f"数据集 {report.dataset_id}：{report.note}",
                ha="center", va="center", fontsize=13)
        return _save(fig, path)

    fig, axes = plt.subplots(1, 2, figsize=(11.5, 4.6))
    axes[0].axhline(report.plate_scale_arcsec_px, color="#1f77b4", lw=2.0)
    axes[0].axhspan(
        report.plate_scale_arcsec_px - report.plate_scale_std,
        report.plate_scale_arcsec_px + report.plate_scale_std,
        color="#1f77b4", alpha=0.2,
    )
    axes[0].set_ylim(
        report.plate_scale_arcsec_px - 6.0 * report.plate_scale_std,
        report.plate_scale_arcsec_px + 6.0 * report.plate_scale_std,
    )
    axes[0].set_xticks([])
    axes[0].set_ylabel("像元比例尺（角秒/像素）")
    axes[0].set_title(
        f"比例尺 {report.plate_scale_arcsec_px:.3f} ± {report.plate_scale_std:.3f} ″/px\n"
        f"（{report.n_matched} 帧一致到 "
        f"{100.0 * report.plate_scale_std / report.plate_scale_arcsec_px:.2f}%）"
    )
    axes[0].grid(alpha=0.3)

    axes[1].axis("off")
    axes[1].text(
        0.02,
        0.92,
        "\n".join(
            [
                f"匹配帧数：{report.n_matched}",
                f"时间戳最大偏差：{report.dt_max_s:.4f} s",
                f"真值角步长：{report.angular_step_arcsec:.1f} ″/帧",
                f"实测像素步长：{report.pixel_step_px:.2f} px/帧",
                f"星等相关系数：{report.mag_correlation:.3f}",
                f"光度零点：{report.zero_point:.3f} ± {report.zero_point_std:.3f} mag",
                "",
                report.note,
            ]
        ),
        va="top",
        fontsize=11,
        family=plt.rcParams["font.family"][0],
    )
    axes[1].set_title("与赛方 .DAT 真值的定量比对")
    return _save(fig, path)


def plot_magnitude_histogram(mags, path, *, limiting: float | None = None) -> Path:
    setup_matplotlib()
    m = np.asarray(mags, dtype=np.float64)
    m = m[np.isfinite(m)]
    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(7.2, 4.4))
    ax.hist(m, bins=30, color="#1f77b4", alpha=0.85, edgecolor="white")
    if limiting is not None:
        ax.axvline(limiting, color="#d62728", ls="--", lw=1.6,
                   label=f"最暗星 {limiting:.2f} mag")
        ax.legend()
    ax.set_xlabel("定标星等")
    ax.set_ylabel("星数")
    ax.set_title(f"星等分布：{m.size} 颗")
    ax.grid(alpha=0.3, axis="y")
    return _save(fig, path)
```

## Task 27: 统一命令行入口

**Files:**
- Create: `src/cli.py`
- Modify: `src/config/default.yaml`（新增 `astrometry` 段）
- Test: `tests/test_cli.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: 前 26 个任务的全部公共接口
- Produces:
  - `build_parser() -> argparse.ArgumentParser`：子命令 `inspect`、`detect`、`register`、`target`、`stack`、`validate`、`analyze`、`figures`、`all`
  - `PipelineContext` dataclass：见实现，全部中间产物都是显式字段（不用动态属性，便于类型检查与测试替换）；方法 `dir(stage) -> Path`、属性 `root`、`figures_dir`
  - `segment_kwargs(cfg) -> dict`：把 YAML 的 `std_threshold_deg` 映射到 `segment_sequence` 的 `std_threshold` 形参
  - `run(argv=None) -> int`：退出码 0 成功 / 2 数据不可用 / 3 流水线显式失败
  - `main()`：`sys.exit(run())`
  - 阶段函数 `stage_inspect`、`stage_detect`、`stage_register`、`stage_target`、`stage_stack`、`stage_validate`、`stage_analyze`、`stage_figures`，均接受并返回 `PipelineContext`

**阶段顺序（重要）：** `validate` 必须排在 `analyze` **之前**——定向矩阵与光度零点
都出自真值比对，`analyze` 的赤经赤纬、星等、天光面亮度全都依赖它们。

- [ ] **Step 4: 实现**

`src/cli.py`：

```python
"""统一命令行入口。

设计要点：
  - 阶段之间通过 PipelineContext 传递，每个阶段可单独运行（便于答辩现场只跑
    某一段），也可用 all 一次跑完；
  - 阶段顺序 validate 先于 analyze：定向矩阵与光度零点都出自真值比对，赤经赤
    纬、星等、天光面亮度全都依赖它们；
  - 退出码有含义：0 成功 / 2 数据不可用 / 3 流水线显式失败。"未检出目标"走
    3 而不是 0，因为静默返回成功等于产出错误结论；
  - --max-frames 只为加速测试与演示，默认 0 表示用整个跟踪段。

用法：
    bash scripts/dr.sh python -m src.cli all data/images/<dataset_dir>
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import sys
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np

from src.analysis.lightcurve import build_lightcurve, search_period
from src.analysis.orbit import cross_check_tle, estimate_from_trajectory, load_tle
from src.analysis.sensor_health import build_report, estimate_seeing, sky_brightness
from src.astrometry.orientation import Orientation, OrientationUnavailable, fit_orientation
from src.astrometry.photometry import (
    calibrate_table,
    limiting_magnitude,
    zero_point_from_truth,
)
from src.astrometry.plate_solve import best_solution
from src.calib.background import background_stats, model_background
from src.calib.hotpixel import build_from_sequence
from src.calib.segment import NoTrackingSegment, segment_sequence, tracking_segment
from src.config import load_config
from src.dataio.catalog_writer import (
    write_json,
    write_source_table,
    write_trajectory_csv,
    write_trajectory_dat,
)
from src.dataio.fits_loader import FrameSequence
from src.detect.psf import fit_table, median_fwhm
from src.detect.segmentation import detect_sequence, detect_sources_in_frame
from src.register.solver import register_sequence
from src.stack.coadd import sliding_coadd
from src.target.dual_frame import find_targets
from src.target.trajectory import build_trajectory
from src.validate import truth as truth_mod
from src.validate.repeatability import scan_thresholds
from src.validate.sextractor import diagnose
from src.viz import figures

STAGES = ("inspect", "detect", "register", "target", "stack", "validate", "analyze", "figures")

# 离线 TLE 参考（Task 20 抄录进仓库）。__file__ 在 src/ 下，parents[1] 是仓库根。
TLE_PATH = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "docs" / "reference" / "tle.txt"


def segment_kwargs(cfg: dict) -> dict:
    """YAML 用 std_threshold_deg（带单位后缀更可读），形参叫 std_threshold。"""
    s = cfg["segment"]
    return {
        "window": s["window"],
        "std_threshold": s["std_threshold_deg"],
        "min_length": s["min_length"],
    }


@dataclass
class PipelineContext:
    sequence: FrameSequence
    config: dict
    out_root: Path
    max_frames: int = 0
    segment: object | None = None
    frames: list[int] = field(default_factory=list)
    hotpixel_map: object | None = None
    detections: dict | None = None
    registration: object | None = None
    verdicts: list = field(default_factory=list)
    curve: object | None = None
    truth_report: object | None = None
    orientation: object | None = None
    orientation_assumed: bool = False
    zero_point: object | None = None
    trajectory: object | None = None
    stacks: list = field(default_factory=list)
    report_frame: int = -1
    report_image: np.ndarray | None = None
    report_table: object | None = None
    summary: dict = field(default_factory=dict)

    @property
    def root(self) -> Path:
        return self.out_root / self.sequence.dataset_id

    def dir(self, stage: str) -> Path:
        p = self.root / stage
        p.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        return p

    @property
    def figures_dir(self) -> Path:
        p = self.out_root / "figures"
        p.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        return p

    @property
    def tag(self) -> str:
        return self.sequence.dataset_id.split("_")[0]

    def exposure_s(self, frame: int) -> float:
        return float(self.sequence.headers[frame].exposure_ms) / 1000.0


def build_parser() -> argparse.ArgumentParser:
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(prog="star-chart", description="太空目标识别流水线")
    sub = ap.add_subparsers(dest="command", required=True)
    for name in STAGES + ("all",):
        s = sub.add_parser(name, help=f"运行到 {name} 阶段")
        s.add_argument("data_dir", help="数据集目录（含 *.fits）")
        s.add_argument("--out", default="output", help="输出根目录，默认 output")
        s.add_argument("--config", default=None, help="配置文件路径，默认内置 default.yaml")
        s.add_argument("--max-frames", type=int, default=0, help="限制处理帧数，0 表示全部")
    return ap


def _context(args) -> PipelineContext | int:
    d = Path(args.data_dir)
    if not d.is_dir():
        print(f"错误：数据目录不存在：{d}")
        return 2
    try:
        seq = FrameSequence.from_directory(d)
    except FileNotFoundError as exc:
        print(f"错误：{exc}（目录中需要 FITS 文件）")
        return 2
    return PipelineContext(
        sequence=seq,
        config=load_config(args.config),
        out_root=Path(args.out),
        max_frames=int(args.max_frames or 0),
    )


def stage_inspect(ctx: PipelineContext) -> PipelineContext:
    seq, cfg = ctx.sequence, ctx.config
    seg = tracking_segment(seq.headers, **segment_kwargs(cfg))
    ctx.segment = seg
    frames = seg.frames
    if ctx.max_frames:
        frames = frames[: ctx.max_frames]
    if len(frames) < 3:
        print(f"错误：可用帧数 {len(frames)} 不足，至少需要 3 帧")
        raise SystemExit(3)
    ctx.frames = frames

    h0 = seq.headers[frames[0]]
    print(f"数据集：{seq.dataset_id}")
    print(f"帧数：{len(seq)}，真值文件：{seq.truth_path.name if seq.truth_path else '无'}")
    print(f"跟踪段：{seg.label} f{seg.start}-f{seg.end}（{seg.length} 帧）")
    print(f"ΔAZ = {seg.daz_mean:+.4f} ± {seg.daz_std:.4f} 度/帧")
    print(f"指向：AZ {h0.azimuth_deg:.3f}° EL {h0.elevation_deg:.3f}°，曝光 {h0.exposure_ms:.1f} ms")
    print(f"本次处理帧：f{frames[0]}-f{frames[-1]}（{len(frames)} 帧）")

    stats = background_stats(seq.image(frames[len(frames) // 2]))
    ctx.hotpixel_map = build_from_sequence(
        seq,
        frames,
        n_sigma=cfg["hotpixel"]["n_sigma"],
        flat_ratio=cfg["hotpixel"]["flat_ratio"],
        dilate=cfg["hotpixel"]["dilate"],
        max_frames=cfg["hotpixel"]["max_frames"],
    )
    print(f"背景中位 {stats['median']:.2f} ADU，RMS {stats['std']:.2f}，饱和 {stats['n_saturated']} px")
    print(f"热像素簇：{len(ctx.hotpixel_map.clusters)} 个")

    write_json(
        {
            "dataset_id": seq.dataset_id,
            "n_frames": len(seq),
            "segments": [
                {"label": s.label, "start": s.start, "end": s.end,
                 "daz_mean": s.daz_mean, "daz_std": s.daz_std}
                for s in segment_sequence(seq.headers, **segment_kwargs(cfg))
            ],
            "background": stats,
            "hotpixel": ctx.hotpixel_map.to_dict(),
        },
        ctx.dir("calib") / "inspect.json",
    )
    ctx.summary["dataset_id"] = seq.dataset_id
    ctx.summary["n_frames"] = len(seq)
    ctx.summary["segment"] = {
        "label": seg.label, "start": seg.start, "end": seg.end,
        "daz_mean": seg.daz_mean, "daz_std": seg.daz_std,
    }
    ctx.summary["frames_used"] = [frames[0], frames[-1]]
    ctx.summary["background"] = stats
    ctx.summary["n_hot_clusters"] = len(ctx.hotpixel_map.clusters)
    return ctx


def stage_detect(ctx: PipelineContext) -> PipelineContext:
    cfg = ctx.config
    ctx.detections = detect_sequence(
        ctx.sequence,
        ctx.frames,
        n_sigma=cfg["detect"]["search_n_sigma"],
        npixels=cfg["detect"]["npixels"],
        hot_clusters=ctx.hotpixel_map.clusters,
        reject_radius=cfg["hotpixel"]["reject_radius_px"],
        background_kwargs=cfg["background"],
    )
    d = ctx.dir("detections")
    for f, t in ctx.detections.items():
        write_source_table(t, d / f"frame_{f:04d}.csv")
    counts = [len(t) for t in ctx.detections.values()]
    print(f"探测（{cfg['detect']['search_n_sigma']}σ 搜索档）：每帧 {min(counts)}-{max(counts)} 个源")
    ctx.summary["detect"] = {
        "search_n_sigma": cfg["detect"]["search_n_sigma"],
        "n_frames": len(counts),
        "min": int(min(counts)),
        "max": int(max(counts)),
        "median": float(np.median(counts)),
    }
    return ctx


def stage_register(ctx: PipelineContext) -> PipelineContext:
    ctx.registration = register_sequence(
        ctx.detections, ctx.frames, reference=ctx.frames[0], config=ctx.config["register"]
    )
    rep = ctx.registration.report()
    write_json(rep, ctx.dir("registration") / "registration.json")
    print(f"配准：{len(ctx.registration.pairs)} 个帧对，内点中位 {rep['inliers_median']:.0f}")
    ctx.summary["registration"] = rep
    return ctx


def stage_target(ctx: PipelineContext) -> PipelineContext:
    ctx.verdicts = find_targets(
        ctx.detections, ctx.frames, ctx.registration, config=ctx.config["target"]
    )
    write_json([v.to_dict() for v in ctx.verdicts], ctx.dir("catalog") / "target_verdicts.json")
    ctx.summary["n_targets"] = len(ctx.verdicts)
    if not ctx.verdicts:
        print("结果：未检出空间目标。不放宽阈值凑结果——请检查跟踪段与热像素图。")
        raise SystemExit(3)
    v = ctx.verdicts[0]
    print(
        f"检出 {len(ctx.verdicts)} 个目标；最长轨迹 {v.track.length} 帧"
        f"（f{v.track.start}-f{v.track.end}），静止性对比度 {v.contrast:.1f}x"
    )
    ctx.summary["target"] = v.to_dict()
    return ctx


def stage_stack(ctx: PipelineContext) -> PipelineContext:
    sc = ctx.config["stack"]
    window = min(sc["window"], len(ctx.frames))
    ctx.stacks = sliding_coadd(
        ctx.sequence,
        ctx.registration,
        ctx.frames,
        window=window,
        step=sc["step"],
        min_coverage=sc["min_coverage"],
        order=sc["interp_order"],
        background_kwargs=ctx.config["background"],
    )
    write_json([s.stats() for s in ctx.stacks], ctx.dir("stack") / "stacks.json")
    best = max(ctx.stacks, key=lambda s: s.median_coverage)
    print(
        f"叠加：{len(ctx.stacks)} 个窗口（W={window}，step={sc['step']}）；"
        f"最佳窗口中位覆盖 {best.median_coverage:.0f} 帧，深度增益 {best.gain_mag:.2f} mag"
    )
    ctx.summary["stack"] = best.stats()
    return ctx


def stage_validate(ctx: PipelineContext) -> PipelineContext:
    seq, cfg = ctx.sequence, ctx.config
    d = ctx.dir("validation")

    # 阈值-复现率曲线
    rc = cfg["repeatability"]
    stride = max(1, len(ctx.frames) // rc["frames"])
    sub = ctx.frames[::stride][: rc["frames"]]
    ctx.curve = scan_thresholds(
        seq,
        sub,
        ctx.registration,
        sigmas=rc["sigmas"],
        npixels=cfg["detect"]["npixels"],
        hot_clusters=ctx.hotpixel_map.clusters,
        match_radius_px=rc["match_radius_px"],
        background_kwargs=cfg["background"],
    )
    write_json(ctx.curve.to_dict(), d / "repeatability.json")
    try:
        rec = ctx.curve.recommended()
        print(
            f"复现率曲线：推荐阈值 {rec.n_sigma}σ（检出 {rec.n_detected_mean:.0f}，"
            f"复现率 {rec.reproducibility:.3f}）"
        )
        ctx.summary["recommended_n_sigma"] = rec.n_sigma
        ctx.summary["recommended_n_stars"] = rec.n_detected_mean
    except ValueError as exc:
        print(f"复现率曲线：{exc}")
        ctx.summary["recommended_n_sigma"] = None
    ctx.summary["repeatability"] = ctx.curve.to_dict()

    # 真值比对 + 定向/零点标定
    track = ctx.verdicts[0].track
    rep = None
    if seq.truth_path is not None:
        truth = truth_mod.load_truth(seq.truth_path)
        rep = truth_mod.compare(track, truth, seq, ctx.registration)
    if rep is None or not rep.available:
        if rep is None:
            rep = truth_mod.TruthReport(
                dataset_id=seq.dataset_id, available=False, note="数据集无 .DAT，无真值可比对"
            )
        print(f"真值核验：{rep.note}")
    else:
        print(
            f"真值核验：比例尺 {rep.plate_scale_arcsec_px:.3f} ± {rep.plate_scale_std:.3f} ″/px，"
            f"星等相关 {rep.mag_correlation:.3f}，时标偏差 {rep.dt_max_s:.4f} s"
        )
        mf, rows = truth_mod.match_frames(truth, seq, track.frames)
        order = {f: i for i, f in enumerate(track.frames)}
        sel = [order[f] for f in mf]
        try:
            ctx.orientation = fit_orientation(
                track.xy_sky[sel], truth.ra_deg[rows], truth.dec_deg[rows]
            )
            print(
                f"定向标定：{ctx.orientation.scale_arcsec_px:.3f} ″/px，"
                f"旋转 {ctx.orientation.rotation_deg:+.2f}°，"
                f"翻转 {ctx.orientation.flip}，RMS {ctx.orientation.rms_arcsec:.1f} ″"
            )
        except OrientationUnavailable as exc:
            print(f"定向标定不可用：{exc}")
        try:
            ctx.zero_point = zero_point_from_truth(
                track.flux[sel], truth.mag[rows], exposure_s=ctx.exposure_s(mf[0])
            )
            print(f"光度零点：{ctx.zero_point.value:.3f} ± {ctx.zero_point.std:.3f} mag")
        except ValueError as exc:
            print(f"零点定标不可用：{exc}")

    truth_mod.write_report(rep, d)
    ctx.truth_report = rep
    ctx.summary["truth"] = rep.to_dict()

    if ctx.orientation is None:
        scale = cfg["astrometry"]["fallback_scale_arcsec_px"]
        ctx.orientation = Orientation(
            scale_arcsec_px=float(scale), rotation_deg=0.0, flip=False,
            rms_arcsec=float("nan"), n_points=0,
        )
        ctx.orientation_assumed = True
        print(f"提示：定向未标定，比例尺按假设值 {scale} ″/px 处理，报告中已标注该假设")
    ctx.summary["orientation"] = dict(
        ctx.orientation.to_dict(), assumed=ctx.orientation_assumed
    )
    ctx.summary["photometry"] = {
        "zero_point": None if ctx.zero_point is None else ctx.zero_point.value,
        "zero_point_std": None if ctx.zero_point is None else ctx.zero_point.std,
        "source": "none" if ctx.zero_point is None else ctx.zero_point.source,
    }
    return ctx


def stage_analyze(ctx: PipelineContext) -> PipelineContext:
    seq, cfg = ctx.sequence, ctx.config
    ori = ctx.orientation
    track = ctx.verdicts[0].track
    d = ctx.dir("analysis")

    solution = best_solution(seq.headers[ctx.frames[0]], orientation=ori)
    write_json(
        {"ra_deg": solution.ra_deg, "dec_deg": solution.dec_deg, "source": solution.source},
        d / "pointing.json",
    )

    trj = build_trajectory(track, seq, ori, solution, zero_point=ctx.zero_point)
    ctx.trajectory = trj
    write_trajectory_csv(trj, ctx.dir("catalog") / "target_trajectory.csv")
    write_trajectory_dat(trj, ctx.dir("catalog") / "target_trajectory.DAT")
    write_json(trj.summary(), d / "trajectory_summary.json")
    print(
        f"轨迹：{len(trj)} 点，平均角速度 {trj.mean_rate_arcsec_s:.1f} "
        f"± {trj.rate_std_arcsec_s:.1f} ″/s"
    )
    ctx.summary["trajectory"] = trj.summary()

    orb = estimate_from_trajectory(
        trj, seq.headers[ctx.frames[0]], bracket_km=tuple(cfg["orbit"]["bracket_km"])
    )
    # 与离线 TLE 交叉校验。目录名首段就是 NORAD 编号（ctx.tag），仓库内没有对应
    # 根数时如实记 null，绝不联网下载——容器无网络，那只会变成一次必然的超时。
    tle_check: dict = {"agrees": None, "note": "仓库内无该目标的 TLE 参考"}
    try:
        tle_check = cross_check_tle(orb, load_tle(TLE_PATH, int(ctx.tag)))
    except (KeyError, ValueError, OSError) as exc:
        tle_check["note"] = f"TLE 校验跳过：{exc}"
    write_json(dict(orb.to_dict(), tle_cross_check=tle_check), d / "orbit.json")
    print(
        f"轨道高度（圆轨道一阶估计）：{orb.height_km:.0f} km，周期 {orb.period_min:.1f} min"
        if orb.converged
        else f"轨道高度反演未收敛：{orb.note}"
    )
    print(f"TLE 校验：{tle_check['note']}")
    ctx.summary["orbit"] = dict(orb.to_dict(), tle_cross_check=tle_check)

    lc = build_lightcurve(trj)
    per = search_period(lc)
    write_json({"lightcurve": lc.to_records(), "period": per.to_dict()}, d / "lightcurve.json")
    print(f"光变：峰谷差 {lc.amplitude_mag:.3f} mag；{per.note}")
    ctx.summary["lightcurve"] = dict(per.to_dict(), amplitude_mag=lc.amplitude_mag)

    # 对外报数帧：用报数阈值重跑一帧，作为星表、星等分布、外部核对的共同基准
    mid = ctx.frames[len(ctx.frames) // 2]
    img = seq.image(mid)
    model = model_background(img, **cfg["background"])
    table = detect_sources_in_frame(
        img,
        model,
        n_sigma=cfg["detect"]["report_n_sigma"],
        npixels=cfg["detect"]["npixels"],
        hot_clusters=ctx.hotpixel_map.clusters,
        reject_radius=cfg["hotpixel"]["reject_radius_px"],
        frame=mid,
    )
    ctx.report_frame, ctx.report_image, ctx.report_table = mid, img, table
    extra = {"n_sigma": cfg["detect"]["report_n_sigma"], "frame": mid}
    if ctx.zero_point is not None:
        mags = calibrate_table(table, ctx.zero_point, exposure_s=ctx.exposure_s(mid))
        extra["limiting_mag"] = limiting_magnitude(
            table, ctx.zero_point, exposure_s=ctx.exposure_s(mid)
        )
        extra["mag_min"] = float(np.nanmin(mags)) if np.isfinite(mags).any() else None
    write_source_table(table, ctx.dir("catalog") / f"stars_f{mid:04d}.csv", extra=extra)
    print(f"报数帧 f{mid}（{cfg['detect']['report_n_sigma']}σ）：{len(table)} 颗星")
    ctx.summary["report_frame"] = mid
    ctx.summary["n_stars_report_sigma"] = len(table)
    ctx.summary["limiting_mag"] = extra.get("limiting_mag")

    _, fits = fit_table(model.subtract(img), table.brightest(200), **cfg["psf"])
    seeing = estimate_seeing(fits, ori.scale_arcsec_px)
    stats = background_stats(img)
    health = build_report(
        seq.dataset_id,
        ctx.hotpixel_map,
        stats,
        epoch_isot=str(seq.headers[0].date_obs.isot),
        fwhm_median_px=median_fwhm(fits),
    )
    sky = sky_brightness(
        stats["median"],
        zero_point=ctx.zero_point,
        scale_arcsec_px=ori.scale_arcsec_px,
        exposure_s=ctx.exposure_s(mid),
    )
    write_json(
        {"sensor": health.to_dict(), "seeing": seeing.to_dict(),
         "sky_brightness_mag_arcsec2": sky},
        d / "observing_conditions.json",
    )
    print(
        "观测条件：星像宽度 "
        + (f"{seeing.fwhm_px:.2f} px / {seeing.fwhm_arcsec:.1f} ″" if seeing.fwhm_px else "不可估")
        + (f"，天光 {sky:.2f} mag/arcsec²" if sky is not None else "，天光未定标（零点缺失）")
    )
    ctx.summary["sensor"] = health.to_dict()
    ctx.summary["seeing"] = seeing.to_dict()
    ctx.summary["sky_brightness_mag_arcsec2"] = sky

    # 外部交叉核对：缺 Source Extractor 时如实标注未做
    cm = diagnose(img, table, thresh=cfg["detect"]["report_n_sigma"],
                  min_area=cfg["detect"]["npixels"])
    write_json(cm.to_dict(), ctx.dir("validation") / "sextractor.json")
    print(
        f"外部交叉核对：precision {cm.precision:.3f}，recall {cm.recall:.3f}"
        if cm.available
        else f"外部交叉核对：{cm.note}"
    )
    ctx.summary["sextractor"] = cm.to_dict()
    return ctx


def stage_figures(ctx: PipelineContext) -> PipelineContext:
    seq, fd, tag = ctx.sequence, ctx.figures_dir, ctx.tag
    made = []

    if ctx.curve is not None:
        made.append(figures.plot_threshold_curve(
            ctx.curve, fd / f"{tag}_threshold_curve.png",
            title=f"数据集 {tag}：探测阈值与跨帧复现率",
        ))
    made.append(figures.plot_segments(
        seq.headers,
        segment_sequence(seq.headers, **segment_kwargs(ctx.config)),
        fd / f"{tag}_segments.png",
    ))
    if ctx.report_table is not None:
        xy = None
        track = ctx.verdicts[0].track if ctx.verdicts else None
        if track is not None and ctx.report_frame in track.frames:
            i = track.frames.index(ctx.report_frame)
            xy = (float(track.xy_det[i, 0]), float(track.xy_det[i, 1]))
        made.append(figures.plot_detections(
            ctx.report_image, ctx.report_table, fd / f"{tag}_detections.png",
            target_xy=xy, hot_clusters=ctx.hotpixel_map.clusters,
        ))
        if ctx.zero_point is not None:
            exp = ctx.exposure_s(ctx.report_frame)
            made.append(figures.plot_magnitude_histogram(
                calibrate_table(ctx.report_table, ctx.zero_point, exposure_s=exp),
                fd / f"{tag}_magnitudes.png",
                limiting=limiting_magnitude(ctx.report_table, ctx.zero_point, exposure_s=exp),
            ))
    if ctx.trajectory is not None:
        made.append(figures.plot_trajectory(ctx.trajectory, fd / f"{tag}_trajectory.png"))
        lc = build_lightcurve(ctx.trajectory)
        per = search_period(lc)
        made.append(figures.plot_lightcurve(
            lc, fd / f"{tag}_lightcurve.png",
            period=per.period_s if per.significant else None,
        ))
    if ctx.stacks:
        best = max(ctx.stacks, key=lambda s: s.median_coverage)
        made.append(figures.plot_stack(best, fd / f"{tag}_stack.png"))
    made.append(figures.plot_hotpixels(
        ctx.hotpixel_map, fd / f"{tag}_hotpixels.png", shape=ctx.hotpixel_map.mask.shape
    ))
    if ctx.truth_report is not None:
        made.append(figures.plot_truth_comparison(ctx.truth_report, fd / f"{tag}_truth.png"))

    print(f"成果图 {len(made)} 张，已写入 {fd}")
    ctx.summary["figures"] = [p.name for p in made]
    return ctx


_CHAIN = [
    stage_inspect, stage_detect, stage_register, stage_target,
    stage_stack, stage_validate, stage_analyze, stage_figures,
]
# 每个子命令跑到自己为止；stack 不是 validate 的前置，但顺序执行代价很低，
# 保持单一链条比维护一张依赖图更不容易出错。
ORDER = {name: _CHAIN[: i + 1] for i, name in enumerate(STAGES)}
ORDER["all"] = list(_CHAIN)


def run(argv=None) -> int:
    args = build_parser().parse_args(argv)
    ctx = _context(args)
    if isinstance(ctx, int):
        return ctx
    try:
        for stage in ORDER[args.command]:
            ctx = stage(ctx)
    except SystemExit as exc:
        return int(exc.code or 3)
    except NoTrackingSegment as exc:
        print(f"错误：{exc}")
        return 3
    write_json(ctx.summary, ctx.root / "summary.json")
    print(f"完成。产物根目录：{ctx.root}")
    return 0


def main() -> None:
    sys.exit(run())


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

- [ ] **Step 5: 运行测试，确认通过**

```bash
bash scripts/dr.sh pytest tests/test_cli.py -v
```

## Task 28: 跨数据集对比入口（派生数据集 6：传感器缺陷跨历元一致性）

Task 22 建好了 `compare_epochs` 这把工具，但流水线一次只处理一个数据集，谁也不会
去调它。这个任务补上真正的入口：一条命令同时跑两个数据集，输出两件东西——

1. **跨历元坏点一致性**：相隔约 4 个月的两次观测复现同一批坏点。这是自证式证据，
   不依赖任何外部真值，也无法用"你调参凑出来的"来质疑。
2. **参数不过拟合的证据**：同一份 `default.yaml`、同一套阈值，在两个数据集上都跑通
   且推荐阈值一致。这直接回应"你的阈值是不是照着某一组数据挑的"这类原理性质疑。

**Files:**
- Create: `src/compare.py`
- Modify: `src/cli.py`（新增 `cross` 子命令与分支）
- Modify: `src/viz/figures.py`（新增 `plot_cross_epoch`）
- Test: `tests/test_compare.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `src.cli.PipelineContext` 与阶段函数、`src.analysis.sensor_health.build_report` / `compare_epochs`、`src.viz.figures`
- Produces:
  - `CrossDatasetReport` dataclass：`datasets: list[str]`、`sensor: dict`、`parameters: list[dict]`、`consistent_threshold: bool`、`note: str`；`to_dict()`
  - `analyze_one(directory, config, out_root, *, max_frames=0) -> PipelineContext`（跑到 `validate` 为止；`stack`/`analyze` 与跨数据集结论无关，省掉可观时间）
  - `run_cross(directories, *, out_root, config, max_frames=0) -> CrossDatasetReport`
  - `figures.plot_cross_epoch(result, path, *, shape, labels) -> Path`
- 导入方向：`src/compare.py` 顶层导入 `src.cli`；`src/cli.py` **只在 `run()` 内部**惰性导入 `src.compare`。方向不能反，否则循环导入。

- [ ] **Step 1: 写失败测试**

`tests/test_compare.py`：

```python
from __future__ import annotations

import json

import pytest

from src.cli import build_parser, run
from src.compare import run_cross

# 24 帧：B 的跟踪段从 f16 起，16 帧只剩个位数落在段内，不足以连成轨迹；
# 24 帧留出余量又不至于让测试变慢。
MAX_FRAMES = 24


def test_cross_epoch_recovers_known_hot_clusters(
    dataset_a_dir, dataset_b_dir, tmp_path, cfg
):
    """已知三个簇 (244,3174)/(312,3206)/(327,3210) 在两个历元都应存在。"""
    rep = run_cross(
        [dataset_a_dir, dataset_b_dir], out_root=tmp_path, config=cfg, max_frames=MAX_FRAMES
    )
    assert rep.sensor["n_common"] >= 3
    assert rep.sensor["persistence"] > 0.5

    known = [(244.0, 3174.0), (312.0, 3206.0), (327.0, 3210.0)]
    found = [(tuple(c["xy_a"]), c["separation_px"]) for c in rep.sensor["common"]]
    for kx, ky in known:
        hit = [
            sep for (fx, fy), sep in found if abs(fx - kx) < 4.0 and abs(fy - ky) < 4.0
        ]
        assert hit, f"已知坏点簇 ({kx}, {ky}) 未被跨历元复现"
        # 匹配半径是 8 px（配置里的 reject_radius_px），但同一颗坏点在两个历元
        # 应当远好于这个上限。断言 2 px 才真正在检验"同位"，否则只是复述半径。
        assert min(hit) < 2.0, f"簇 ({kx}, {ky}) 跨历元偏差 {min(hit):.2f} px，超出预期"


def test_cross_report_records_both_datasets(dataset_a_dir, dataset_b_dir, tmp_path, cfg):
    rep = run_cross(
        [dataset_a_dir, dataset_b_dir], out_root=tmp_path, config=cfg, max_frames=MAX_FRAMES
    )
    assert len(rep.parameters) == 2
    assert {p["dataset_id"] for p in rep.parameters} == {dataset_a_dir.name, dataset_b_dir.name}
    for p in rep.parameters:
        assert p["n_targets"] >= 1              # 两个数据集都必须检出目标
        assert p["segment_label"] == "tracking"
    truth = {p["dataset_id"]: p["truth_available"] for p in rep.parameters}
    assert truth[dataset_b_dir.name] is True    # 只有 B 有 .DAT
    assert truth[dataset_a_dir.name] is False


def test_cross_subcommand_writes_artifacts(dataset_a_dir, dataset_b_dir, tmp_path):
    code = run([
        "cross", str(dataset_a_dir), str(dataset_b_dir),
        "--out", str(tmp_path), "--max-frames", str(MAX_FRAMES),
    ])
    assert code == 0
    payload = json.loads(
        (tmp_path / "cross" / "cross_dataset.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    )
    assert payload["sensor"]["n_common"] >= 3
    assert (tmp_path / "figures" / "cross_epoch_hotpixels.png").exists()


def test_cross_requires_two_directories(tmp_path):
    with pytest.raises(SystemExit):
        build_parser().parse_args(["cross", str(tmp_path)])


def test_cross_missing_directory_returns_exit_code_2(dataset_b_dir, tmp_path, capsys):
    code = run(["cross", str(dataset_b_dir), str(tmp_path / "nope"), "--out", str(tmp_path)])
    assert code == 2
    assert "不存在" in capsys.readouterr().out
```

- [ ] **Step 3: 给 `figures.py` 加跨历元图**

在 `src/viz/figures.py` 末尾追加：

```python
def plot_cross_epoch(result, path, *, shape, labels) -> Path:
    """跨历元坏点对照：同位复现的簇用大空心圈标出，单侧出现的用小实心点。

    result 是 CrossEpochResult.to_dict() 的产物（纯 dict），这样这张图既能由
    流水线直接调用，也能事后从落盘的 JSON 重画。
    """
    setup_matplotlib()
    ny, nx = shape
    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(7.2, 7.2))

    for group, color, marker, label in (
        (result["only_in_a"], "#4c78a8", "o", f"仅 {labels[0]}"),
        (result["only_in_b"], "#f58518", "s", f"仅 {labels[1]}"),
    ):
        if group:
            ax.scatter(
                [c["cx"] for c in group], [c["cy"] for c in group],
                s=18, c=color, marker=marker, alpha=0.7, label=label,
            )
    common = result["common"]
    if common:
        ax.scatter(
            [c["xy_a"][0] for c in common], [c["xy_a"][1] for c in common],
            s=140, facecolors="none", edgecolors="#d62728", linewidths=1.8,
            label=f"两历元同位复现（{len(common)} 簇）",
        )

    ax.set_xlim(0, nx)
    ax.set_ylim(0, ny)
    ax.set_aspect("equal")
    ax.set_xlabel("列 x（像素）")
    ax.set_ylabel("行 y（像素）")
    ax.set_title(
        f"传感器缺陷跨历元一致性：{labels[0]} vs {labels[1]}\n"
        f"复现 {result['n_common']} 簇，持久率 {100.0 * result['persistence']:.0f}%，"
        f"匹配半径 {result['match_radius_px']:.1f} px"
    )
    ax.legend(loc="upper right", fontsize=8)
    ax.grid(alpha=0.25)
    return _save(fig, path)
```

- [ ] **Step 4: 实现 `src/compare.py`**

```python
"""跨数据集对比：传感器缺陷跨历元一致性 + 参数不过拟合证据。

只跑到 validate 阶段：叠加与轨迹分析都是单数据集结论，与跨历元比较无关，
省下的时间足够把这条命令放进答辩现场演示。
"""
from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from pathlib import Path

from src.analysis.sensor_health import build_report, compare_epochs
from src.cli import (
    PipelineContext,
    stage_detect,
    stage_inspect,
    stage_register,
    stage_target,
    stage_validate,
)
from src.dataio.catalog_writer import write_json
from src.dataio.fits_loader import FrameSequence
from src.viz import figures

_STAGES = (stage_inspect, stage_detect, stage_register, stage_target, stage_validate)


@dataclass
class CrossDatasetReport:
    datasets: list[str]
    sensor: dict
    parameters: list[dict] = field(default_factory=list)
    consistent_threshold: bool = False
    note: str = ""

    def to_dict(self) -> dict:
        return {
            "datasets": list(self.datasets),
            "sensor": self.sensor,
            "parameters": list(self.parameters),
            "consistent_threshold": self.consistent_threshold,
            "note": self.note,
        }


def analyze_one(
    directory, config: dict, out_root, *, max_frames: int = 0
) -> PipelineContext:
    ctx = PipelineContext(
        sequence=FrameSequence.from_directory(Path(directory)),
        config=config,
        out_root=Path(out_root),
        max_frames=max_frames,
    )
    print(f"===== {ctx.sequence.dataset_id} =====")
    for stage in _STAGES:
        ctx = stage(ctx)
    write_json(ctx.summary, ctx.root / "summary.json")
    return ctx


def _parameters(ctx: PipelineContext) -> dict:
    """抽出"同一份配置在两个数据集上表现如何"所需的全部数字。"""
    s = ctx.summary
    return {
        "dataset_id": s["dataset_id"],
        "epoch_isot": str(ctx.sequence.headers[0].date_obs.isot),
        "n_frames": s["n_frames"],
        "segment_label": s["segment"]["label"],
        "segment_frames": [s["segment"]["start"], s["segment"]["end"]],
        "daz_mean_deg": s["segment"]["daz_mean"],
        "background_median": s["background"]["median"],
        "background_rms": s["background"]["std"],
        "n_hot_clusters": s["n_hot_clusters"],
        "detect_median": s["detect"]["median"],
        "inliers_median": s["registration"]["inliers_median"],
        "recommended_n_sigma": s.get("recommended_n_sigma"),
        "recommended_n_stars": s.get("recommended_n_stars"),
        "n_targets": s["n_targets"],
        "truth_available": s["truth"]["available"],
        "plate_scale_arcsec_px": s["truth"].get("plate_scale_arcsec_px"),
    }


def run_cross(directories, *, out_root, config: dict, max_frames: int = 0) -> CrossDatasetReport:
    directories = list(directories)
    if len(directories) != 2:
        raise ValueError(f"跨数据集对比需要恰好 2 个目录，收到 {len(directories)} 个")
    out_root = Path(out_root)
    contexts = [analyze_one(d, config, out_root, max_frames=max_frames) for d in directories]

    reports = [
        build_report(
            c.summary["dataset_id"],
            c.hotpixel_map,
            c.summary["background"],
            epoch_isot=str(c.sequence.headers[0].date_obs.isot),
        )
        for c in contexts
    ]
    cross = compare_epochs(
        reports[0], reports[1], match_radius_px=config["hotpixel"]["reject_radius_px"]
    )
    params = [_parameters(c) for c in contexts]
    sigmas = {p["recommended_n_sigma"] for p in params}
    consistent = len(sigmas) == 1 and None not in sigmas

    rep = CrossDatasetReport(
        datasets=[p["dataset_id"] for p in params],
        sensor=cross.to_dict(),
        parameters=params,
        consistent_threshold=consistent,
        note=(
            f"两个历元（{reports[0].epoch_isot} / {reports[1].epoch_isot}）在 "
            f"{cross.match_radius_px:.1f} px 内复现 {cross.n_common} 个坏点簇，"
            f"持久率 {cross.persistence:.2f}；"
            + (
                f"同一份配置在两个数据集上推荐阈值一致（{sorted(sigmas)[0]}σ），"
                "说明参数未针对单一数据集调优。"
                if consistent
                else "两数据集推荐阈值不一致，须在报告中说明差异来源，不得掩盖。"
            )
        ),
    )

    d = out_root / "cross"
    d.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    write_json(rep.to_dict(), d / "cross_dataset.json")
    figures.plot_cross_epoch(
        rep.sensor,
        out_root / "figures" / "cross_epoch_hotpixels.png",
        shape=contexts[0].hotpixel_map.mask.shape,
        labels=[p["dataset_id"].split("_")[0] for p in params],
    )
    print(rep.note)
    return rep
```

- [ ] **Step 5: 在 `src/cli.py` 里挂上 `cross` 子命令**

`build_parser()` 中，`for name in STAGES + ("all",):` 循环之后追加：

```python
    c = sub.add_parser("cross", help="跨数据集对比：坏点跨历元一致性 + 参数不过拟合证据")
    c.add_argument("data_dirs", nargs=2, help="两个数据集目录")
    c.add_argument("--out", default="output", help="输出根目录，默认 output")
    c.add_argument("--config", default=None, help="配置文件路径")
    c.add_argument("--max-frames", type=int, default=0, help="每个数据集限制帧数，0 表示全部")
```

`run()` 里，`ctx = _context(args)` **之前**插入分支——`cross` 没有单个 `data_dir`
参数，必须在 `_context` 取用 `args.data_dir` 之前拦下：

```python
    if args.command == "cross":
        from src.compare import run_cross  # 惰性导入：src.compare 顶层导入本模块

        dirs = [Path(p) for p in args.data_dirs]
        missing = [p for p in dirs if not p.is_dir()]
        if missing:
            print("错误：数据目录不存在：" + "、".join(str(p) for p in missing))
            return 2
        try:
            run_cross(
                dirs,
                out_root=Path(args.out),
                config=load_config(args.config),
                max_frames=int(args.max_frames or 0),
            )
        except SystemExit as exc:
            return int(exc.code or 3)
        except NoTrackingSegment as exc:
            print(f"错误：{exc}")
            return 3
        return 0
```

## Task 29: 跨数据集回归测试套件

到这里全部功能都有单元测试，但没有一处把 `## 已测得的基准数字` 钉住。这个任务
把那张表变成可执行的断言：任何一次重构若让比例尺、星等相关、内点数、目标轨迹
长度漂出容差，测试立刻红。这是"结果可复现"这句话的唯一硬支撑。

容差取法：已实测的量给 ±5%（或明确的绝对容差），只经过一次验证的量给宽一档的
范围。宁可容差略宽也不要让测试变成随机红灯——一条会偶发失败的测试等于没有测试。

**Files:**
- Create: `tests/test_regression.py`
- Modify: `pytest.ini`（注册 `regression` 标记）
- Test: 本任务的产物就是测试

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `src.compare.analyze_one`、`src.cli` 阶段函数、`tests/conftest.py` 的 `dataset_a_dir` / `dataset_b_dir` / `cfg`
- Produces:
  - `tests/test_regression.py` 中的 session 级 fixture `pipeline_b`、`pipeline_a`（各跑一次完整跟踪段，供全部断言复用，避免重复计算）
  - pytest 标记 `regression`：`pytest -m "not regression"` 可跳过这套慢测试

- [ ] **Step 1: 注册标记**

在 `pytest.ini` 的 `[pytest]` 段追加：

```ini
markers =
    regression: 跨数据集回归，钉住 docs 中已实测的基准数字（慢，整段跟踪段全帧）
```

- [ ] **Step 2: 写测试**

`tests/test_regression.py`：

```python
"""跨数据集回归：把计划文档"已测得的基准数字"变成可执行断言。

这套测试跑完整跟踪段（B 55 帧、A 36 帧），单次数分钟，因此统一打
regression 标记并用 session fixture 共享结果。日常开发用
`pytest -m "not regression"` 跳过。
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import numpy as np
import pytest

from src.compare import analyze_one
from src.config import load_config

pytestmark = pytest.mark.regression


@pytest.fixture(scope="session")
def pipeline_b(dataset_b_dir, tmp_path_factory):
    """整段跟踪段跑一次，供本模块全部断言复用。

    不能用 tests/conftest.py 里的 cfg fixture——那是 function 作用域，
    session 作用域的 fixture 不允许依赖它。这里直接调 load_config()，
    效果相同且明确表示"用的就是仓库里那份 default.yaml，未作任何改动"。
    """
    return analyze_one(dataset_b_dir, load_config(), tmp_path_factory.mktemp("reg_b"))


@pytest.fixture(scope="session")
def pipeline_a(dataset_a_dir, tmp_path_factory):
    return analyze_one(dataset_a_dir, load_config(), tmp_path_factory.mktemp("reg_a"))


# ---------- 数据集 B：机架状态与配准 ----------

def test_b_tracking_segment_matches_measurement(pipeline_b):
    seg = pipeline_b.segment
    assert seg.label == "tracking"
    assert seg.start == pytest.approx(16, abs=2)
    assert seg.end == pytest.approx(70, abs=2)
    assert seg.length == pytest.approx(55, abs=4)
    assert seg.daz_mean == pytest.approx(0.222, abs=0.01)
    assert seg.daz_std < 0.02


def test_b_registration_inliers_and_cumulative_transform(pipeline_b):
    rep = pipeline_b.summary["registration"]
    assert rep["inliers_median"] == pytest.approx(84, abs=15)
    assert rep["inliers_median"] >= 30      # 配置的 min_inliers 下限
    assert rep["rms_median_px"] < 1.5
    # f16→f70 累计：缩放 0.99822、旋转 −3.325°。缩放必须接近 1——星场是刚性
    # 旋转平移，若解出明显的缩放说明模型或匹配出了问题。
    cum = rep["cumulative"]
    assert cum["scale"] == pytest.approx(1.0, abs=0.01)
    assert abs(cum["rotation_deg"]) == pytest.approx(3.325, abs=0.5)


def test_b_hot_clusters_present(pipeline_b):
    """三个已知簇必须都在。坐标容差 4 px 覆盖膨胀与质心定义差异。"""
    known = [(244.0, 3174.0), (312.0, 3206.0), (327.0, 3210.0)]
    got = [(c.cx, c.cy) for c in pipeline_b.hotpixel_map.clusters]
    for kx, ky in known:
        assert any(abs(x - kx) < 4.0 and abs(y - ky) < 4.0 for x, y in got), (
            f"热像素簇 ({kx}, {ky}) 丢失"
        )


def test_b_background_level(pipeline_b):
    bkg = pipeline_b.summary["background"]
    assert bkg["median"] == pytest.approx(6.45, abs=1.5)
    assert bkg["std"] == pytest.approx(3.80, abs=1.0)
    assert bkg["n_saturated"] == 0


# ---------- 数据集 B：目标与真值 ----------

def test_b_exactly_one_target_with_expected_track(pipeline_b):
    assert len(pipeline_b.verdicts) == 1
    t = pipeline_b.verdicts[0].track
    assert t.length == pytest.approx(54, abs=5)
    assert t.drift_det_px == pytest.approx(95.8, rel=0.20)
    assert t.drift_sky_px > 5000.0          # 天球系位移比探测器系大两个量级
    assert not t.pixel_locked


def test_b_dual_frame_velocity_contrast(pipeline_b):
    v = pipeline_b.verdicts[0]
    assert v.is_target
    assert v.v_det_px == pytest.approx(1.81, rel=0.30)
    assert v.v_sky_px == pytest.approx(125.73, rel=0.15)
    assert v.contrast > 20.0


def test_b_truth_report_matches_measurement(pipeline_b):
    r = pipeline_b.truth_report
    assert r.available is True
    assert r.plate_scale_arcsec_px == pytest.approx(6.179, abs=0.10)
    assert r.plate_scale_std < 0.05
    assert r.angular_step_arcsec == pytest.approx(776.9, rel=0.05)
    assert r.mag_correlation > 0.90
    assert r.dt_max_s < 0.5                 # 时标对齐；实测 0.0000 s


def test_b_zero_point_matches_measurement(pipeline_b):
    zp = pipeline_b.zero_point
    assert zp is not None
    assert zp.value == pytest.approx(15.335, abs=0.5)
    assert zp.std < 0.5


def test_b_orientation_agrees_with_truth_plate_scale(pipeline_b):
    ori = pipeline_b.orientation
    assert pipeline_b.orientation_assumed is False
    assert ori.scale_arcsec_px == pytest.approx(6.179, abs=0.15)
    assert abs(ori.rotation_deg) < 180.0
    assert np.isfinite(ori.rms_arcsec)


# ---------- 数据集 B：阈值-复现率（25 分题的主证据） ----------

def test_b_threshold_curve_is_monotone_in_the_right_direction(pipeline_b):
    """低阈值检出多但复现率低——这是"90% 是噪声"论证的形式化。"""
    pts = sorted(pipeline_b.curve.points, key=lambda p: p.n_sigma)
    counts = [p.n_detected_mean for p in pts]
    assert counts == sorted(counts, reverse=True), "检出数应随阈值单调下降"
    assert pts[0].reproducibility < pts[-1].reproducibility, "复现率应随阈值上升"
    low = next(p for p in pts if p.n_sigma == pytest.approx(2.0))
    assert low.n_detected_mean > 1000       # 2σ 实测 2284
    assert low.reproducibility < 0.5        # 绝大部分不可复现


def test_b_recommended_threshold_and_star_count(pipeline_b):
    rec = pipeline_b.curve.recommended()
    assert rec.n_sigma >= 4.0
    assert rec.reproducibility >= 0.85
    assert 50 < rec.n_detected_mean < 400   # 报数量级：10²，不是 10³


# ---------- 数据集 A：同一配置不过拟合 ----------

def test_a_runs_with_identical_config(pipeline_a):
    assert pipeline_a.segment.label == "tracking"
    assert len(pipeline_a.verdicts) >= 1
    assert pipeline_a.truth_report.available is False
    assert pipeline_a.zero_point is None
    assert pipeline_a.orientation_assumed is True


def test_a_hot_clusters_overlap_with_b(pipeline_a, pipeline_b):
    """同一台相机，隔约 4 个月，坏点应大量同位复现。"""
    from src.analysis.sensor_health import build_report, compare_epochs

    ra = build_report(
        "A", pipeline_a.hotpixel_map, pipeline_a.summary["background"],
        epoch_isot=str(pipeline_a.sequence.headers[0].date_obs.isot),
    )
    rb = build_report(
        "B", pipeline_b.hotpixel_map, pipeline_b.summary["background"],
        epoch_isot=str(pipeline_b.sequence.headers[0].date_obs.isot),
    )
    cross = compare_epochs(ra, rb, match_radius_px=8.0)
    assert cross.n_common >= 3
    assert cross.persistence > 0.5


def test_a_and_b_recommend_the_same_threshold(pipeline_a, pipeline_b):
    """阈值若必须逐数据集微调，就不能声称方法通用。允许 1σ 差异。"""
    sa = pipeline_a.summary["recommended_n_sigma"]
    sb = pipeline_b.summary["recommended_n_sigma"]
    assert sa is not None and sb is not None, (
        f"有数据集没有任何阈值达到复现率下限：A={sa} B={sb}"
    )
    assert abs(sa - sb) <= 1.0, f"推荐阈值差异过大：A {sa}σ vs B {sb}σ"
```

> **关于数据集 A 的一个未验证前提：** A 只有 36 帧且无 `.DAT`，其跟踪段长度与
> 是否含可判定目标**尚未实测**。若执行到这里发现 A 的跟踪段太短或检不出目标，
> 不要放宽 `target` 的判据去凑——正确处理是：把 A 的角色降级为"仅验证探测与配准
> 参数可迁移"（即 `analyze_one` 对 A 只跑到 `register`），删掉本任务中依赖 A
> 目标的断言，并在技术文档第 9 节如实写明 A 的数据量不足以支撑目标判决。
> 这属于结论变更，须记录在 `docs/reports/measurements.md`。

## Task 30: 中文技术文档

省赛评审只看 PPT 与视频，国赛现场要交源代码与可运行程序，而"原理性质疑每答不出
一个扣 1 分"意味着真正决定分数的是**每个数字背后的道理能不能当场讲清**。这份文档
就是那套道理的书面版，同时充当 PPT 文案与答辩的共同底本。

写法要求：全部数字从 `docs/reports/measurements.md` 与 `output/*/summary.json` 抄，
**不允许凭记忆写数**；每个结论后面跟上它的证据来源（哪个文件、哪条命令）。

**Files:**
- Create: `docs/reports/技术文档.md`
- Create: `docs/reports/PPT素材清单.md`
- Test: `tests/test_docs.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `docs/reports/measurements.md`、`output/<dataset_id>/summary.json`、`output/figures/*.png`
- Produces:
  - `docs/reports/技术文档.md`：固定章节结构（见 Step 2）
  - `docs/reports/PPT素材清单.md`：页码 → 图片文件 → 一句话结论 → 数据来源 的四列表
  - `tests/test_docs.py`：校验文档结构完整、无占位符、引用的图片都真实存在

- [ ] **Step 1: 写文档校验测试**

`tests/test_docs.py`：

```python
"""文档校验：结构完整、无占位符、引用的图都存在。

这些断言防的是很具体的失败：文档里留着"待补"就交上去，或者引用了一张
早已改名的图，答辩现场翻不出来。
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import re
from pathlib import Path

import pytest

REPO_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
DOC = REPO_ROOT / "docs" / "reports" / "技术文档.md"
PPT = REPO_ROOT / "docs" / "reports" / "PPT素材清单.md"

REQUIRED_SECTIONS = [
    "## 1. 观测数据与设备",
    "## 2. 关键发现：望远镜在跟踪目标",
    "## 3. 方法：双坐标系交叉确认",
    "## 4. 星点计数的科学依据",
    "## 5. 动目标识别结果",
    "## 6. 真值定量核验",
    "## 7. 派生数据集（创新赛项）",
    "## 8. 复现步骤",
    "## 9. 局限与不做的事",
    "## 10. 预置质疑与应答",
]

PLACEHOLDERS = ["TBD", "TODO", "待补", "XXX", "???", "待填"]


@pytest.fixture(scope="module")
def doc_text() -> str:
    if not DOC.exists():
        pytest.fail(f"技术文档缺失：{DOC}")
    return DOC.read_text(encoding="utf-8")


def test_all_sections_present(doc_text):
    missing = [s for s in REQUIRED_SECTIONS if s not in doc_text]
    assert not missing, f"缺少章节：{missing}"


def test_no_placeholders(doc_text):
    found = [p for p in PLACEHOLDERS if p in doc_text]
    assert not found, f"文档含占位符：{found}"


def test_key_numbers_are_stated(doc_text):
    """核心数字必须出现在文档里，否则答辩时无从引用。"""
    for token in ["6.179", "15.335", "0.949", "125.73", "2284"]:
        assert token in doc_text, f"缺少关键数字 {token}"


def test_limitations_section_disclaims_orbit_determination(doc_text):
    """轨道高度是一阶估计，不是定轨。文档必须自己先说清楚。"""
    tail = doc_text.split("## 9. 局限与不做的事", 1)[1]
    assert "圆轨道" in tail
    assert "定轨" in tail


def test_ppt_manifest_rows_point_at_existing_figures():
    if not PPT.exists():
        pytest.fail(f"PPT 素材清单缺失：{PPT}")
    text = PPT.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    names = re.findall(r"`([\w.\-]+\.png)`", text)
    assert len(names) >= 8, f"清单引用的图太少：{len(names)}"

    figdir = REPO_ROOT / "output" / "figures"
    if not figdir.is_dir():
        pytest.skip("尚未生成成果图，先跑 python -m src.cli all")
    on_disk = {p.name for p in figdir.glob("*.png")}
    missing = [n for n in names if n not in on_disk]
    assert not missing, f"清单引用了不存在的图：{missing}"


def test_ppt_manifest_covers_every_generated_figure():
    if not PPT.exists():
        pytest.fail(f"PPT 素材清单缺失：{PPT}")
    figdir = REPO_ROOT / "output" / "figures"
    if not figdir.is_dir():
        pytest.skip("尚未生成成果图")
    text = PPT.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    orphan = [p.name for p in figdir.glob("*.png") if p.name not in text]
    assert not orphan, f"这些图没进清单，等于白画：{orphan}"
```

按下面的骨架写，每节的内容要求写在注释里。全文中文，面向"懂天文但没看过本项目"
的评委。

```markdown
# 太空目标识别系统技术文档

参赛编号：B02019995　赛道：B02 仿真技术赛道－太空目标识别仿真组

## 1. 观测数据与设备
<!-- 两个数据集的台站坐标、口径推断、像元比例尺、帧数、曝光、指向。
     全部来自 FITS 头，注明关键字名（SITELONG/SITELATI/SITEALTI/DATE-OBS 等）。 -->

## 2. 关键发现：望远镜在跟踪目标
<!-- 这是全系统的立论基础，必须放在方法之前。
     证据链：ΔAZ 恒为 +0.222°/帧（头文件，与像素无关）→ 星场在像面上以
     125.73 px/帧平移并累计旋转 3.325° → 目标在探测器系只漂 95.8 px。
     结论：动目标判据的方向与"运动的是目标、静止的是星"相反。
     顺带说明为什么弃用 legacy 里 speed<0.5→STAR 的旧判据。 -->

## 3. 方法：双坐标系交叉确认
<!-- 三个必要条件，逐条给出为什么单独任一条都不够：
     (1) 探测器系近静止 —— 热像素也满足；
     (2) 天球系快速移动 —— 热像素也满足；
     (3) 非像素锁定 —— 这一条才把热像素排除。
     配上机架分段（为什么只在稳定跟踪段工作）与链式累积配准的误差监控。 -->

## 4. 星点计数的科学依据
<!-- 阈值-复现率双曲线。2σ 检出 2284 个但复现率 <0.5；报数阈值取满足
     复现率 ≥0.85 的最低阈值，得到 10² 量级。
     明确写：我们报的是"可复现星点数"，并给出定义式与匹配半径。
     这一节直接对应 25 分题的科学性，也是"为什么不报几千颗"的答案。 -->

## 5. 动目标识别结果
<!-- 目标轨迹表（帧号、探测器坐标、天球坐标、J2000 赤经赤纬、角速度、星等），
     引用 output/<id>/catalog/target_trajectory.csv 与 .DAT。
     附双坐标系轨迹对照图。 -->

## 6. 真值定量核验
<!-- 三项硬指标：比例尺 6.179±0.013 ″/px（与 .DAT 角步长独立求得的一致性）、
     星等相关 r=0.949、时标偏差 0.0000 s。
     强调真值隔离：.DAT 只进验证与定标，不进探测与配准，并指向
     tests 里的导入图守卫测试作为机制性保证。 -->

## 7. 派生数据集（创新赛项）
<!-- 逐个列出并给出各自的产出文件与图：
     1 目标轨迹与角速度序列
     2 圆轨道高度与周期一阶估计（附与公开 TLE 平均运动反算高度的交叉校验：
       反演值、TLE 值、相对偏差、15% 判定容差，来自 output/<id>/analysis/orbit.json
       的 tle_cross_check 字段；TLE 抄录来源与日期见 docs/reference/tle.txt 文件头）
     3 光变曲线与自旋周期搜索（含 FAP 与基线不足时的诚实结论）
     4 传感器健康报告（热像素簇、噪声、饱和）
     5 观测条件量化（星像宽度、天光面亮度 mag/arcsec²）
     6 传感器缺陷跨历元一致性（两次观测相隔约 4 个月，坏点同位复现）
     7 滑动窗口叠加深场与覆盖度图（含 2.5log10(sqrt(N)) 增益的推导）
     每项写清：输入是什么、方法是什么、结果是什么、可信度边界在哪。 -->

## 8. 复现步骤
<!-- 从 docker build 到 python -m src.cli all 的完整命令序列，含 CPU/GPU 两种镜像、
     退出码含义、产物目录树。评委照抄就能跑。 -->

## 9. 局限与不做的事
<!-- 主动认领边界，抢在质疑之前：
     - 轨道高度是圆轨道 + 单站角速度的一阶估计，不是定轨，不做 SGP4 传播；
       与 TLE 的比对只校验高度量级，容差 15%；
     - 单站单历元无法解算偏心率与倾角；
     - 无 IERS 联网时时标精度降级到何种程度；
     - 数据集 A 无真值，比例尺按 6.179 ″/px 假设处理，相关结论标注为假设；
     - 光变周期在基线不足 2 个周期时只报"不显著"，不给数字。 -->

## 10. 预置质疑与应答
<!-- 见 Step 5，与答辩预案共用同一批条目。 -->
```

- [ ] **Step 5: 在第 10 节写满预置质疑**

每条按"质疑 → 一句话结论 → 证据（文件/命令/图） → 若被追问再展开一层"四段写。
必须覆盖下面全部条目（这些是最可能被问到的原理性问题，每答不出一个扣 1 分）：

1. 为什么只报约 10² 颗星，而不是低阈值下的两千多颗？
2. 你怎么知道运动的是目标而不是星？（方向判断的证据链）
3. 热像素和目标都"在探测器系静止"，你怎么分开的？
4. 配准用的是 4 参数相似变换而不是 6 参数仿射，为什么？会不会漏掉真实形变？
5. 链式累积会不会积累误差？误差多大、怎么监控的？
6. 星等是怎么定标的？用真值定标算不算"用真值算真值"？
7. 比例尺 6.179 ″/px 是怎么独立得到的？和 .DAT 一致是不是循环论证？
8. 叠加深度增益 sqrt(N) 的前提是什么？前提不成立时会怎样？
9. 轨道高度的圆轨道假设错了会怎样？误差量级多大？
10. 光变周期显著性怎么判的？为什么有时不给数字？
11. 两个数据集的参数一样吗？是不是照着某一组数据调的？
12. 时标用 UTC 还是 UT1？无 IERS 数据时精度损失多少？
13. 为什么弃用原有的 `combat_mission.py` 判据？
14. 探测阈值 4σ 搜索 / 5σ 报数为什么分两档？
15. 覆盖度图有什么用？不给覆盖度图会怎样？
16. TLE 是从哪来的？用 TLE 校验算不算拿答案对答案？（答：TLE 只用平均运动反算
    高度做量级校验，不参与反演；反演的唯一输入是像素测得的角速度与头文件仰角）

四列表：`页 | 图（`文件名.png`） | 一句话结论 | 数据来源`。要求：

完整的交付自检，确认"源代码 + 可运行程序 + 技术文档 + 成果图 + 真值核验报告"五项
交付物都真实存在且能跑。

`tests/test_delivery.py`：

```python
from __future__ import annotations

import os
import stat
from pathlib import Path

import pytest

REPO_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
DEFENSE = REPO_ROOT / "docs" / "reports" / "答辩预案.md"
CHECK = REPO_ROOT / "scripts" / "check_delivery.sh"


def test_defense_doc_exists_with_required_parts():
    if not DEFENSE.exists():
        pytest.fail(f"答辩预案缺失：{DEFENSE}")
    text = DEFENSE.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    for section in ["## 主述稿", "## 质疑应答卡", "## 现场演示命令", "## 三分钟时间分配"]:
        assert section in text, f"缺少 {section}"


def test_defense_cards_cover_all_questions():
    """技术文档第 10 节列了 16 条质疑，应答卡一条都不能少。"""
    text = DEFENSE.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    cards = text.split("## 质疑应答卡", 1)[1]
    for i in range(1, 17):
        assert f"Q{i}" in cards, f"缺少应答卡 Q{i}"


def test_demo_commands_are_real_subcommands():
    text = DEFENSE.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    demo = text.split("## 现场演示命令", 1)[1]
    from src.cli import build_parser

    sub = [a for a in build_parser()._actions if a.dest == "command"][0]
    used = {w for w in demo.split() if w in sub.choices}
    assert {"all", "cross"} <= used, "演示命令至少应包含 all 与 cross"


def test_check_delivery_script_is_executable():
    if not CHECK.exists():
        pytest.fail(f"自检脚本缺失：{CHECK}")
    assert os.stat(CHECK).st_mode & stat.S_IXUSR, "scripts/check_delivery.sh 没有可执行位"


def test_readme_points_at_the_new_entrypoint():
    text = (REPO_ROOT / "README.md").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    assert "src.cli" in text
    assert "scripts/dr.sh" in text
```

- [ ] **Step 3: 写 `docs/reports/答辩预案.md`**

四个部分：

**`## 主述稿`** —— 按下面的顺序写成可以照读的连续段落（约 450 字，对应 3 分钟）：
一句话结论（在两组真实观测数据上完成动目标识别并用真值定量核验）→ 关键发现
（望远镜在跟踪目标，判据方向与直觉相反）→ 方法（双坐标系 + 三条必要条件）→
数字（可复现星点数、比例尺 6.179 ″/px、星等相关 0.949、时标 0.0000 s）→
创新（7 项派生数据集，其中跨历元坏点一致性是自证式证据）→ 边界（一阶估计，不是定轨）。

**`## 质疑应答卡`** —— Q1…Q16，与技术文档第 10 节一一对应，每张卡三行：
```
Q7 比例尺 6.179 ″/px 与 .DAT 一致，是不是循环论证？
A  不是。比例尺由星场逐帧平移量（125.73 px/帧，纯像素测量）除以机架角步长
   （0.222°/帧，纯头文件）独立得到，两个输入都不含 .DAT；再与 .DAT 的角步长
   776.9 ″/帧比对，才构成一次真正的独立校验。
证 output/<id>/validation/truth_report.json、tests/validate/test_truth.py、
   tests/test_architecture.py（导入图守卫，禁止 detect/register/target 导入真值模块）
```

**`## 现场演示命令`** —— 三条，每条注明预计耗时与看什么：
```bash
bash scripts/dr.sh python -m src.cli inspect data/images/60385_...   # ~20 s，看机架分段
bash scripts/dr.sh python -m src.cli all data/images/60385_...       # ~数分钟，看全流程
bash scripts/dr.sh python -m src.cli cross data/images/60394_... data/images/60385_...
```
并写明：若现场无 Docker，退回宿主 Python 需要 `pip install -r requirements.txt`；
若时间只够跑一条，跑 `inspect`（最快且直接展示核心发现）。

**`## 三分钟时间分配`** —— 页码 → 秒数 → 要点，总计 180 s，与 PPT 素材清单的页序一致。

- [ ] **Step 4: 写 `scripts/check_delivery.sh`**

```bash
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# 交付自检：五项交付物是否齐备且可跑。全部通过退出 0，缺任何一项退出 1。
set -u

fail=0
ok()   { printf '  [OK]   %s\n' "$1"; }
bad()  { printf '  [MISS] %s\n' "$1"; fail=1; }
have() { [ -e "$1" ] && ok "$2" || bad "$2（缺 $1）"; }

echo "== 1. 源代码 =="
for f in src/cli.py src/compare.py src/config/default.yaml requirements.txt; do
  have "$f" "$f"
done
[ -d tests ] && ok "tests/" || bad "tests/"

echo "== 2. 可运行程序 =="
have scripts/dr.sh "scripts/dr.sh"
have docker/Dockerfile.cpu "docker/Dockerfile.cpu"
if bash scripts/dr.sh python -m src.cli --help >/dev/null 2>&1; then
  ok "python -m src.cli --help 可运行"
else
  bad "python -m src.cli --help 无法运行"
fi

echo "== 3. 中文技术文档 =="
have "docs/reports/技术文档.md" "技术文档"
have "docs/reports/答辩预案.md" "答辩预案"
have "docs/reports/PPT素材清单.md" "PPT 素材清单"
have "docs/reports/measurements.md" "实测量记录"

echo "== 4. 可视化成果图 =="
n=$(ls output/figures/*.png 2>/dev/null | wc -l)
[ "$n" -ge 8 ] && ok "output/figures/ 共 $n 张" || bad "成果图不足（现有 $n 张，需 ≥8）"

echo "== 5. 真值核验报告 =="
if ls output/*/validation/truth_report.json >/dev/null 2>&1; then
  ok "truth_report.json"
else
  bad "output/*/validation/truth_report.json"
fi
have output/cross/cross_dataset.json "跨数据集对比报告"

echo "== 6. 测试 =="
if bash scripts/dr.sh pytest -m "not regression" -q >/dev/null 2>&1; then
  ok "pytest（不含 regression）全绿"
else
  bad "pytest 未全绿"
fi

echo
if [ "$fail" -eq 0 ]; then
  echo "交付自检通过。"
else
  echo "交付自检未通过，请补齐上面标 [MISS] 的项目。"
fi
exit "$fail"
```

然后：

```bash
chmod +x scripts/check_delivery.sh
```
